<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "     1\t\"\"\"P3: real Deep Agents members — delegation, approval interrupt/resume, denial,\n     2\tstep limit and mid-turn injection, all through the production runtime.\"\"\"\n     3\t\n     4\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     5\t\n     6\timport asyncio\n     7\timport json\n     8\t\n     9\timport pytest\n    10\t\n    11\tfrom conftest import Harness, leader, member, msg_channel, spec_of, task_channel\n    12\tfrom scripted_model import ScriptedChatModel, ai_text, ai_tool, find_task_id, last_tool_result\n    13\tfrom teamagents.permissions import ApprovalGate, PermissionPolicy\n    14\tfrom teamagents.models import PermissionMode, TeamAction\n    15\tfrom teamagents.runners import DeepAgentsRunner\n    16\tfrom teamagents.runtime import SessionRuntime\n    17\tfrom teamagents.storage import Store\n    18\tfrom teamagents.models import UserConfig, ModelProfile\n    19\t\n    20\t\n    21\tdef build_runtime(tmp_path, spec, models: dict[str, ScriptedChatModel],\n    22\t                  workdir=None, policy: PermissionPolicy | None = None,\n    23\t                  session_id: str = \"s1\") -&gt; SessionRuntime:\n    24\t    workdir = workdir or (tmp_path / \"work\")\n    25\t    workdir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    26\t    artifacts = tmp_path / \"artifacts\"\n    27\t    artifacts.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n    28\t    store = Store(tmp_path / f\"{session_id}.db\")\n    29\t    if store.get_session(session_id) is None:\n    30\t        store.create_session(session_id, str(workdir), \"approved_scope\")\n    31\t        store.save_team_spec(session_id, spec)\n    32\t        for agent in spec.agents:\n    33\t            store.ensure_agent(session_id, agent.id)\n    34\t    catalog = UserConfig(models={\"test\": ModelProfile(provider=\"openai\", model=\"test\")})\n    35\t    approvals = ApprovalGate(store, session_id, policy or PermissionPolicy())\n    36\t    from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import InMemorySaver\n    37\t    runners = {agent.id: DeepAgentsRunner(\n    38\t        agent=agent, catalog=catalog, session_id=session_id, workdir=workdir,\n    39\t        artifacts_dir=artifacts, checkpointer=InMemorySaver(), approvals=approvals,\n    40\t        model_override=models[agent.id]) for agent in spec.agents}\n    41\t    return SessionRuntime(store, session_id, catalog, runners=runners,\n    42\t                          approvals=approvals)\n    43\t\n    44\t\n    45\tasync def test_deepagents_delegation_end_to_end(tmp_path):\n    46\t    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"),\n    47\t                   channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"]), msg_channel(\"b\", [\"leader\"])])\n    48\t\n    49\t    def leader_finish(messages):\n    50\t        task_id = last_tool_result(messages).get(\"result\", {}).get(\"task_id\")\n    51\t        return ai_tool(\"wait_for_tasks\", {\"task_ids\": [task_id]})\n    52\t\n    53\t    leader_model = ScriptedChatModel(script=[\n    54\t        ai_tool(\"assign_task\", {\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"write report.md\",\n    55\t                                \"acceptance\": \"file exists\"}),\n    56\t        leader_finish,\n    57\t        ai_tool(\"signal_done\", {\"summary\": \"report delivered\"}),\n    58\t        ai_text(\"all done\"),\n    59\t    ])\n    60\t\n    61\t    def b_complete(messages):\n    62\t        text = json.dumps([m.content for m in messages if hasattr(m, \"content\")])\n    63\t        return ai_tool(\"complete_task\", {\"task_id\": find_task_id(text),\n    64\t                                         \"summary\": \"wrote it\",\n    65\t                                         \"result_refs\": [\"report.md\"]})\n    66\t\n    67\t    b_model = ScriptedChatModel(script=[b_complete, ai_text(\"done\")])\n    68\t    rt = build_runtime(tmp_path, spec, {\"leader\": leader_model, \"b\": b_model})\n    69\t    h = Harness(rt, {})\n    70\t    await h.start()\n    71\t    try:\n    72\t        receipt = rt.user_message(\"please produce the report\")\n    73\t        assert receipt.ok\n    74\t        assert await rt.settle(15), \"session did not settle\"\n    75\t        tasks = rt.store.tasks_for_session(\"s1\")\n    76\t        assert len(tasks) == 1 and tasks[0].status == \"SUCCEEDED\"\n    77\t        assert tasks[0].result_refs == [\"report.md\"]\n    78\t        assert rt.store.get_session(\"s1\")[\"goal_state\"] == \"done\"\n    79\t        # both members used the real graph: the leader's tools were bound\n    80\t        assert \"assign_task\" in leader_model.tool_names\n    81\t        assert \"signal_done\" in leader_model.tool_names\n    82\t        assert \"shell\" in b_model.tool_names\n    83\t    finally:\n    84\t        await rt.close()\n    85\t        rt.store.close()\n    86\t\n    87\t\n    88\tasync def test_deepagents_shell_runs_isolated_inside_workdir(tmp_path):\n    89\t    spec = spec_of(leader(), channels=[])\n    90\t    model = ScriptedChatModel(script=[\n    91\t        ai_tool(\"shell\", {\"command\": \"echo hi &gt; made.txt &amp;&amp; cat made.txt\"}),\n    92\t        ai_tool(\"shell\", {\"command\": \"ls /home/rimuru 2&gt;&amp;1 | head -1\"}),\n    93\t rt.store.close()\n   111\t\n   112\t\n   113\tasync def test_deepagents_approval_interrupt_and_resume(tmp_path):\n   114\t    spec = spec_of(leader(), channels=[])\n   115\t    model = int:\n   121\t    \"\"\"Add two integers.\"\"\"\n   122\t    return a + b\n   123\t\n   124\t\n   125\t@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"profile\", PROVIDERS)\n   126\tasync def test_provider_contract_tool_call_and_continuation(profile):\n   127\t    model = build_chat_model(profile).bind_tools([add])\n   128\t    prompt = (\"You must call the add tool with a=17 and b=25. first.tool_calls:\n   132\t        # model judgement varies between runs; one retry, then fail honestly\n   133\t        first = await model.ainvoke([HumanMessage(\n   134\t            content=prompt + \" Call the add tool now; answering directly is wrong.\")])\n   135\t    assert first.tool_calls, f\"{profile.model}: no tool call produced\"\n   136\t    call = first.tool_calls[0]\n   137\t    assert call[\"name\"] == \"add\"\n   138\t    assert call[\"args\"].get(\"a\") == 17 and call[\"args\"].get(\"b\") == 25, \\\n   139\t        \"tool arguments must be assembled completely\"\n   140\t    second = await model.ainvoke([\n   141\t        HumanMessage(content=prompt), first,\n   142\t        ToolMessage(content=\"42\", tool_call_id=call[\"id\"])])\n   143\t    assert \"42\" in test_provider_contract_error_propagation(profile):\n   162\t    bad = profile.model_copy(update={\"model\": \"definitely-not-a-model-xyz\"})\n   163\t    model = build_chat_model(bad)\n   164\t    try:\n   165\t        response = await model.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content=\"hi\")])\n   166\t    except Exception as err:  # the expected path on a first-party endpoint\n   167\t        message = str(err).lower()\n   168\t        assert (\"model\" in message or \"not found\" in message or \"invalid\" in message\n   169\t                or \"400\" in message or \"404\" in message), \\\n   170\t            f\"provider error must surface: {err}\"\n   171\t        return\n   172\t    if profile.base_url:\n   173\t        # a relay may pin its own model and ignore the requested name: the\n   174\t        # response is then a normal completion, which is not an error at all\n   175\t        assert str(response.content).strip() or response.tool_calls\n   176\t        return\n   177\t    pytest.fail(f\"unknown model {bad.model!r} did not raise a provider error\")\n   178\t\n   179\t\n   180\t_ = (asyncio, json, Path, UserConfig)\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "     1\t\"\"\"P6/T20: the TUI stays usable while members work — input, navigation,\n     2\tapprovals, streaming refresh, narrow terminals and multi-line Chinese input.\"\"\"\n     3\t\n     4\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n     5\t\n     6\timport asyncio\n     7\timport json\n     8\timport time\n     9\t\n    10\timport pytest\n 18\t\n    19\tasync def _session(harness_factory, *, busy: bool = False):\n    20\t    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"),\n    21\t                   channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"]), msg_channel(\"b\", [\"leader\"])])\n    22\t    if busy:\n    23\t        members = scripts(\n    24\t            leader=[(\"call\", \"assign_task\", {\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"slow\"}),\n    25\t                    (\"call\", \"wait_for_tasks\", {\"task_ids\": [\"$r0.result.task_id\"]}),\n    26\t                    (\"wait\",), (\"call\", \"signal_done\", {}), (\"end\",)],\n    27\t            b=[(\"sleep\", 1.2),\n    28\t               (\"call\", \"complete_task\", {\"task_id\": \"$inbox0.payload.task_id\"}),\n    29\t               (\"end\",)],\n    30\t        )\n    31\t    else:\n    32\t        members = scripts(leader=[(\"call\", \"signal_done\", {\"summary\": \"hi\"}),\n    33\t                                  (\"end\",)],\n    34\t                          b=[(\"end\",)])\n    35\t    h = await harness_factory(spec, members)\n    36\t    return h\n    37\t\n    38\t\n    39\tasync def test_tui_accepts_multiline_chinese_input_and_shows_reply(harness_factory):\n    40\t    h = await _session(harness_factory)\n    41\t    app = TeamAgentsApp(runtime=h.rt)\n    42\t    async with app.run_test(size=(120, 40)) as pilot:\n    43\t        await pilot.pause()\n    44\t        prompt = app.query_one(\"#prompt\", PromptInput)\n    45\t        prompt.text = \"第一行：请总结\\n第二行：包含中文\"\n    46\t        await pilot.press(\"enter\")\n    47\t        await pilot.pause(0.3)\n    48\t        await h.settle(10)\n    49\t        await pilot.pause(0.4)\n    50\t\n    51\t        messages = [e for e in h.rt.store.events(\"s1\")\n    52\t                    if e[\"kind\"] == \"user_message\"]\n    53\t        assert messages, \"UI 输入必须进入团队事务\"\n    54\t        payload = <message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"Team control flow: ingest -&gt; validate -&gt; reduce -&gt; schedule -&gt; persist.\n\nEvery team action enters here exactly once; the business transaction (receipt,\nevents, task/shared-space changes, queued execution intents) commits together.\nAction ids are stable (`run_id:tool_call_id`), repeats return the original receipt.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport json\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom . import views\nfrom .models import (\n    ActionKind,\n    AgentSpec,\n    AgentStatus,\n    ApprovalStatus,\n    ChannelSpec,\n    EventKind,\n    Limits,\n    PatchStatus,\n    Receipt,\n    SessionStatus,\n    SharedEntry,\n    Task,\n    TaskStatus,\n    TeamAction,\n    TeamEvent,\n    TeamSpec,\n    TopologyPatch,\n    TurnRun,\n    TurnStatus,\n    TURN_TERMINAL_STATUSES,\n    UserConfig,\n    new_id,\n    now,\n)\nfrom .storage import Store\n\n#: capabilities the runtime provides natively; they need no user-config entry\nBUILTIN_TOOL_BINDINGS = {\"files\", \"shell\", \"web\"}\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass EventDraft:\n    kind: EventKind\n    payload: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    task_id: str | None = None\n    targets: list[str] | None = None\n    actor_id: str | None = None\n    audience: list[str] | None = None\n    push: list[str] | None = None\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass Reduction:\n    events: list[EventDraft]\n    receipt: Receipt\n\n\nclass Control:\n    \"\"\"The single serialized entry point for team transactions per session.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, store: Store, session_id: str, catalog: UserConfig | None = None):\n        self.store = store\n        self.session_id = session_id\n        self.catalog = catalog or UserConfig()\n        self.last_schedule_error: str | None = None\n        #: (run_id, inbox items) recorded when a running member receives mid-turn input\n        self.mid_turn_pushes: list[tuple[str, list[dict]]] = []\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ submit\n\n    def submit(self, action: TeamAction) -&gt; Receipt:\n        with self.store.tx():\n            prior = self.store.get_action_receipt(action.action_id)\n            if prior is not None:\n                return prior\n            spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n            try:\n                error = self._validate(action, spec)\n                if error is not None:\n                    receipt = Receipt.failure(action, error)\n                    self.store.record_action(\n                        action.action_id, self.session_id, action.actor_id, action.run_id,\n                        action.kind, self._payload_hash(action), receipt,\n                    )\n                    return receipt\n                reduction = self._reduce(action, spec)\n            except Exception as e:\n                # malformed model input is a readable refusal, never a crash:\n                # the transaction rolls back and nothing half-applies\n                receipt = Receipt.failure(action, f\"{type(e).__name__}: {e}\")\n                self.store.record_action(\n                    action.action_id, self.session_id, action.actor_id, action.run_id,\n                    action.kind, self._payload_hash(action), receipt,\n                )\n                return receipt\n            self._persist_events(action, spec, reduction.events)\n            self._schedule(spec)\n            self.store.record_action(\n                action.action_id, self.session_id, action.actor_id, action.run_id,\n                action.kind, self._payload_hash(action), reduction.receipt,\n            )\n            return reduction.receipt\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _derived_task_id(action: TeamAction) -&gt; str:\n        \"\"\"Deterministic per action id, unique even for several tasks in one run.\"\"\"\n        if explicit := action.payload.get(\"task_id\"):\n            return str(explicit)\n        digest = hashlib.sha256(action.action_id.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]\n        return f\"task_{digest}\"\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _payload_hash(action: TeamAction) -&gt; str:\n        blob = json.dumps(action.payload, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)\n        return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode()).hexdigest()[:32]\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------ public API\n\n    def schedule(self) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Re-run scheduling against the committed state (called after turn ends).\"\"\"\n        with self.store.tx():\n            self._schedule(self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id))\n\n    def emit(self, drafts: list[EventDraft], actor_id: str = \"system\") -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Persist runtime-originated events (run/task lifecycle) and schedule.\"\"\"\n        with self.store.tx():\n            spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n            action = TeamAction(action_id=new_id(\"sys\"), session_id=self.session_id,\n                                actor_id=actor_id, kind=ActionKind.SEND_MESSAGE, payload={})\n            self._persist_events(action, spec, drafts)\n            self._schedule(spec)\n\n    # -------------------------------------------------------------- validation\n\n    def _validate(self, action: TeamAction, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; str | None:\n        kind = action.kind\n        member_ids = {a.id for a in spec.agents}\n        actor = action.actor_id\n\n        if kind is ActionKind.USER_MESSAGE or kind is ActionKind.USER_SUPPLEMENT:\n            if actor != \"user\":\n                return \"only the local user can submit user input\"\n            if not str(action.payload.get(\"text\", \"\")).strip():\n                return \"user input text must not be empty\"\n            return None\n\n        # actor identity is runtime-injected; the user actor may only drive\n        # control-plane actions, never member actions\n        user_kinds = {ActionKind.CANCEL_TASK, ActionKind.CANCEL_RUN,\n                      ActionKind.APPROVAL_DECISION, ActionKind.SET_PERMISSION_MODE,\n                      ActionKind.PAUSE_SESSION}\n        if actor == \"user\":\n            if kind not in user_kinds:\n                return f\"the local user cannot submit {kind}\"\n        elif actor not in member_ids:\n            return f\"actor {actor!r} is not a team member\"\n        if actor in member_ids and action.run_id is not None:\n            run = self.store.get_run(action.run_id)\n            if run is None:\n                return f\"unknown run {action.run_id!r}\"\n            if run.agent_id != actor:\n                return \"run does not belong to the acting member\"\n\n        match kind:\n            case ActionKind.SEND_MESSAGE:\n                target = action.payload.get(\"target\")\n                if target == \"*\":\n                    if not any(ch.source == actor and ch.mode.value == \"broadcast\"\n                               for ch in spec.channels):\n                        return f\"{actor!r} has no broadcast channel\"\n                    return None\n                if target not in member_ids:\n                    return f\"unknown message target {target!r}\"\n                if not spec.can_send(actor, target):\n                    return f\"{actor!r} is not allowed to message {target!r}\"\n            case ActionKind.ASSIGN_TASK:\n                assignee = action.payload.get(\"assignee\")\n                if assignee not in member_ids:\n                    return f\"unknown assignee {assignee!r}\"\n                if not spec.can_delegate(actor, assignee):\n                    return f\"{actor!r} is not allowed to assign tasks to {assignee!r}\"\n                if (spec.agent(assignee).runtime_kind.value == \"codex\"\n                        and actor != spec.leader_id):\n                    return \"codex members can only be delegated to by the Leader\"\n                if not str(action.payload.get(\"description\", \"\")).strip():\n                    return \"task description must not be empty\"\n                deps = action.payload.get(\"dependencies\") or []\n                for dep in deps:\n                    dep_task = self.store.get_task(dep)\n                    if dep_task is None:\n                        return f\"unknown dependency task {dep!r}\"\n                new_task_id = self._derived_task_id(action)\n                if self._dependency_cycle(new_task_id, deps):\n                    return \"task dependencies would form a cycle\"\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

task.assignee != actor:\n                    return \"only the current assignee can complete a task\"\n                if task.status not in (TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING):\n active run\"\n                for tid in action.payload.get(\"task_ids\") or []:\n                    if self.store.get_task(tid) is None:\n                        return f\"unknown task {tid!r}\"\n            case ActionKind.PUBLISH_SHARED:\n                space_id = action.payload.get(\"space_id\")\n                try:\n                    space = spec.space(space_id)\n                except         if not (action.payload.get(\"content\") or action.payload.get(\"ref\")):\n                    return \"shared entry needs content or a ref\"\n            case ActionKind.READ_SHARED |             return f\"{actor!r} has no read access to shared space {space_id!r}\"\n            case ActionKind.REQUEST_HELP:\n                if not str(action.payload.get(\"message\",         if not isinstance(ops, list) or not ops:\n                    return \"proposal needs a non-empty operations list\"\n            case ActionKind.APPLY_TOPOLOGY_PATCH:\n                if actor      patch = self.store.get_patch(patch_id)\n                    if patch is None:\n                        return f\"unknown patch {patch_id!r}\"\n                    if patch.status not in (PatchStatus.PROPOSED, PatchStatus.ACCEPTED):\n                        return f\"patch is {patch.status}, cannot decide\"\n                    return None\n                ops = action.payload.get(\"operations\")\n                if not isinstance(ops, list) or not ops:\n                    return \"patch needs a non-empty operations list\"\n                base = action.payload.get(\"base_revision\")\n                if base != self.store.current_revision(self.session_id):\n                    return (f\"patch base_revision {base} is stale; \"\n                            f\"current is {self.store.current_revision(self.session_id)}\")\n            case ActionKind.SIGNAL_DONE:\n                if actor != spec.leader_id:\n                    return \"only         if actor not in (spec.leader_id, \"user\"):\n                    return \"only the Leader or the user can cancel tasks\"\n                if self.store.get_task(action.payload.get(\"task_id\", \"\")) is None:\n                    return f\"unknown task {action.payload.get('task_id')!r}\"\n            case ActionKind.CANCEL_RUN:\n                if actor not in (spec.leader_id, \"user\"):\n                    return \"only the Leader or the user can cancel runs\"\n                if self.store.get_run(action.payload.get(\"run_id\", \"\")) is None:\n                    return f\"unknown run {action.payload.get('run_id')!r}\"\n            case ActionKind.APPROVAL_DECISION:\n                if actor != \"user\":\n                    return \"only the local user can decide approvals\"\n                req = self.store.get_approval(action.payload.get(\"approval_id\", \"\"))\n                if req is None:\n                    return f\"unknown approval {action.payload.get('approval_id')!r}\"\n                if req.status is not ApprovalStatus.PENDING:\n                    return f\"approval is already {req.status}\"\n                if action.payload.get(\"decision\") not in (\"once\", \"session\", \"deny\"):\n                    return \"decision must be one of: once, session, deny\"\n            case ActionKind.SET_PERMISSION_MODE:\n                if actor != \"user\":\n                    return \"only the local user can change the permission mode\"\n                if action.payload.get(\"mode\") not in (\"approved_scope\", \"full_auto\"):\n                    return \"mode must be approved_scope or full_auto\"\n            case ActionKind.PAUSE_SESSION:\n                if actor != \"user\":\n                    return \"only the local user can pause the session\"\n            case _:\n                return f\"unsupported action kind {kind}\"\n        return None\n\n    def _dependency_cycle(self, new_task_id: str, deps: list[str]) -&gt; bool:\n        \"\"\"Adding new_task_id -&gt; deps edges must not create a cycle.\"\"\"\n        seen: set[str] = set()\n        stack = list(deps)\n        while stack:\n            tid = stack.pop()\n            if tid == new_task_id:\n                return True\n            if tid in seen:\n                continue\n            seen.add(tid)\n            t = self.store.get_task(tid)\n            if t is not None:\n                stack.extend(t.dependencies)\n        return False\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ reduce\n\n    def _reduce(self, action: TeamAction, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; Reduction:\n        kind = action.kind\n        actor = action.actor_id\n        p = action.payload\n\n        match kind:\n            case ActionKind.USER_MESSAGE | ActionKind.USER_SUPPLEMENT:\n                # user input resumes a paused session (plan section 9.3)\n                session = self.store.get_session(self.session_id)\n                if session is not None and SessionStatus(session[\"status\"]) is SessionStatus.PAUSED:\n                    self.store.set_session_status(self.session_id, SessionStatus.ACTIVE)\n                goal_id = self._ensure_goal(spec)\n                text = str(p[\"text\"])\n                return Reduction(\n                    events=[EventDraft(\n                        kind=EventKind.USER_MESSAGE,\n                        payload={\"text\": text, \"goal_id\": goal_id,\n                                 \"supplement\": kind is ActionKind.USER_SUPPLEMENT,\n                                 \"to\": spec.leader_id},\n                        actor_id=\"user\",\n                    )],\n                    receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,\n                                    result={\"received\": True, \"goal_id\": goal_id}),\n                )\n\n            case ActionKind.SEND_MESSAGE:\n                target = p.get(\"target\")\n                targets = ([a.id for a in spec.agents if a.id != actor] if target == \"*\"\n                           else [target])\n                targets = [t for t in targets if t != actor and spec.can_send(actor, t)]\n                text = str(p.get(\"text\", \"\"))\n                return Reduction(\n                    events=[EventDraft(kind=EventKind.MESSAGE,\n                                       payload={\"text\": text, \"from\": actor, \"target\": t},\n                                       targets=[t]) for t in targets],\n                    receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,\n                                    result={\"delivered_to\": targets}),\n                )\n\n            case ActionKind.ASSIGN_TASK:\n                deps = list(p.get(\"dependencies\") or [])\n                task = Task(\n                    task_id=self._derived_task_id(action),\n                    parent_task_id=p.get(\"parent_task_id\"),\n                    goal_id=self._current_goal_id(),\n                    requester=actor,\n                    assignee=p[\"assignee\"],\n                    description=str(p[\"description\"]),\n                    acceptance=str(p.get(\"acceptance\", \"\")),\n                    dependencies=deps,\n                )\n                self.store.insert_task(self.session_id, task)\n                return Reduction(\n                    events=[EventDraft(kind=EventKind.TASK_CREATED,\n                                       payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                                                \"requester\": task.requester,\n                                                \"description\": task.description,\n                                                \"acceptance\": task.acceptance,\n                                                \"dependencies\": deps,\n                                                \"status\": \"PENDING\"},\n                                       task_id=task.task_id)],\n                    receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,\n                                    result={\"task_id\": task.task_id}),\n                )\n\n            case ActionKind.COMPLETE_TASK:\n                task_id = p[\"task_id\"]\n                result_refs = list(p.get(\"result_refs\") or [])\n                summary = str(p.get(\"summary\", \"\"))\n                self.store.record_completion_request(\n                    action.run_id or \"\", task_id, result_refs, summary)\n                return Reduction(\n                    events=[],\n                    receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,\n                                    result={\"recorded\": True, \"task_id\": task_id,\n                                            \"applies_at\": \"turn_end\"}),\n                )\n\n            case ActionKind.WAIT_FOR_TASKS:\n                task_ids = list(p.get(\"task_ids\") or [])\n                pending = [t for t in task_ids\n                           if (task := self.store.get_task(t)) is not None\n                           and task.status not in (TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED, TaskStatus.FAILED,\n                                                   TaskStatus.CANCELLED)]\n                if pending:\n                    run = self.store.get_run(action.run_id or \"\")\n                    if run is not None:\n                        self.store.update_run_status_where(\n                            run.run_id, TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK)\n                        self.store.deliver_wait_registration(run.run_id, pending)\n                    return Reduction(\n                        events=[EventDraft(kind=EventKind.RUN_WAITING,\n                                           payload={\"run_id\": action.run_id, \"agent_id\": actor,\n                                                    \"waiting_on\": pending})],\n                        receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,\n                                        result={\"waiting\": True, \"task_ids\": pending}),\n                    )\n                results = {tid: self._task_result(tid) for tid in task_ids}\n                return Reduction(\n                    events=[],\n                    receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,\n                                    result={\"waiting\": False, \"results\": results}),\n                )\n\n            case ActionKind.PUBLISH_SHARED:\n                entry = SharedEntry(\n                    entry_id=new_id(\"share\"),\n                    space_id=p[\"space_id\"],\n                    author=actor,\n                    kind=str(p.get(\"kind\", \"note\")),\n                    content=str(p.get(\"content\", \"\")),\n                    ref=p.get(\"ref\"),\n                    supersedes=p.get(\"supersedes\"),\n                )\n                seq = self.store.add_shared_entry(entry, self.session_id)\n                return Reduction(\n                    events=[EventDraft(kind=EventKind.SHARED_PUBLISHED,\n                                       payload={\"entry_id\": entry.entry_id,\n                                                \"space_id\": entry.space_id, \"author\": actor,\n                                                \"kind\": entry.kind,\n                                                \"summary\": entry.content[:200] or entry.ref,\n                                                \"sequence\": seq})],\n                    receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,\n                                    result={\"entry_id\": entry.entry_id, \"sequence\": seq}),\n                )\n\n            case ActionKind.READ_SHARED:\n                return self._read_shared(action, spec, advance=True)\n            case ActionKind.LIST_SHARED:\n                spaces = [s for s in spec.shared_spaces\n                          if actor in s.readers or actor in s.writers]\n                infos = []\n                for s in spaces:\n                    last = self.store.shared_entries(self.session_id, [s.id], limit=1)\n                    entries = self.store.shared_entries(self.session_id, [s.id], limit=1000)\n                    infos.append({\"space_id\": s.id, \"entries\": len(entries),\n                                  \"last_sequence\": entries[-1].sequence if entries else 0,\n                                  \"readable\": True, \"writable\": actor in s.writers})\n                return Reduction(\n                    events=[],\n                    receipt=Receipt(action_id=action.action_id, ok=True, kind=kind,\n                                    result={\"spaces\": infos}),\n                )\n\n            case ActionKind.REQUEST_HELP:\n                task_id = p.get(\"task_id\")\n                return Reduction(\n                    events=[EventDraft(kind=EventKind.MESSAGE,\n any(a[\"id\"] == agent.get(\"id\") for a in data[\"agents\"]):\n                        return None, f\"member {agent.get('id')!r} already exists\"\n                    if agent.get(\"model_profile\") tool binding {tb!r}\"\n                    data[\"agents\"].append(agent)\n                    for ch in op.get(\"channels\") or []:\n                        data[\"channels\"].append(ch)\n []})\n                case \"remove_agent\":\n                    agent_id = op.get(\"agent_id\")\n                    if agent_id == spec.leader_id:\n                        return None, \"the Leader cannot be removed\"\n                    if not any(a[\"id\"] == agent_id for a in data[\"agents\"]):\n                        return None, f\"unknown member {agent_id!r}\"\n                    data[\"agents\"] = [a for a in data[\"agents\"] if a[\"id\"] != agent_id]\n                    data[\"channels\"] = [c for c in data[\"channels\"]\n                                        if c[\"source\"] != agent_id\n                                        and agent_id not in c[\"targets\"]]\n                    data[\"observers\"] = [o for o in data[\"observers\"]\n = next((a for a in data[\"agents\"] if a[\"id\"] == agent_id), None)\n                    if target is None:\n                        return None, f\"unknown member {agent_id!r}\"\n                    changes = op.get(\"changes\") or {}\n                    if \"model_profile\" in changes and changes[\"model_profile\"] not in cfg_models:\n                        return None, f\"unknown model profile {changes['model_profile']!r}\"\n                    for tb in changes.get(\"tool_bindings\") or []:\n                        if tb not in cfg_tools and tb not in BUILTIN_TOOL_BINDINGS:\n                            return None, f\"unknown tool binding {tb!r}\"\n                    target.update(changes)\n                case \"add_channel\":\n                    data[\"channels\"].append(op.get(\"channel\") or {})\n                case \"remove_channel\":\n                    src, tgts = op.get(\"source\"), op.get(\"targets\") or []\n                    for t in tgts:\n                        for c in data[\"channels\"]:\n                            if c[\"source\"] == src and t in c[\"targets\"]:\n                                c[\"targets\"].remove(t)\n                        data[\"channels\"] = [c for c in data[\"channels\"]\n                                            if not (c[\"source\"] == src and not c[\"targets\"])]\n                case \"set_observer\":\n                    ob = op.get(\"observer\") or {}\n                    data[\"observers\"] = [o for o in data[\"observers\"]\n                                         if o[\"agent_id\"] != ob.get(\"agent_id\")]\n                    if not op.get(\"remove\"):\n                        data[\"observers\"].append(ob)\n                case \"set_space_acl\":\n                    sid = op.get(\"space_id\")\n                    target = next((s for s in data[\"shared_spaces\"] if s[\"id\"] == sid), None)\n                    if target is None:\n                        return None, f\"unknown shared space {sid!r}\"\n                    for key in (\"readers\", \"writers\"):\n                        if key in op:\n                            target[key] = op[key]\n @staticmethod\n    def _permissions_changed(old: TeamSpec, new: TeamSpec, agent_id: str) -&gt; bool:\n        def perms(spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; tuple:\n            sends = sorted(a.id for a in spec.agents if spec.can_send(agent_id, a.id))\n            delegates = sorted(a.id for a in spec.agents if spec.can_delegate(agent_id, a.id))\n            spaces = sorted(\n                (s.id, agent_id in s.readers, agent_id in s.writers)\n                for s in spec.shared_spaces\n            )\n            observers = sorted(\n                (o.agent_id, tuple(o.subjects), tuple(sorted(o.event_types)))\n                for o in spec.observers\n            )\n            return sends, delegates, spaces, observers\n\n        return perms(old) != perms(new)\n\n    def _sync_members(self, new: TeamSpec, old: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Ensure runtime rows exist for new members; bump config revisions on change.\"\"\"\n        for a in new.agents:\n            self.store.ensure_agent(self.session_id, a.id)\n        for a in new.agents:\n            old_a = next((x for x in old.agents if x.id == a.id), None)\n            if old_a is None or old_a != a:\n                revision = self.store.bump_config_revision(self.session_id, a.id)\n                if self.store.agent_status(self.session_id, a.id) is AgentStatus.DRAINING:\n                    self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, a.id, AgentStatus.IDLE)\n                _ = revision\n        for a in old.agents:\n            if not any(x.id == a.id for x in new.agents):\n                self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, a.id, AgentStatus.REMOVED)\n                self._hand_over_removed_member(a.id, new)\n\n    def _hand_over_removed_member(self, agent_id: str, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"A removed member's unfinished work goes back to the Leader for a new\n        decision; results and audit records are kept (plan section 8).\"\"\"\n        handed = self.store.reassign_tasks(\n            agent_id, spec.leader_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.BLOCKED])\n        for task_id in handed:\n            # a handed-over task must be announced to its before delivery\")\n        action = TeamAction(action_id=f\"member-removed:{agent_id}\",\n                            session_id=self.session_id, actor_id=\"system\",\n                            kind=ActionKind.APPLY_TOPOLOGY_PATCH, payload={})\n        self._persist_events(action, spec, [EventDraft(\n            kind=EventKind.MEMBER_REMOVED,\n            payload={\"agent_id\": agent_id, and (active.task_id == task.task_id or active.task_id is None):\n            self.store.set_run_cancel_requested(active.run_id)\n            self.store.set_task_cancel_requested(task.task_id)\n            status = \"CANCEL_REQUESTED\"\n        else:\n            self.store.compare_and_set_task(task.task_id, task.status, TaskStatus.CANCELLED)\n            status = \"CANCELLED\"\n        return \"status\": status}))\n\n    def _completion_blockers(self, spec: TeamSpec, current_run: str | None = None) -&gt; list[str]:\n        blockers: list[str] = []\n        live_runs = self.store.runs_for_session(\n            self.session_id,\n            [TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK,\n             TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL])\n        live_runs = [r for r in live_runs if r.run_id != current_run]\n        if live_runs:\n            blockers.append(\"active turns: \" + \", \".join(\n                f\"{r.agent_id}:{r.status}\" for r in live_runs))\n        unknown = self.store.runs_for_session(self.session_id, [TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN])\n        if unknown:\n            blockers.append(\"outcome-unknown operations: \" + \", \".join(\n                r.run_id for r in unknown))\n        un = self.store.tasks_for_session(\n            self.session_id,\n            [TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING, self.store.pending_approvals(self.session_id)\n        pending_approvals = [a for a in pend if a is not None]\n        if pending_approvals:\n            blockers.append(\"pending approvals: \" + \", _persist_events(self, action: TeamAction, spec: TeamSpec,\n                        drafts: list[EventDraft]) -&gt; None:\n        batch_by_agent: dict[str, int] = {}\n        for draft in drafts:\n        )\n            self.store.append_event(event)\n            for recipient in push:\n                batch = batch_by_agent.get(recipient)\n                if batch is None:\n self.store.create_delivery(self.session_id, recipient, event.event_id, batch,\n                                           payload_override=override)\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- schedule\n\n    def _schedule(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Create/wake runs for pending deliveries and ready tasks.\"\"\"\n        session = self.session_id\n        self._apply_boundary_patches(spec)\n        self._block_unrunnable_tasks(spec)\n        self._announce_ready_tasks(spec)\n        for agent in ids = [d[\"delivery_id\"] for d in fresh]\n                if not ids:\n                    continue\n                self.store.append_run_inputs(run.run_id, ids)\n                if run.status is TurnStatus.RUNNING:\n                    self.mid_turn_pushes.append((run.run_id, [\n                        {\"kind\": d[\"event_kind\"], \"from\": d[\"event_actor\"],\n                         \"task_id\": d[\"event_task_id\"],\n                         \"payload\": json.loads(d[\"payload_json\"])}\n                        for d in fresh\n                    ]))\n                continue\n            if waiting is not None:\n                if waiting.status is TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL:\n                    continue\n                # ponytail: a waiting turn resumes when ALL waited tasks are\n                # terminal, or on user input / cancel. Partial results stay\n                # queued; split waits into separate waits if partial wake needed.\n                pending_wait = [t for t in waiting.waiting_on\n                                if (task := self.store.get_task(t)) is not None\n                                and task.status not in (TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED, TaskStatus.FAILED,\n                                                        TaskStatus.CANCELLED)]\n                user_input = any(d[\"event_kind\"] == EventKind.USER_MESSAGE for d in pending)\n                if pending_wait and not user_input and not waiting.cancel_requested:\n                    continue\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "                    continue\n                if self.store.update_run_status_where(\n                        waiting.run_id, TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK, TurnStatus.RUNNING):\n                    self.store.append_run_inputs(\n                        waiting.run_id, [d[\"delivery_id\"] for d in pending])\n                continue\n            if not self._turn_budget_ok():\n                self._emit_limit_reached()\n                continue\n            task = self._next_ready_task(agent.id)\n            run = TurnRun(\n                run_id=new_id(\"run\"),\n                session_id=session,\n                task_id=task.task_id if task else None,\n                goal_id=self._current_goal_id(),\n                agent_id=agent.id,\n                config_revision=self.store.agent_config_revision(session, agent.id),\n                topology_revision=self.store.current_revision(session),\n                status=TurnStatus.QUEUED,\n                input_delivery_ids=[d[\"delivery_id\"] for d in pending],\n                context_ref=self._context_ref(agent.id),\n            )\n            self.store.insert_run(run)\n\n    def _block_unrunnable_tasks(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Dependencies that failed/cancelled make dependents BLOCKED, never silently stuck.\"\"\"\n        for task in self.store.tasks_for_session(self.session_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING]):\n            deps = [self.store.get_task(d) for d in task.dependencies]\n            broken = [d for d in deps\n                      if d is not None and d.status in (TaskStatus.FAILED, TaskStatus.CANCELLED)]\n            if not broken:\n                continue\n            if self.store.compare_and_set_task(task.task_id, TaskStatus.PENDING,\n                                               TaskStatus.BLOCKED):\n                draft = EventDraft(\n                    kind=EventKind.TASK_BLOCKED,\n                    payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                             \"requester\": task.requester,\n                             \"reason\": \"dependency \" + \", \".join(\n                                 f\"{d.task_id}:{d.status}\" for d in broken)},\n                    task_id=task.task_id,\n                )\n                action = TeamAction(action_id=f\"blocked:{task.task_id}\",\n                                    session_id=self.session_id, actor_id=\"system\",\n                                    kind=ActionKind.CANCEL_TASK, payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id})\n                self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])\n\n    def _announce_ready_tasks(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"One TASK_READY ping per task, once its dependencies are satisfied.\"\"\"\n        for task in sorted(self.store.tasks_for_session(self.session_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING]),\n                           key=lambda t: t.created_at):\n            deps = [self.store.get_task(d) for d in task.dependencies]\n            if not all(d is not None and d.status is TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED for d in deps):\n                continue\n            if self.store.get_meta(f\"task_ready_announced:{task.task_id}\"):\n                continue\n            draft = EventDraft(\n                kind=EventKind.TASK_READY,\n                payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                         \"requester\": task.requester,\n                         \"description\": task.description, \"acceptance\": task.acceptance},\n                task_id=task.task_id,\n            )\n            action = TeamAction(action_id=f\"ready:{task.task_id}\", session_id=self.session_id,\n                                actor_id=\"system\", kind=ActionKind.CANCEL_TASK, payload={})\n            self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])\n            self.store.set_meta(f\"task_ready_announced:{task.task_id}\", \"1\")\n\n    def _apply_boundary_patches(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:\n        for patch in self.store.patches_in_status(self.session_id, PatchStatus.WAITING_BOUNDARY):\n            if any(self._agent_has_live_run(a) for a in patch.affected_agents):\n                continue\n            current = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n            new_spec, error = self._apply_operations(current, patch.operations)\n            if error:\n                self.store.set_patch_status(patch.patch_id, PatchStatus.FAILED)\n                continue\n            revision = self.store.save_team_spec(self.session_id, new_spec)\n            self.store.set_patch_status(patch.patch_id, PatchStatus.APPLIED)\n            self._sync_members(new_spec, current)\n            self._persist_patch_event(patch, revision)\n\n    def _persist_patch_event(self, patch: TopologyPatch, revision: int) -&gt; None:\n        spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n        draft = EventDraft(kind=EventKind.TOPOLOGY_APPLIED,\n                           payload={\"patch_id\": patch.patch_id, \"revision\": revision,\n                                    \"decided_by\": patch.decided_by,\n                                    \"operations\": patch.operations})\n        action = TeamAction(action_id=f\"patch-apply:{patch.patch_id}\",\n                            session_id=self.session_id, actor_id=patch.decided_by or patch.proposer,\n                            kind=ActionKind.APPLY_TOPOLOGY_PATCH, payload={})\n        self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])\n\n    def _emit_limit_reached(self) -&gt; None:\n        key = f\"limit_notified:{self._current_goal_id()}\"\n        if self.store.get_meta(key):\n            return\n        self.store.set_meta(key, \"1\")\n        spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n        draft = EventDraft(kind=EventKind.LIMIT_REACHED,\n                           payload={\"kind\": \"max_turns_per_goal\",\n                                    \"limit\": spec.limits.max_turns_per_goal,\n                                    \"goal_id\": self._current_goal_id()})\n        action = TeamAction(action_id=new_id(\"sys\"), session_id=self.session_id,\n                            actor_id=\"system\", kind=ActionKind.PAUSE_SESSION, payload={})\n        self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------- small helpers\n\n    def _agent_has_live_run(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; bool:\n        return any(r.agent_id == agent_id for r in self.store.runs_for_session(\n            self.session_id,\n            [TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK,\n             TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL]))\n\n    def _active_runs(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; list[TurnRun]:\n        return [r for r in self.store.runs_for_session(\n            self.session_id, [TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING])\n            if r.agent_id == agent_id]\n\n    def _waiting_run(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; TurnRun | None:\n        \"\"\"The member's parked turn (waiting on tasks or on an approval).\"\"\"\n        for r in self.store.runs_for_session(\n                self.session_id, [TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK, TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL]):\n            if r.agent_id == agent_id:\n                return r\n        return None\n\n    def _next_ready_task(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; Task | None:\n        for task in sorted(self.store.tasks_for_session(self.session_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING]),\n                           key=lambda t: t.created_at):\n            if task.assignee != agent_id:\n                continue\n            deps = [self.store.get_task(d) for d in task.dependencies]\n            if all(d is not None and d.status is TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED for d in deps):\n                return task\n        return None\n\n    def _turn_budget_ok(self) -&gt; bool:\n        goal_id = self._current_goal_id()\n        if not goal_id:\n            return True\n        spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n        used = self.store.count_goal_runs(self.session_id, goal_id)\n        return used &lt; spec.limits.max_turns_per_goal\n\n    def _task_result(self, task_id: str) -&gt; dict:\n        task = self.store.get_task(task_id)\n        return {\"task_id\": task_id, \"status\": task.status if task else \"UNKNOWN\",\n                \"result_refs\": task.result_refs if task else []}\n\n    def _context_ref(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; str:\n        epoch = self.store.agent_context_epoch(self.session_id, agent_id)\n        return f\"ctx:{agent_id}:{epoch}\"\n\n    def _ensure_goal(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; str:\n        session = self.store.get_session(self.session_id)\n        goal_id = session[\"goal_id\"] if session else None\n        if goal_id and session[\"goal_state\"] in (\"active\", \"done\"):\n            if session[\"goal_state\"] == \"done\":\n                goal_id = new_id(\"goal\")\n                self.store.set_goal_state(self.session_id, goal_id, \"active\")\n            return goal_id\n        goal_id = goal_id or new_id(\"goal\")\n        self.store.set_goal_state(self.session_id, goal_id, \"active\")\n        return goal_id\n\n    def _current_goal_id(self) -&gt; str | None:\n        session = self.store.get_session(self.session_id)\n        return session[\"goal_id\"] if session else None\n\n    def _wake_approval_run(self, run_id: str) -&gt; None:\n        run = self.store.get_run(run_id)\n        if run is not None and run.status is TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL:\n            self.store.update_run_status_where(\n                run_id, TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL, TurnStatus.RUNNING)\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"Session runtime: member execution handles, concurrency, turn lifecycle.\n\nThe runtime consumes persisted execution intents (QUEUED TurnRuns) created by\nthe control flow, starts each member's own execution, and writes results back\nthrough one serialized path. It never waits for all members before reacting;\none member's crash or pause changes only that member's execution state.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport contextlib\nimport logging\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Callable\n\nfrom .agents import AgentRunner, FakeMember, TEAM_TOOLS, ToolGateway, TurnOutcome, WakeInfo\nfrom .control import Control, EventDraft\nfrom .models import (\n    ActionKind,\n    AgentStatus,\n    ApprovalStatus,\n    EventKind,\n    Receipt,\n    SessionStatus,\n    TaskStatus,\n    TeamAction,\n    TurnRun,\n    TurnStatus,\n    UserConfig,\n    new_id,\n)\nfrom .permissions import ApprovalGate, PermissionPolicy\nfrom .storage import Store\nfrom .views import build_agent_view\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(\"teamagents.runtime\")\n\n\nclass SessionRuntime:\n    \"\"\"Owns execution for one session. Single writer, single executor loop.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, store: Store, session_id: str,\n                 catalog: UserConfig | None = None,\n                 runners: dict[str, AgentRunner] | None = None,\n                 approvals: ApprovalGate | None = None,\n                 tool_executor: Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], Any] | None = None,\n                 lock: asyncio.Lock | None = None,\n                 cleanup: Callable[[], Any] | None = None,\n                 runner_factory: Callable[[Any], AgentRunner] | None = None):\n        self.store = store\n        self.session_id = session_id\n        self.catalog = catalog or UserConfig()\n        self.runners: dict[str, AgentRunner] = runners or {}\n        self.approvals = approvals or ApprovalGate(\n            store, session_id, PermissionPolicy())\n        self.tool_executor = tool_executor\n        self.lock = lock or asyncio.Lock()\n        self.cleanup = cleanup\n        self.runner_factory = runner_factory\n        #: UI sinks; high-frequency deltas are coalesced by the consumer, never\n        #: persisted (plan section 13: bounded queue, coalesced refresh)\n        self.stream_sink: Callable[[str, str, str], None] | None = None\n        self.control = Control(store, session_id, self.catalog)\n        self._inflight: dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}\n        self._cancel_tasks: dict[str, asyncio.Task] = {}\n        self._wake = asyncio.Event()\n        self._closed = False\n        self._loop_task: asyncio.Task | None = None\n        self._step_counts: dict[str, int] = {}\n        self._runner_revisions: dict[str, int] = {}\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------- lifecycle\n\n    def add_runner(self, agent_id: str, runner: AgentRunner) -&gt; None:\n        self.runners[agent_id] = runner\n\n    def _refresh_runner_if_stale(self, agent_id: str, spec) -&gt; bool:\n        \"\"\"Rebuild a member's backend when its config revision moved (model change,\n        tool change...). Returns True when a rebuild was scheduled (skip this run\n        until the swap completes).\"\"\"\n        if self.runner_factory is None:\n            return False\n        revision = self.store.agent_config_revision(self.session_id, agent_id)\n        known = self._runner_revisions.get(agent_id)\n        if known is None:\n            self._runner_revisions[agent_id] = revision\n            return False\n        if known == revision:\n            return False\n        try:\n            new_runner = self.runner_factory(spec.agent(agent_id))\n        except Exception as e:\n            self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, agent_id, AgentStatus.IDLE)\n            self._emit([EventDraft(kind=EventKind.MEMBER_STATUS,\n                                   payload={\"agent_id\": agent_id,\n                                            \"status\": \"REBUILD_FAILED\",\n                                            \"error\": str(e)})],\n                       actor_id=\"system\")\n            self._runner_revisions[agent_id] = revision\n            return False\n        old = self.runners.get(agent_id)\n        self.runners[agent_id] = new_runner\n        self._runner_revisions[agent_id] = revision\n        if hasattr(new_runner, \"status_hook\"):\n            new_runner.status_hook = self.note_external_status\n        if hasattr(new_runner, \"progress_hook\"):\n            new_runner.progress_hook = self.note_external_progress\n        if hasattr(new_runner, \"stream_hook\"):\n            new_runner.stream_hook = self.note_stream_chunk\n        close = getattr(old, \"aclose\", None)\n        if close is not None:\n            asyncio.create_task(close())\n        self._wake.set()\n        return True\n\n    async def start(self) -&gt; None:\n        self._closed = False\n        await self.reconcile()\n        self._loop_task = asyncio.create_task(self._loop(), name=f\"ta-loop-{self.session_id}\")\n\n    async def close(self) -&gt; None:\n        self._closed = True\n        self._wake.set()\n        for task in list(self._inflight.values()):\n            task.cancel()\n        if self._inflight:\n            await asyncio.gather(*self._inflight.values(), return_exceptions=True)\n        if self._loop_task:\n            with contextlib.suppress(asyncio.CancelledError):\n                await self._loop_task\n        if self.cleanup is not None:\n            result = self.cleanup()\n            if asyncio.iscoroutine(result):\n                await result\n\n    async def reconcile(self) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"After a restart: re-check runs that were in flight, never blind-retry side effects.\"\"\"\n        for run in self.store.runs_for_session(self.session_id, [TurnStatus.RUNNING]):\n            runner = self.runners.get(run.agent_id)\n            state = runner.query_state(run.run_id) if runner is not None else None\n            if state is None and runner is not None and hasattr(runner, \"reconcile\"):\n                state = await runner.reconcile(run)\n            if state is not None:\n                self.store.set_run_status(run.run_id, state)\n                continue\n            if run.external_turn_id:\n                self.store.set_run_status(run.run_id, TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN)\n                self._emit([EventDraft(kind=EventKind.RUN_FAILED,\n                                       payload={\"run_id\": run.run_id, \"agent_id\": run.agent_id,\n                                                \"status\": \"OUTCOME_UNKNOWN\"})],\n                           actor_id=\"system\")\n            else:\n                # in-process runner only: safe to re-run the segment\n                self.store.update_run_status_where(\n                    run.run_id, TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.QUEUED)\n        for run in self.store.runs_for_session(self.session_id, [TurnStatus.QUEUED]):\n            _ = run\n        self._wake.set()\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------- ingress\n\n    def submit(self, action: TeamAction) -&gt; Receipt:\n        \"\"\"Synchronous submit through the single serialized control entry.\"\"\"\n        receipt = self.control.submit(action)\n        if action.kind is ActionKind.APPROVAL_DECISION and receipt.ok:\n            self._deliver_approval_decision(action.payload[\"approval_id\"],\n                                            action.payload[\"decision\"])\n        self._drain_mid_turn()\n        self._wake.set()\n        return receipt\n\n    def _deliver_approval_decision(self, approval_id: str, decision: str) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"External backends (Codex) hold a live turn parked on the decision.\"\"\"\n        approval = self.store.get_approval(approval_id)\n        if approval is None:\n            return\n        run = self.store.get_run(approval.run_id)\n        runner = self.runners.get(run.agent_id) if run else None\n        resolve = getattr(runner, \"resolve_approval\", None)\n        if resolve is not None:\n            resolve(approval_id, decision)\n\n    def note_external_status(self, run_id: str, status: TurnStatus) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"A backend (Codex) reports live status changes for a parked turn.\"\"\"\n        run = self.store.get_run(run_id)\n        if run is None or run.status in (TurnStatus.COMPLETED, TurnStatus.FAILED,\n                                          TurnStatus.CANCELLED, TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN):\n            return\n        self.store.set_run_status(run_id, status)\n        if status is TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL:\n            self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, run.agent_id, AgentStatus.WAITING)\n            approvals = [a for a in self.store.pending_approvals(self.session_id)\n                         if a is not None and a.run_id == run_id]\n            if approvals:\n                self._emit([EventDraft(\n                    kind=EventKind.APPROVAL_REQUESTED,\n                    payload={\"approval_id\": approvals[-1].approval_id,\n                             \"agent_id\": run.agent_id, \"run_id\": run_id,\n                             \"scope\": approvals[-1].requested_scope})],\n                    actor_id=run.agent_id)\n        elif status is TurnStatus.RUNNING:\n            self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, run.agent_id, AgentStatus.BUSY)\n        self._wake.set()\n\n    def note_external_progress(self, run_id: str, text: str) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"A backend reports structured progress; it becomes a team event.\"\"\"\n        run = self.store.get_run(run_id)\n        if run is None or not text:\n            return\n        task = self.store.get_task(run.task_id) if run.task_id else None\n        self._emit([EventDraft(kind=EventKind.RUN_PROGRESS,\n                               payload={\"run_id\": run_id, \"agent_id\": run.agent_id,\n                                        \"text\": text[:2000],\n                                        \"requester\": task.requester if task else None,\n                                        \"task_id\": run.task_id})],\n                   actor_id=run.agent_id)\n\n    def note_stream_chunk(self, run_id: str, agent_id: str, text: str) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Transient model output for the UI (not persisted).\"\"\"\n        if self.stream_sink is not None and text:\n            self.stream_sink(run_id, agent_id, text)\n\n    def set_stream_sink(self, sink: Callable[[str, str, str], None] | None) -&gt; None:\n        self.stream_sink = sink\n\n    async def submit_async(self, action: TeamAction) -&gt; Receipt:\n        async with self.lock:\n            return self.submit(action)\n\n    def user_message(self, text: str, action_id: str | None = None,\n                     supplement: bool = False) -&gt; Receipt:\n        return self.submit(TeamAction(\n            action_id=action_id or new_id(\"user\"),\n            session_id=self.session_id,\n            actor_id=\"user\",\n            kind=ActionKind.USER_SUPPLEMENT if supplement else ActionKind.USER_MESSAGE,\n            payload={\"text\": text},\n        ))\n\n    def _drain_mid_turn(self) -&gt; None:\n        pushes = getattr(self.control, \"mid_turn_pushes\", [])\n        while pushes:\n            run_id, items = pushes.pop(0)\n            runner = None\n            run = self.store.get_run(run_id)\n            if run is not None:\n                runner = self.runners.get(run.agent_id)\n            if runner is not None:\n                runner.deliver_mid_turn(run_id, items)\n\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- executor\n\n    async def _loop(self) -&gt; None:\n        while not self._closed:\n            self._start_ready_runs()\n            if self._inflight:\n                done, _ = await asyncio.wait(\n                    list(self._inflight.values()) + [asyncio.create_task(self._wake.wait())],\n                    return_when=asyncio.FIRST_COMPLETED)\n                self._wake.clear()\n            else:\n                await self._wake.wait()\n                self._wake.clear()\n\n    def _start_ready_runs(self) -&gt; None:\n        session = self.store.get_session(self.session_id)\n        if session is None or SessionStatus(session[\"status\"]) is SessionStatus.PAUSED:\n            return\n        spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n        leaders_busy = sum(1 for r in self._inflight.values() if not r.done())\n        workers_busy = 0\n        for run_id in self._inflight:\n            run = self.store.get_run(run_id)\n            # a turn parked on an approval or a wait does not hold an execution slot\n            if (run is not None and run.agent_id != spec.leader_id\n                    and run.status in (TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING)):\n                workers_busy += 1\n        for run in self.store.runs_for_session(self.session_id,\n                                               [TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING]):\n            if run.run_id in self._inflight:\n                continue\n            if any(a.id == run.agent_id for a in spec.agents):\n                if self._refresh_runner_if_stale(run.agent_id, spec):\n                    continue  # a fresh backend is ready on the next pass\n            if run.agent_id in self.runners:\n                if run.agent_id == spec.leader_id:\n                    pass  # the Leader keeps its own slot\n                elif workers_busy &gt;= spec.limits.max_parallel_workers:\n                    continue\n            elif self.runner_factory is not None:\n                # members added by a topology patch get their backend on demand\n                try:\n                    self.runners[run.agent_id] = self.runner_factory(\n                        spec.agent(run.agent_id))\n                except Exception as e:\n                    self._finalize(run, TurnOutcome(\n                        status=TurnStatus.FAILED,\n                        error=f\"cannot start member {run.agent_id!r}: {e}\"))\n                    continue\n            else:\n                continue\n            status = self.store.agent_status(self.session_id, run.agent_id)\n            if status is AgentStatus.REMOVED:\n                continue\n            task = asyncio.create_task(self._execute(run), name=f\"run-{run.run_id}\")\n            self._inflight[run.run_id] = task\n            if run.agent_id != spec.leader_id:\n                workers_busy += 1\n            leaders_busy += 1\n            _ = leaders_busy\n        self._watch_cancellations()\n\n    def _watch_cancellations(self) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Ask the owning backend to stop runs the user/Leader cancelled.\"\"\"\n        for run_id, task in list(self._inflight.items()):\n            if task.done() or run_id in self._cancel_tasks:\n                continue\n            if not self.store.run_cancel_requested(run_id):\n                continue\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
