None\n            if runner is None:\n                continue\n            self._cancel_tasks[run_id] = asyncio.create_task(\n                self._request_stop(run, runner), name=f\"cancel-{run_id}\")\n\n    async def _request_stop(self, run: TurnRun, runner: AgentRunner) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Cancellation waits for the backend to confirm the stop, or marks\n        OUTCOME_UNKNOWN once the confirmation timeout expires.\"\"\"\n        spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n        try:\n            await asyncio.wait_for(runner.request_interrupt(run.run_id),\n                                   timeout=spec.limits.cancel_confirm_timeout_s)\n        except asyncio.TimeoutError:\n            self.store.update_run_status_where(\n                run.run_id, TurnStatus.RUNNING, TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN)\n        finally:\n            self._cancel_tasks.pop(run.run_id, None)\n            self._wake.set()\n\n    async def _execute(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; None:\n        try:\n            await self._execute_inner(run)\n        except asyncio.CancelledError:\n            raise\n        except Exception as e:  # a member crash must not take the team down\n            log.exception(\"run %s crashed\", run.run_id)\n            self._finalize(run, TurnOutcome(status=TurnStatus.FAILED,\n                                            error=f\"{type(e).__name__}: {e}\"))\n        finally:\n            self._inflight.pop(run.run_id, None)\n            self._cancel_tasks.pop(run.run_id, None)\n            self._wake.set()\n\n    async def _execute_inner(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; None:\n        spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n        runner = self.runners.get(run.agent_id)\n        if runner is None:\n            self._finalize(run, TurnOutcome(status=TurnStatus.FAILED,\n                                            error=f\"no runner for member {run.agent_id}\"))\n            return\n        if run.status is TurnStatus.QUEUED:\n            self.store.set_run_status(run.run_id, TurnStatus.RUNNING)\n        run = self.store.get_run(run.run_id) or run\n        self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, run.agent_id, AgentStatus.BUSY)\n        wake = self._wake_info(run)\n        if run.task_id:\n            task = self.store.get_task(run.task_id)\n            if task is not None and self.store.compare_and_set_task(\n                    run.task_id, TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING):\n                self._emit([EventDraft(kind=EventKind.TASK_STARTED,\n                                       payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id,\n                                                \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                                                \"requester\": task.requester,\n                                                \"status\": \"RUNNING\"},\n                                       task_id=task.task_id)],\n                           actor_id=run.agent_id)\n        self._emit([EventDraft(kind=EventKind.RUN_STARTED,\n                               payload={\"run_id\": run.run_id, \"agent_id\": run.agent_id,\n                                        \"status\": \"RUNNING\", \"wake\": wake.reason})],\n                   actor_id=run.agent_id)\n\n        gateway = ToolGateway(self.control, self.session_id, run.agent_id, run.run_id,\n                              self.approvals, self._guarded_executor(run))\n        view = build_agent_view(self.store, spec, self.session_id, run.agent_id, run)\n        timeout = spec.limits.turn_active_timeout_s\n        try:\n            outcome = await asyncio.wait_for(\n                runner.start_or_resume(run, view, gateway, wake), timeout=timeout)\n        except asyncio.TimeoutError:\n            outcome = TurnOutcome(status=TurnStatus.FAILED,\n                                  error=f\"turn active-time limit {timeout}s reached\")\n\n        if self.store.run_cancel_requested(run.run_id) and outcome.status in (\n                TurnStatus.COMPLETED, TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK, TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL):\n            spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n            try:\n                confirmed = await asyncio.wait_for(\n                    runner.request_interrupt(run.run_id),\n                    timeout=spec.limits.cancel_confirm_timeout_s)\n            except asyncio.TimeoutError:\n                confirmed = TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN\n            outcome = TurnOutcome(status=confirmed, note=\"cancelled by request\")\n        self._finalize(run, outcome)\n\n    def _guarded_executor(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], Any]:\n        def execute(tool_name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -&gt; Any:\n            spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n            used = self._step_counts.get(run.run_id, 0) + 1\n            self._step_counts[run.run_id] = used\n            if used &gt; spec.limits.max_model_steps_per_turn:\n                raise RuntimeError(\n                    f\"step limit {spec.limits.max_model_steps_per_turn} reached for this turn\")\n            if self.tool_executor is None:\n                raise RuntimeError(f\"no tool executor configured for {tool_name!r}\")\n            return self.tool_executor(tool_name, args)\n        return execute\n\n    def _wake_info(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; WakeInfo:\n        decisions = self.store.decided_approvals_for_run(run.run_id)\n        if decisions:\n            return WakeInfo(reason=\"approval\",\n                            payload={\"decisions\": [{\"approval_id\": d.approval_id,\n                                                    \"status\": d.status} for d in decisions],\n                                     \"denied\": any(d.status is ApprovalStatus.DENIED\n                                                   for d in decisions)})\n        kinds = self.store.delivery_event_kinds(run.input_delivery_ids)\n        if kinds and kinds[-1] == EventKind.USER_MESSAGE:\n            return WakeInfo(reason=\"user_input\", payload={\"kinds\": kinds})\n        if run.waiting_on:\n            results = {tid: self._task_snapshot(tid) for tid in run.waiting_on}\n            return WakeInfo(reason=\"task_results\",\n                            payload={\"task_ids\": run.waiting_on, \"results\": results})\n        return WakeInfo(reason=\"new_input\", payload={})\n\n    def _task_snapshot(self, task_id: str) -&gt; dict[str, Any]:\n        task = self.store.get_task(task_id)\n        if task is None:\n            return {\"task_id\": task_id, \"status\": \"UNKNOWN\"}\n        return {\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"status\": task.status,\n                \"result_refs\": task.result_refs}\n\n    # -------------------------------------------------------------- finalize\n\n    def _finalize(self, run: TurnRun, outcome: TurnOutcome) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Apply the turn result: completion requests, task semantics, deliveries.\"\"\"\n        events: list[EventDraft] = []\n        req = self.store.completion_request(run.run_id)\n        terminal = outcome.status in (TurnStatus.COMPLETED, TurnStatus.FAILED,\n                                      TurnStatus.CANCELLED, TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN)\n        with self.store.tx():\n            if outcome.status is TurnStatus.COMPLETED and req is not None:\n                if req[\"task_id\"]:\n                    task = self.store.get_task(req[\"task_id\"])\n                    refs = [str(r) for r in __import__(\"json\").loads(req[\"result_refs\"])]\n                    if task is not None and self.store.compare_and_set_task(\n                            task.task_id, task.status, TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED, result_refs=refs):\n                        waiters = self.store.waiters_for_task(self.session_id, task.task_id)\n                        events.append(EventDraft(\n                            kind=EventKind.TASK_COMPLETED,\n                            payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                                     \"requester\": task.requester, \"status\": \"SUCCEEDED\",\n                                     \"result_refs\": refs,\n                                     \"summary\": req[\"summary\"]},\n                            task_id=task.task_id,\n                            push=sorted(set(waiters) | {task.requester}),\n                        ))\n                else:\n                    self.store.set_goal_state(self.session_id, run.goal_id or \"\", \"done\")\n                    events.append(EventDraft(\n                        kind=EventKind.GOAL_DONE,\n                        payload={\"goal_id\": run.goal_id, \"agent_id\": run.agent_id,\n                                 \"summary\": req[\"summary\"]},\n                        push=[],\n                    ))\n            elif outcome.status is TurnStatus.COMPLETED and run.task_id and req is None:\n                task = self.store.get_task(run.task_id)\n                if task is not None and task.status in (TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING):\n                    if self.store.compare_and_set_task(task.task_id, task.status,\n                                                       TaskStatus.BLOCKED):\n                        events.append(EventDraft(\n                            kind=EventKind.TASK_BLOCKED,\n                            payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                                     \"requester\": task.requester,\n                                     \"reason\": \"turn ended without complete_task or wait_for_tasks\"},\n                            task_id=task.task_id,\n                        ))\n            elif outcome.status is TurnStatus.FAILED and run.task_id:\n                task = self.store.get_task(run.task_id)\n                if task is not None and task.status in (TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING):\n                    if self.store.compare_and_set_task(task.task_id, task.status,\n                                                       TaskStatus.FAILED):\n                        events.append(EventDraft(\n                            kind=EventKind.TASK_FAILED,\n                            payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                                     \"requester\": task.requester, \"error\": outcome.error},\n                            task_id=task.task_id,\n                        ))\n            elif outcome.status is TurnStatus.CANCELLED and run.task_id:\n                task = self.store.get_task(run.task_id)\n                if task is not None and task.status in (TaskStatus.PENDING, TaskStatus.RUNNING):\n                    if self.store.compare_and_set_task(task.task_id, task.status,\n                                                       TaskStatus.CANCELLED):\n                        events.append(EventDraft(\n                            kind=EventKind.TASK_CANCELLED,\n                            payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                                     \"requester\": task.requester,\n                                     \"status\": \"CANCELLED\"},\n                            task_id=task.task_id,\n                        ))\n\n        if outcome.status is TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL:\n            pending = [a for a in self.store.pending_approvals(self.session_id)\n                       if a is not None and a.run_id == run.run_id]\n            for a in pending:\n                events.append(EventDraft(\n                    kind=EventKind.APPROVAL_REQUESTED,\n                    payload={\"approval_id\": a.approval_id, \"agent_id\": a.agent_id,\n                             \"run_id\": a.run_id, \"scope\": a.requested_scope},\n                ))\n\n        self.store.set_run_status(run.run_id, outcome.status)\n        if outcome.status is TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK:\n            self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, run.agent_id, AgentStatus.WAITING)\n        elif outcome.status is TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL:\n            self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, run.agent_id, AgentStatus.WAITING)\n        else:\n            self.store.set_agent_status(self.session_id, run.agent_id, AgentStatus.IDLE)\n        self.store.ack_run_deliveries(run)\n        self._step_counts.pop(run.run_id, None)\n\n        if terminal:\n            if outcome.note == \"turn_limit\":\n                events.append(EventDraft(\n                    kind=EventKind.LIMIT_REACHED,\n                    payload={\"kind\": \"max_model_steps_per_turn\", \"run_id\": run.run_id,\n                             \"agent_id\": run.agent_id, \"detail\": outcome.error}))\n            if (outcome.status is TurnStatus.COMPLETED and outcome.reply_text\n                    and run.agent_id == self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id).leader_id):\n                events.append(EventDraft(kind=EventKind.LEADER_REPLY,\n                                         payload={\"text\": outcome.reply_text}))\n            elif outcome.status is TurnStatus.COMPLETED and outcome.reply_text:\n                events.append(EventDraft(\n                    kind=EventKind.RUN_PROGRESS,\n                    payload={\"run_id\": run.run_id, \"agent_id\": run.agent_id,\n                             \"text\": outcome.reply_text[:2000], \"final\": True,\n                             \"task_id\": run.task_id}))\n            events.append(EventDraft(\n                kind={TurnStatus.COMPLETED: EventKind.RUN_COMPLETED,\n                      TurnStatus.FAILED: EventKind.RUN_FAILED,\n                      TurnStatus.CANCELLED: EventKind.RUN_CANCELLED,\n                      TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN: EventKind.RUN_FAILED}[outcome.status],\n                payload={\"run_id\": run.run_id, \"agent_id\": run.agent_id,\n                         \"status\": outcome.status, \"error\": outcome.error},\n            ))\n        if events:\n            self._emit(events, actor_id=run.agent_id)\n        self.control.schedule()\n        self._drain_mid_turn()\n\n    def _emit(self, drafts: list[EventDraft], actor_id: str = \"system\") -&gt; None:\n        self.control.emit(drafts, actor_id=actor_id)\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ idle\n\n    async def settle(self, timeout: float = 10.0) -&gt; bool:\n        \"\"\"Test/CLI helper: wait until no runs are executing or queued.\"\"\"\n        deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + timeout\n        while True:\n            self._wake.set()\n            queued = self.store.runs_for_session(\n                self.session_id, [TurnStatus.QUEUED, TurnStatus.RUNNING])\n            inflight = [t for t in self._inflight.values() if not t.done()]\n            if not queued and not inflight:\n                return True\n            if asyncio.get_event_loop().time() &gt; deadline:\n                return False\n            await asyncio.sleep(0.02)\n\n\ndef default_barriers() -&gt; dict[str, asyncio.Event]:\n    return {}\n\n\ndef fake_session(tmpdir: str | Path, spec, runners: dict[str, FakeMember],\n                 catalog: UserConfig | None = None,\n                 pre_authorized: set[str] | None = None,\n                 require_approval: set[str] | None = None,\n                 tool_executor: Callable[[str, dict[str, Any]], Any] | None = None,\n                 session_id: str = \"s1\") -&gt; SessionRuntime:\n    \"\"\"Build a ready-to-run session with scripted members (tests, examples).\"\"\"\n    from .models import PermissionMode\n\n    path = Path(tmpdir) / f\"{session_id}.db\"\n    store = Store(path)\n    if store.get_session(session_id) is None:\n        store.create_session(session_id, str(tmpdir), \"approved_scope\")\n        store.save_team_spec(session_id, spec)\n        for agent in spec.agents:\n            store.ensure_agent(session_id, agent.id)\n    policy = PermissionPolicy(mode=PermissionMode.APPROVED_SCOPE,\n                              pre_authorized=pre_authorized,\n                              require_approval=require_approval)\n    runtime = SessionRuntime(store, session_id, catalog or UserConfig(),\n                             runners=dict(runners),\n                             approvals=ApprovalGate(store, session_id, policy),\n                             tool_executor=tool_executor)\n    return runtime\n\n\n_ = (TEAM_TOOLS, FakeMember)\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "     1\t\"\"\"Information permissions: event audience, delivery push, and AgentView building.\n     2\t\n     3\tTwo layers (plan sections 2.2 rule 4 and 7):\n     4\t  * ``audience`` — who may see an event at all (queries, observers, TUI).\n     5\t  * ``push`` — who receives it as an inbox delivery (context injection).\n     6\tThe Leader sees task progress/results/directed messages in the log (section 1),\n     7\tbut private member contexts are never injected into the Leader.\n     8\t\"\"\"\n     9\t\n    10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n    11\t\n    12\timport json\n    13\t\n    14\tfrom .models import (\n    15\t    AgentView,\n    16\t    EventKind,\n    17\t    ObserverSpec,\n    18\t    SharedEntry,\n    19\t    TeamSpec,\n    20\t    TurnRun,\n    21\t)\n    22\tfrom .storage import Store\n    23\t\n    24\t#: Event kinds whose payloads may contain private content (message text, results).\n    25\tCONTENT_KINDS = {\n    26\t    EventKind.USER_MESSAGE,\n    27\t    EventKind.MESSAGE,\n    28\t    EventKind.TASK_COMPLETED,\n    29\t    EventKind.TASK_FAILED,\n    30\t    EventKind.TASK_BLOCKED,\n    31\t    EventKind.SHARED_PUBLISHED,\n    32\t}\n    33\t\n    34\t\n    35\tdef observer_matches(ob: ObserverSpec, kind: EventKind, actor_id: str,\n    36\t                     subject_ids: set[str]) -&gt; bool:\n    37\t    if ob.subjects and not (subject_ids &amp; set(ob.subjects)):\n    38\t        return False\n    39\t    return not ob.event_types or kind in ob.event_types\n    40\t\n    41\t\n    42\tdef observer_scope_for(spec: TeamSpec, recipient: str, kind: EventKind, actor_id: str,\n    43\t                       payload: dict) -&gt; str | None:\n    44\t    \"\"\"The payload scope this recipient sees as an observer (None = direct participant).\"\"\"\n    45\t    subjects = _observer_subjects(kind, actor_id, payload)\n    46\t    if recipient in subjects or recipient == actor_id:\n    47\t        return None\n    48\t    for ob in spec.observers:\n    49\t        if ob.agent_id == recipient and observer_matches(ob, kind, actor_id, subjects):\n    50\t            return ob.payload_scope\n    51\t    return None\n    52\t\n    53\t\n    54\tdef _observer_subjects(kind: EventKind, actor_id: str, payload: dict) -&gt; set[str]:\n    55\t    \"\"\"Which members an event is *about* (for observer subject matching).\"\"\"\n    56\t    subs = {actor_id}\n    57\t    for key in (\"assignee\", \"requester\", \"target\", \"author\", \"agent_id\"):\n    58\t        if isinstance(v := payload.get(key), str):\n    59\t            subs.add(v)\n    60\t    return subs\n    61\t\n    62\t\n    63\tdef scope_payload(scope: str, kind: EventKind, payload: dict) -&gt; dict:\n    64\t    \"\"\"Observer payload scoping: status &lt; public_message &lt; result.\"\"\"\n    65\t    if scope == \"status\":\n    66\t        return {k: v for k, v in payload.items() if k in\n    67\t                (\"status\", \"task_id\", \"run_id\", \"agent_id\", \"assignee\", \"requester\", \"kind\")}\n    68\t    if scope == \"public_message\":\n    69\t        if kind in (EventKind.MESSAGE, EventKind.USER_MESSAGE):\n    70\t            return payload\n    71\t        return {k: v for k, v in payload.items() if k in\n    72\t                (\"status\", \"task_id\", \"run_id\", \"agent_id\", \"assignee\", \"requester\",\n    73\t                 \"summary\", \"description\")}\n    74\t    return payload  # result\n    75\t\n    76\t\n    77\tdef event_audience(spec: TeamSpec, kind: EventKind, actor_id: str,\n    78\t                   payload: dict, targets: list[str] | None = None) -&gt; list[str]:\n    79\t    \"\"\"Who may see this event; observers are added per their subscription.\"\"\"\n    80\t    all_members = {a.id for a in spec.agents}\n    81\t    audience: set[str] = set()\n    82\t    match kind:\n    83\t        case EventKind.USER_MESSAGE:\n    84\t            audience = {spec.leader_id}\n    85\t        case EventKind.LEADER_REPLY:\n    86\t            audience = {spec.leader_id}\n    87\t        case EventKind.MESSAGE:\n    88\t            audience = set(targets or []) | {spec.leader_id}\n    89\t        case EventKind.TASK_CREATED | EventKind.TASK_STARTED | EventKind.TASK_COMPLETED \\\n    90\t                | EventKind.TASK_FAILED | EventKind.TASK_CANCELLED | EventKind.TASK_BLOCKED \\\n    91\t                | EventKind.TASK_READY:\n    92\t            audience = {actor_id, spec.leader_id}\n    93\t            audience |= {payload[k] for k in (\"assignee\", \"requester\") if payload.get(k)}\n    94\t            audience &amp;= all_members\n    95\t        case EventKind.SHARED_PUBLISHED:\n    96\t            space_id = payload.get(\"space_id\")\n    97\t            if space_id:\n    98\t                sp = spec.space(space_id)\n    99\t                audience = set(sp.readers) | set(sp.writers)\n   100\t        case EventKind.APPROVAL_REQUESTED | EventKind.APPROVAL_DECIDED:\n   101\t            audience = {spec.leader_id, payload.get(\"agent_id\", actor_id)}\n   102\t        case EventKind.RUN_PROGRESS:\n   103\t            audience = {spec.leader_id, actor_id}\n   104\t            if who := payload.get(\"requester\"):\n   105\t                audience.add(who)\n   106\t        case EventKind.TOPOLOGY_PROPOSED | EventKind.TOPOLOGY_APPLIED | EventKind.TOPOLOGY_REJECTED:\n   107\t            audience = {spec.leader_id, actor_id}\n   108\t        case _:\n   109\t            audience = {spec.leader_id}\n   110\t    subjects = _observer_subjects(kind, actor_id, payload)\n   111\t    for ob in spec.observers:\n   112\t        if observer_matches(ob, kind, actor_id, subjects):\n   113\t            audience.add(ob.agent_id)\n   114\t    return sorted(a for a in audience if a in all_members)\n   115\t\n   116\t\n   117\tdef event_push(spec: TeamSpec, kind: EventKind, actor_id: str, payload: dict,\n   118\t               targets: list[str] | None = None) -&gt; list[str]:\n   119\t    \"\"\"Who receives this event as a context delivery.\"\"\"\n   120\t    all_members = {a.id for a in spec.agents}\n   121\t    push: set[str] = set()\n   122\t    match kind:\n   123\t        case EventKind.USER_MESSAGE:\n   124\t            goal_target = payload.get(\"to\") or spec.leader_id\n   125\t            push = {goal_target}\n   126\t        case EventKind.LEADER_REPLY:\n   127\t            push = set()\n   128\t        case EventKind.MESSAGE:\n   129\t            push = set(targets or [])\n   130\t        case EventKind.TASK_CREATED | EventKind.TASK_STARTED | EventKind.TASK_COMPLETED \\\n   131\t                | EventKind.TASK_FAILED | EventKind.TASK_CANCELLED | EventKind.TASK_BLOCKED \\\n   132\t                | EventKind.TASK_READY:\n   133\t            if kind is EventKind.TASK_READY and payload.get(\"assignee\"):\n   134\t                push.add(payload[\"assignee\"])\n   135\t            elif payload.get(\"requester\"):\n   136\t                push.add(payload[\"requester\"])\n   137\t            if kind in (EventKind.TASK_BLOCKED, EventKind.TASK_FAILED):\n   138\t                push.add(spec.leader_id)\n   139\t        case EventKind.TOPOLOGY_PROPOSED:\n   140\t            push = {spec.leader_id}\n   141\t        case EventKind.APPROVAL_REQUESTED:\n   142\t            push = {spec.leader_id}\n   143\t        case EventKind.RUN_PROGRESS:\n   144\t            push = set()\n   145\t        case EventKind.LIMIT_REACHED | EventKind.GOAL_DONE:\n   146\t            push = {spec.leader_id}\n   147\t        case _:\n   148\t            push = set()\n   149\t    subjects = _observer_subjects(kind, actor_id, payload)\n   150\t    for ob in spec.observers:\n   151\t        if ob.wake_policy == \"on_event\" and observer_matches(ob, kind, actor_id, subjects):\n   152\t            push.add(ob.agent_id)\n   153\t    return sorted(p for p in push if p in all_members and p != actor_id)\n   154\t\n   155\t\n   156\tdef build_agent_view(store: Store, spec: TeamSpec, session_id: str, agent_id: str,\n   157\t                     run: TurnRun | None = None) -&gt; AgentView:\n   158\t    \"\"\"What this member may see right now: assignments, inbox delta, shared delta.\"\"\"\n   159\t    assignment = [t for t in store.tasks_for_session(session_id) if t.assignee == agent_id]\n   160\t    pending = store.pending_deliveries(session_id, agent_id)\n   161\t    inbox: list[dict] = []\n   162\t    delivery_ids: list[int] = []\n   163\t    batch_max = 0\n   164\t    for d in pending:\n   165\t        payload = (json.loads(d[\"payload_override\"]) if d[\"payload_override\"]\n   166\t                   else json.loads(d[\"payload_json\"]))\n   167\t        inbox.append({\n   168\t            \"event_id\": d[\"event_id\"],\n   169\t            \"kind\": d[\"event_kind\"],\n   170\t            \"from\": d[\"event_actor\"],\n   171\t            \"task_id\": d[\"event_task_id\"],\n   172\t            \"payload\": payload,\n   173\t        })\n   174\t        delivery_ids.append(d[\"delivery_id\"])\n   175\t        batch_max = max(batch_max, d[\"batch_no\"])\n   176\t\n   177\t    readable_spaces = [s.id for s in spec.shared_spaces\n   178\t                       if agent_id in s.readers or agent_id in s.writers]\n   179\t    shared_delta: list[SharedEntry] = []\n   180\t    for space_id in readable_spaces:\n   181\t        cursor = store.shared_cursor(session_id, agent_id, space_id)\n   182\t        shared_delta.extend(store.shared_entries(session_id, [space_id],\n   183\t                                                 after_sequence=cursor, limit=50))\n   184\t    capabilities = sorted(set(spec.agent(agent_id).tool_bindings))\n   185\t    return AgentView(\n   186\t        agent_id=agent_id,\n   187\t        assignment=assignment,\n   188\t        inbox_delta=inbox,\n   189\t        permitted_shared_delta=shared_delta,\n   190\t        relevant_topology={\n   191\t            \"revision\": <message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"SQLite business database: the authority for team facts and action receipts.\n\nOne writer per session (the session runtime serializes via an asyncio lock),\ntransactions + constraints, WAL, busy timeout (plan section 9.1).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport sqlite3\nimport threading\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Iterable\n\nfrom .models import (\n    AgentStatus,\n    ApprovalRequest,\n    ApprovalStatus,\n    PatchStatus,\n    Receipt,\n    SessionStatus,\n    SharedEntry,\n    Task,\n    TaskStatus,\n    TeamEvent,\n    TeamSpec,\n    TopologyPatch,\n    TurnRun,\n    TurnStatus,\n)\n\nDB_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS meta(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions(\n  session_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n  status TEXT NOT NULL,\n  cwd TEXT NOT NULL,\n  permissions_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'approved_scope',\n  goal_id TEXT,\n  goal_state TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'idle',\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n  updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS team_specs(\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  revision INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  spec_json TEXT NOT NULL,\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n  PRIMARY KEY(session_id, revision)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tasks(\n  task_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  parent_task_id TEXT,\n  goal_id TEXT,\n  requester TEXT NOT NULL,\n  assignee TEXT NOT NULL,\n  description TEXT NOT NULL,\n  acceptance TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n  dependencies TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n  status TEXT NOT NULL,\n  result_refs TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n  cancel_requested INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n  updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_tasks_session ON tasks(session_id, status);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS turn_runs(\n  run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  task_id TEXT,\n  goal_id TEXT,\n  agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  config_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  topology_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  status TEXT NOT NULL,\n  input_delivery_ids TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n  context_ref TEXT,\n  external_turn_id TEXT,\n  cancel_requested INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  waiting_on TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n  updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n-- at most one active turn per member (plan section 2.2 rule 5)\nCREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS uniq_active_run\n  ON turn_runs(agent_id) WHERE status = 'RUNNING';\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_runs_session ON turn_runs(session_id, status);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS actions(\n  action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  actor_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  run_id TEXT,\n  kind TEXT NOT NULL,\n  payload_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n  receipt_json TEXT NOT NULL,\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS events(\n  sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n  event_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  actor_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  task_id TEXT,\n  kind TEXT NOT NULL,\n  payload_json TEXT NOT NULL,\n  audience_json TEXT NOT NULL,\n  topology_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  causation_id TEXT,\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_events_session ON events(session_id, sequence);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS deliveries(\n  delivery_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  event_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  batch_no INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',\n  payload_override TEXT,\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n  applied_at REAL\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_deliveries_agent ON deliveries(session_id, agent_id, status);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS agent_runtime(\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IDLE',\n  config_revision INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n  context_epoch INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n  last_applied_batch INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  next_batch_no INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,\n  external_thread_id TEXT,\n  updated_at REAL NOT NULL,\n  PRIMARY KEY(session_id, agent_id)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS approvals(\n  approval_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  run_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  tool_call_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  operation_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n  requested_scope TEXT NOT NULL,\n  policy_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  status TEXT NOT NULL,\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n  decided_at REAL\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_approvals_session ON approvals(session_id, status);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS shared_entries(\n  sequence INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,\n  entry_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  space_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  author TEXT NOT NULL,\n  kind TEXT NOT NULL,\n  content TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n  ref TEXT,\n  supersedes TEXT,\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_shared_space ON shared_entries(session_id, space_id, sequence);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS topology_patches(\n  patch_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  base_revision INTEGER NOT NULL,\n  proposer TEXT NOT NULL,\n  decided_by TEXT,\n  operations TEXT NOT NULL,\n  affected_agents TEXT NOT NULL,\n  status TEXT NOT NULL,\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n  updated_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS completion_requests(\n  run_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,\n  task_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  result_refs TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',\n  summary TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS shared_cursors(\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  agent_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  space_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  sequence INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  PRIMARY KEY(session_id, agent_id, space_id)\n);\n\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS session_approval_cache(\n  session_id TEXT NOT NULL,\n  operation_hash TEXT NOT NULL,\n  scope_json TEXT NOT NULL,\n  created_at REAL NOT NULL,\n  PRIMARY KEY(session_id, operation_hash)\n);\n\"\"\"\n\n\nclass Store:\n    \"\"\"Thin synchronous SQLite wrapper. The caller serializes writes.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, path: str | Path):\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "    def get_action_receipt(self, action_id: str) -&gt; Receipt | None:\n        row = self.conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT receipt_json FROM actions WHERE action_id=?\", (action_id,)\n        ).fetchone()\n        return Receipt.model_validate_json(row[\"receipt_json\"]) if row else None\n\n    def record_action(self, action_id: str, session_id: str, actor_id: str,\n                      run_id: str | None, kind: str, payload_hash: str, receipt: Receipt) -&gt; None:\n        self.conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO actions(action_id, session_id, actor_id, run_id, kind, payload_hash,\"\n            \" receipt_json, created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)\",\n            (action_id, session_id, actor_id, run_id, kind, payload_hash,\n             receipt.model_dump_json(), time.time()),\n        )\n\n    # -- events and deliveries ----------------------------------------------\n\n    def append_event(self, event: TeamEvent) -&gt; int:\n        \"\"\"Insert event, return its sequence. Caller holds the transaction.\"\"\"\n        cur = self.conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO events(event_id, session_id, actor_id, task_id, kind, payload_json,\"\n            \" audience_json, topology_revision, causation_id, created_at)\"\n            \" VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)\",\n            (event.event_id, event.session_id, event.actor_id, event.task_id, event.kind,\n             json.dumps(event.payload), json.dumps(event.audience),\n             event.topology_revision, event.causation_id, event.created_at),\n        )\n        return int(cur.lastrowid)\n\n    def events(self, session_id: str, after_sequence: int = 0, limit: int = 1000) -&gt; list[sqlite3.Row]:\n        return self.conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT * FROM events WHERE session_id=? AND sequence&gt;? ORDER BY sequence LIMIT ?\",\n            (session_id, after_sequence, limit),\n        ).fetchall()\n\n    def create_delivery(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str, event_id: str, batch_no: int,\n                        payload_override: dict | None = None) -&gt; int:\n        cur = self.conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO deliveries(session_id, agent_id, event_id, batch_no, status,\"\n            \" payload_override, created_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?, 'pending', ?, ?)\",\n            (session_id, agent_id, event_id, batch_no,\n             json.dumps(payload_override) if payload_override is not None else None,\n             time.time()),\n        )\n        return int(cur.lastrowid)\n\n    def next_batch_no(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str) -&gt; int:\n        \"\"\"Caller holds the transaction. Monotonic per member.\"\"\"\n        row = self.conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT next_batch_no FROM agent_runtime WHERE session_id=? AND agent_id=?\",\n            (session_id, agent_id),\n        ).fetchone()\n        batch = int(row[\"next_batch_no\"]) if row else 1\n        self.conn.execute(\n            \"INSERT INTO agent_runtime(session_id, agent_id, next_batch_no, updated_at)\"\n            \" VALUES(?,?,?,?)\"\n            \" ON CONFLICT(session_id, agent_id) DO UPDATE SET next_batch_no=excluded.next_batch_no,\"\n            \" updated_at=excluded.updated_at\",\n            (session_id, agent_id, batch + 1, time.time()),\n        )\n        return batch\n\n    def pending_deliveries(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str) -&gt; list[sqlite3.Row]:\n        return self.conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT d.*, e.kind AS event_kind, e.payload_json, e.actor_id AS event_actor,\"\n            \" e.task_id AS event_task_id, e.sequence AS event_sequence, e.audience_json\"\n            \" FROM deliveries d JOIN events e ON e.event_id = d.event_id\"\n            \" WHERE d.session_id=? AND d.agent_id=? AND d.status='pending'\"\n            \" ORDER BY d.batch_no, e.sequence\",\n            (session_id, agent_id),\n        ).fetchall()\n\n    def delivery_event_kinds(self, delivery_ids: list[int]) -&gt; list[str]:\n        if not delivery_ids:\n            return []\n        marks = \",\".join(\"?\" * len(delivery_ids))\n        rows = self.conn.execute(\n            f\"SELECT e.kind FROM deliveries d JOIN events e ON e.event_id=d.event_id\"\n            f\" WHERE d.delivery_id IN ({marks}) ORDER BY e.sequence\",\n            tuple(delivery_ids),\n        ).fetchall()\n        return [r[\"kind\"] for r in rows]\n\n    def applied_batch(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str) -&gt; int:\n        row = self.conn.execute(\n            \"SELECT last_applied_batch FROM agent_runtime WHERE session_id=? AND agent_id=?\",\n            (session_id, agent_id),\n        ).fetchone()\n        return int(row[\"last_applied_batch\"]) if row else 0\n\n    def ack_deliveries(self, session_id: str, agent_id: str, batch_no: int) -&gt; int:\n        \"\"\"Record that a member applied a delivery batch; advance consume cursor.\"\"\"\n        with self.tx():\n            cur = self.conn.execute(\n                \"UPDATE deliveries SET status='applied', applied_at=?\"\n                \" WHERE session_id=? AND agent_id=? AND batch_no&lt;=? AND status='pending'\",\n                (time.time(), session_id, agent_id, batch_no),\n            )\n            self.conn.execute(\n                \"INSERT INTO agent_runtime(session_id, agent_id, last_applied_batch, updated_at)\"\n                \" VALUES(?,?,?,?)\"\n                \" ON CONFLICT(session_id, agent_id) DO UPDATE SET\"\n                \" last_applied_batch=MAX(last_applied_batch, excluded.last_applied_batch),\"\n                \" updated_at=excluded.updated_at\",\n                (session_id, agent_id, batch_no, time.time()),\n            )\n            return cur.rowcount\n\n    def ack_run_deliveries(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Acknowledge every delivery the member applied during this run.\"\"\"\n        if not run.input_delivery_ids:\n            return\n        marks = \",\".join(\"?\" * len(run.input_delivery_ids))\n        row = self.conn.execute(\n            f\"SELECT MAX(batch_no) AS b FROM deliveries WHERE delivery_id IN ({marks})\",\n            tuple(run.input_delivery_ids),\n        ).fetchone()\n        if row and row[\"b\"] is not None:\n            self.ack_deliveries(run.session_id, run.agent_id, int(row[\"b\"]))\n\n    def waiters_for_task(self, session_id: str, task_id: str) -&gt; list[str]:\n        \"\"\"Members parked in WAITING_TASK on this task (to wake with the result).\"\"\"\n        out = []\n        for run in self.runs_for_session(session_id, [TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK]):\n            if task_id in run.waiting_on:\n                out.append(run.agent_id)\n        return out\n\n    def decided_approvals_for_run(self, run_id: str) -&gt; list[ApprovalRequest]:\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"DeepAgentsRunner: real Deep Agents member execution (plan section 10.1).\n\nThe runner owns one member's graph, its private checkpoint thread, its tool\nsurface (file tools from the Deep Agents backend, the isolated shell, MCP/web\ntools and the team tools) and the middleware that injects authorized input,\nenforces approvals and bounds the turn.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Annotated, Any\n\nfrom langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, ToolMessage\nfrom langchain_core.messages import AIMessageChunk\nfrom langchain_core.tools import BaseTool, InjectedToolCallId, tool\nfrom langchain.agents.middleware import AgentMiddleware\nfrom langchain.agents.middleware.types import AgentState\nfrom langgraph.types import Command, interrupt\n\nfrom deepagents import create_deep_agent\nfrom deepagents.backends import CompositeBackend, FilesystemBackend\n\nfrom .agents import TEAM_TOOLS, ToolGateway, TurnOutcome, WakeInfo\nfrom .execution import IsolatedShellBackend, run_isolated\nfrom .models import AgentSpec, AgentView, TurnRun, TurnStatus, UserConfig\nfrom .permissions import ApprovalGate\nfrom .providers import build_chat_model, resolve_profile\n\n\nclass TurnLimitExceeded(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Raised inside the graph when the per-turn model-step budget is spent.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass TeamMemberState(AgentState):\n    \"\"\"Checkpointed member state. `applied_batch` brackets the last delivery\n    batch that reached the conversation, so a restart can tell whether a batch\n    is already inside the thread.\"\"\"\n\n    applied_batch: int\n\n\nclass Inbox:\n    \"\"\"Per-run inbox: kind=\\\"{}\\\"&gt;{}&lt;/inbox&gt;\".format(\n            item[\"from\"], item[\"kind\"], json.dumps(item[\"payload\"], ensure_ascii=False)))\n    if view.permitted_shared_delta:\n '\"skills\":[],\"workspace_policy\":\"shared\"},\"channels\":[{\"source\",\"targets\":[],'\n    '\"mode\":\"message|task|broadcast\"}],\"shared_spaces\":[{\"id\",\"readers\":[],\"writers\":[]}]}; '\n    '{\"op\":\"remove_agent\",\"agent_id\"}; '\n    '{\"op\":\"update_agent\",\"agent_id\",\"changes\":{role?/instructions?/model_profile?/tool_bindings?}}; '\n    '{\"op\":\"add_channel\",\"channel\":{\"source\",\"targets\":[],\"mode\"}}; '\n    '{\"op\":\"remove_channel\",\"source\",\"targets\":[]}; '\n    '{\"op\":\"set_observer\",\"observer\":{\"agent_id\",\"subjects\":[],\"event_types\":[],'\n    '\"payload_scope\":\"status|public_message|result\",\"wake_policy\":\"none|on_event\",'\n    '\"capabilities\":[]},\"remove\":false}; '\n    '{\"op\":\"set_space_acl\",\"space_id\",\"readers\":[],\"writers\":[]}. '\n    \"Model profiles and non-builtin tool bindings must already exist in user config.\"\n)\n\n\nTEAM_TOOL_DOCS: dict[str, str] = {\n    \"send_message\": \"Send a message to a teammate you are allowed to reach. \"\n                    \"target='*' broadcasts where a broadcast channel exists.\",\n    \"assign_task\": \"Assign a task to a teammate; returns a task_id immediately \"\n                   \"and never waits for completion. Include acceptance criteria.\",\n    \"complete_task\": \"Report the current task finished with result refs and a short \"\n                     \"summary; the task becomes SUCCEEDED when your turn ends cleanly.\",\n    \"wait_for_tasks\": \"Park this turn until the given tasks finish (or the user \"\n                      \"sends new input). Releases your execution slot.\",\n    \"publish_shared\": \"Append a structured entry (finding/decision/artifact ref) to \"\n                      \"a shared space you can write to.\",\n    \"read_shared\": \"Read shared-space entries after a sequence cursor.\",\n    \"list_shared\": \"List shared spaces you can read and their entry counts.\",\n    \"request_help\": \"Ask the Leader for help with your current task.\",\n    \"propose_team_change\": \"Propose a team/topology change to the Leader; only the \"\n                           \"Leader can apply it. \" + TOPOLOGY_OPS_DOC,\n    \"apply_topology_patch\": \"Leader only: apply (or reject) a topology patch from a \"\n                            \"base revision. \" + TOPOLOGY_OPS_DOC,\n    \"signal_done\": \"Leader only: declare the current user goal complete; the runtime \"\n                   \"verifies no work, approvals or unknown outcomes are outstanding.\",\n}\n\n\ndef build_team_tools(runner: \"DeepAgentsRunner\") -&gt; list[BaseTool]:\n    \"\"\"The eleven team actions. Identity comes from the runner's *current* turn\n    (one active turn per member), so a cached graph never reuses a stale run.\"\"\"\n\n    async def call(name: str, args: dict[str, Any], tool_call_id: str) -&gt; str:\n        \"\"\"Append a structured entry to a shared space you can write to.\"\"\"\n        return await call(\"publish_shared\", {\"space_id\": space_id, \"content\": content,\n                                       \"kind\": kind, \"ref\": ref,\n                                       \"supersedes\": supersedes}, tool_call_id)\n\n    @tool\n    async def read_shared(space_id: str | None = None, after_sequence: int | None = None,\n                    limit: int = 50,\n                    tool_call_id: Annotated[str, InjectedToolCallId] = \"\") -&gt; str:\n        \"\"\"Read shared-space entries after a sequence cursor.\"\"\"\n        return await call(\"read_shared\", {\"space_id\": space_id,\n                                    \"after_sequence\": after_sequence,\n                                    \"limit\": limit}, tool_call_id)\n\n    @tool\n    async def list_shared(tool_call_id: Annotated[str, InjectedToolCallId] = \"\") -&gt; str:\n        \"\"\"List shared spaces you can read and their entry counts.\"\"\"\n        return await call(\"list_shared\", {}, tool_call_id)\n\n    @tool\n    async def request_help(message: str, task_id: str | None = None,\n                     tool_call_id: Annotated[str, InjectedToolCallId] = \"\") -&gt; Do not retry it; choose \"\n                        \"another approach or ask the user.\",\n                tool_call_id=tool_call_id, name=name, status=\"error\")\n        if status in (\"APPROVED_ONCE\", \"APPROVED_SESSION\"):\n            result = await handler(request)\n            if status == \"APPROVED_ONCE\":\n                self.approvals.consume_once(approval.approval_id)\n            return result\n        answer = interrupt({\"approval_required\": approval.requested_scope,\n                            \"approval_id\": approval.approval_id})\n        allow = bool(answer.get(\"allow\")) if isinstance(answer, dict) else bool(answer)\n        if not allow:\n            return ToolMessage(\n                content=\"The user denied this operation. Do not retry it; choose \"\n                        \"another approach or ask the user.\",\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

_switch_effort_to_max(self) -&gt; bool:\n        \"\"\"Rewrite this member's profile effort to `max` and drop the cached graph.\"\"\"\n        if self.model_override is not None:\n            return False\n        profile = self.catalog.models.get(self.agent.model_profile)\n        if profile is None:\n            return False\n        options = dict(profile.generation_options)\n        if options.get(\"reasoning_effort\") in (None, \"max\", \"max\".upper()):\n            return False\n        options[\"reasoning_effort\"] = \"max\"\n        self.catalog.models[self.agent.model_profile] = profile.model_copy(\n            update={\"generation_options\": options})\n        self._graph = None\n        self._graph_revision = None\n        return True\n\n    async def _stream_graph(self, graph, graph_input, cfg, run: TurnRun):\n        \"\"\"Run one graph segment, forwarding model text to the UI sink and\n        returning (final state, reply text). Deltas are transient by design.\"\"\"\n        state: dict | None = None\n        parts: list[str] = []\n        async for mode, payload in graph.astream(\n                graph_input, cfg, stream_mode=[\"messages\", \"values\"]):\n            if mode == \"messages\":\n                chunk, _meta = payload\n                if not isinstance(chunk, AIMessageChunk):\n                    continue\n                if getattr(chunk, \"tool_call_chunks\", None) or getattr(chunk, \"tool_calls\", None):\n                    continue\n                text = chunk.content if isinstance(chunk.content, str) else \"\"\n                if text:\n                    parts.append(text)\n                    if self.stream_hook is not None:\n                        self.stream_hook(run.run_id, self.agent.id, text)\n            elif mode == \"values\":\n                state = payload\n        reply = \"\".join(parts).strip()\n        return state or {}, reply or None\n\n    async def request_interrupt(self, run_id: str) -&gt; TurnStatus:\n        \"\"\"Cancellation reports a confirmed stop at the next model boundary;\n        side effects already produced are never rolled back (plan section 9.3).\"\"\"\n        self._states[run_id] = TurnStatus.CANCELLED\n        return TurnStatus.CANCELLED\n\n    def query_state(self, run_id: str) -&gt; TurnStatus | None:\n        return self._states.get(run_id)\n\n    def deliver_mid_turn(self, run_id: str, items: list[dict[str, Any]]) -&gt; None:\n        inbox = self._inboxes.setdefault(run_id, Inbox())\n        inbox.push(items, inbox.batch)\n\n    def bound_tool_names(self) -&gt; set[str]:\n        \"\"\"Tools the user bound to this member (their binding is the authorization).\"\"\"\n        return {t.name for t in [*(self._bound_tools or []), *self.extra_tools]}\n\n    async def reconcile(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; TurnStatus | None:\n        \"\"\"After a restart: believe this member's own checkpoint, not the DB row.\"\"\"\n        if self._graph is None:\n            return None\n        thread_id = self._threads.get(run.run_id) or self._thread_id(run)\n        cfg = {\"configurable\": {\"thread_id\": thread_id}}\n        try:\n            state = await self._graph.aget_state(cfg)\n        except Exception:\n            return None\n        if state is None or not getattr(state, \"values\", None):\n            return None\n        applied = int(state.values.get(\"applied_batch\") or 0)\n        if applied:\n            self.approvals.store.ack_deliveries(self.session_id, self.agent.id, applied)\n        if state.next:\n            kind = \"approval\"\n            for task in getattr(state, \"tasks\", ()) or ():\n                for intr in getattr(task, \"interrupts\", ()) or ():\n                    value = getattr(intr, \"value\", {}) or {}\n                    kind = \"approval\" if \"approval_required\" in value else \"waiting\"\n            self._paused_kind[run.run_id] = kind\n            return (TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL if kind == \"approval\"\n                    else TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK)\n        return TurnStatus.COMPLETED\ndef _looks_like_effort_error(error: Exception) -&gt; bool:\n    text = str(error).lower()\n    return any(token in text for token in\n               (\"reasoning_effort\", \"reasoning effort\", \"effort\", \"unsupported value\"))\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

str | None:\nsrc/teamagents/providers.py:61:def build_chat_model(profile: ModelProfile,\n====\n\"\"\"Model profiles -&gt; chat model instances (plan section 11).\n\nProfiles are logical names; provider, protocol, address and key reference live\nin user config. Provider-specific fields needed for tool-call continuation are\npreserved by the vendor integrations; nothing here can change team permissions.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom langchain_core.language_models.chat_models import BaseChatModel\n\nfrom .models import ModelProfile, UserConfig\n\n\nclass ModelConfigError(RuntimeError):\n    pass\n\n\n#: protocols whose models do not advertise an `xhigh` reasoning level\nNO_XHIGH_PROTOCOLS = {\"deepseek\"}\n\n\ndef normalize_effort(profile: ModelProfile) -&gt; tuple[dict[str, Any], str | None]:\n    \"\"\"Map a requested reasoning effort onto what this provider can accept.\n\n    Rule (user decision 2026-09-12): when a model does not support `xhigh`,\n    the effort maps to `max` instead of being dropped or erroring out.\n    \"\"\"\n    options = dict(profile.generation_options)\n    effort = options.get(\"reasoning_effort\")\n    if isinstance(effort, str) and effort.lower() == \"xhigh\" and \\\n            profile.protocol in NO_XHIGH_PROTOCOLS:\n        options[\"reasoning_effort\"] = \"max\"\n        return options, f\"reasoning_effort xhigh -&gt; max ({profile.protocol})\"\n    return options, None\n\n\ndef resolve_profile(catalog: UserConfig, name: str) -&gt; ModelProfile:\n    if name not in catalog.models:\n        raise ModelConfigError(\n            f\"unknown model profile {name!r}; configure it under [models.{name}] \"\n            \"in ~/.config/teamagents/config.toml (copy examples/config.toml from the \"\n            \"repository as a starting point)\")\n    return catalog.models[name]\n\n\ndef _api_key(profile: ModelProfile) -&gt; str | None:\n    if profile.api_key_env:\n        key = os.environ.get(profile.api_key_env)\n        if not key:\n            raise ModelConfigError(\n                f\"model profile {profile.provider!r} needs environment variable \"\n                f\"{profile.api_key_env}; export it (secrets never enter TeamSpec)\")\n        return key\n    return None\n\n\ndef build_chat_model(profile: ModelProfile,\n                     overrides: dict[str, Any] | None = None) -&gt; BaseChatModel:\n    \"\"\"Build the chat model for one profile. Raises ModelConfigError on gaps.\"\"\"\n    normalized, _note = normalize_effort(profile)\n    options = {**normalized, **(overrides or {})}\n    common: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"model\": profile.model,\n        \"timeout\": profile.timeout,\n        \"max_retries\": profile.max_retries,\n        **options,\n    }\n    key = _api_key(profile)\n    if key is not None:\n        common[\"api_key\"] = key\n    if profile.base_url:\n        common[\"base_url\"] = profile.base_url\n    match profile.protocol:\n        case \"anthropic\":\n            from langchain_anthropic import ChatAnthropic\n            return ChatAnthropic(**common)\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "tests/test_p3_web_tools.py:25:                \"http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/\", \"file:///etc/passwd\",\n===\n\"\"\"Workspace policies: shared / isolated / git worktree (plan section 12.3).\n\nA worktree isolates working files; it is not a security sandbox. Uncommitted\ntask inputs in the original directory are never ignored silently: the policy\nfalls back to shared mode and says why. Directories with unmerged results,\nunresolved conflicts or local modifications are never auto-deleted.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport dataclasses\nimport subprocess\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom .models import AgentSpec, WorkspacePolicy\n\n\n@dataclasses.dataclass\nclass Workspace:\n    path: Path\n    policy: WorkspacePolicy\n    note: str | None = None\n    branch: str | None = None\n    base_commit: str | None = None\n\n\nclass WorkspaceError(RuntimeError):\n    pass\n\n\ndef _git(cwd: Path, *args: str, timeout: int = 30) -&gt; subprocess.CompletedProcess:\n    return subprocess.run([\"git\", \"-C\", str(cwd), *args], capture_output=True,\n                          text=True, timeout=timeout)\n\n\ndef is_git_repo(cwd: Path) -&gt; bool:\n    return _git(cwd, \"rev-parse\", \"--git-dir\").returncode == 0\n\n\ndef is_dirty(cwd: Path) -&gt; bool:\n    result = _git(cwd, \"status\", \"--porcelain\")\n    return bool(result.stdout.strip())\n\n\ndef head_commit(cwd: Path) -&gt; str | None:\n    result = _git(cwd, \"rev-parse\", \"HEAD\")\n    return result.stdout.strip() if result.returncode == 0 else None\n\n\ndef prepare(agent: AgentSpec, project_cwd: Path, member_dir: Path) -&gt; Workspace:\n    \"\"\"Resolve where this member works, applying the configured policy.\"\"\"\n    policy = agent.workspace_policy\n    if policy is WorkspacePolicy.SHARED:\n        return Workspace(path=project_cwd, policy=policy)\n    if policy is WorkspacePolicy.ISOLATED:\n        path = member_dir / \"work\"\n        path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        (path / \"INPUTS.md\").touch(exist_ok=True)\n        return Workspace(path=path, policy=policy,\n                         note=\"isolated directory: copy inputs explicitly, \"\n                              \"deliver results through artifact refs\")\n    if not is_git_repo(project_cwd):\n        return Workspace(path=project_cwd, policy=WorkspacePolicy.SHARED,\n                         note=\"git_worktree requested but the directory is not a git \"\n                              \"repository; using shared mode\")\n    if is_dirty(project_cwd):\n        return Workspace(path=project_cwd, policy=WorkspacePolicy.SHARED,\n                         note=\"git_worktree requested but the project has uncommitted \"\n                              \"changes; using shared mode so those inputs are not ignored\")\n    branch = f\"teamagents/{agent.id}-{int(time.time())}\"\n    base = head_commit(project_cwd)\n    path = member_dir / \"work\"\n    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    result = _git(project_cwd, \"worktree\", \"add\", \"-b\", branch, str(path), base or \"HEAD\",\n                  timeout=120)\n    if result.returncode != 0:\n        raise WorkspaceError(f\"git worktree add failed: {result.stderr.strip()}\")\n    return Workspace(path=path, policy=policy, branch=branch, base_commit=base)\n\n\ndef cleanup(workspace: Workspace, project_cwd: Path, *, force: bool = False) -&gt; tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"Remove an isolated/worktree directory only when nothing would be lost.\"\"\"\n    if workspace.policy is WorkspacePolicy.SHARED:\n        return False, \"shared workspace is never removed\"\n    if workspace.policy is WorkspacePolicy.GIT_WORKTREE:\n        if not force:\n            if is_dirty(workspace.path):\n                return False, (\"worktree has uncommitted or unmerged changes; \"\n                               \"review and merge them first\")\n            porcelain = _git(workspace.path, \"status\", \"--porcelain\").stdout\n            if any(line[:2] in (\"UU\", \"AA\", \"DD\", \"AU\", \"UA\", \"DU\", \"UD\")\n                   for line in porcelain.splitlines()):\n                return False, \"unresolved merge conflicts in the worktree\"\n            if workspace.branch:\n                unmerged = _git(project_cwd, \"branch\", \"--no-merged\", \"HEAD\",\n                                \"--list\", workspace.branch).stdout\n                if workspace.branch in unmerged:\n                    return False, (\"worktree results are unmerged (committed but not \"\n                                   \"merged); merge them before cleanup\")\n        args = [\"worktree\", \"remove\"]\n        if force:\n            args.append(\"--force\")\n        args.append(str(workspace.path))\n        result = _git(project_cwd, *args, timeout=120)\n        if result.returncode != 0:\n            return False, result.stderr.strip()\n        if workspace.branch:\n            _git(project_cwd, \"branch\", \"-D\" if force else \"-d\", workspace.branch)\n        return True, \"worktree removed\"\n    if workspace.policy is WorkspacePolicy.ISOLATED:\n        if any(workspace.path.iterdir()) and not force:\n            return False, \"isolated directory still holds results; archive them first\"\n        for child in sorted(workspace.path.iterdir(), reverse=True):\n            if child.is_dir():\n                for sub in sorted(child.rglob(\"*\"), reverse=True):\n                    sub.rmdir() if sub.is_dir() else sub.unlink()\n                child.rmdir()\n            else:\n                child.unlink()\n        workspace.path.rmdir()\n        return True, \"isolated directory removed\"\n    return False, \"unknown workspace policy\"\n\n\ndef merge_branch(project_cwd: Path, branch: str,\n                 message: str | None = None) -&gt; tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"Leader-side merge helper: merge a member branch, report conflicts.\"\"\"\n    result = _git(project_cwd, \"merge\", \"--no-ff\", branch,\n                  \"-m\", message or f\"merge {branch}\", timeout=120)\n    if result.returncode == 0:\n        return True, result.stdout.strip()\n    if \"CONFLICT\" in result.stdout or \"CONFLICT\" in result.stderr:\n        return False, \"merge conflicts: \" + result.stdout.strip()\n    return False, result.stderr.strip()\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"Linux execution backend: bubblewrap-isolated shell + guarded file backend.\n\nMounts and network flags are constructed here and unit-tested (T23); the\nisolation facility being unavailable must never silently degrade to full\naccess (plan section 12.2).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport dataclasses\nimport os\nimport signal\nimport subprocess\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any, Sequence\n\nfrom deepagents.backends import FilesystemBackend\nfrom deepagents.backends.protocol import ExecuteResponse\n\n#: read-only system paths mounted into every isolated command when present\nSYSTEM_RO = [\"/usr\", \"/etc\", \"/opt\"]\nSYSTEM_LINKS = [(\"usr/lib\", \"/lib\"), (\"usr/lib64\", \"/lib64\"),\n                (\"usr/bin\", \"/bin\"), (\"usr/bin\", \"/sbin\")]\n\n\nclass IsolationUnavailable(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when bwrap cannot be used; callers must ask for explicit approval.\"\"\"\n\n\n@dataclasses.dataclass\nclass ExecResult:\n    output: str\n    exit_code: int | None\n    truncated: bool = False\n    artifact: str | None = None\n    timed_out: bool = False\n\n\ndef bwrap_available() -&gt; bool:\n    return _which(\"bwrap\") is not None\n\n\ndef _which(name: str) -&gt; str | None:\n    for p in os.environ.get(\"PATH\", \"/usr/bin:/bin\").split(\":\"):\n        cand = Path(p) / name\n        if cand.exists() and os.access(cand, os.X_OK):\n            return str(cand)\n    return None\n\n\ndef _sanitized_env(workdir: Path, extra: dict[str, str] | None) -&gt; dict[str, str]:\n    \"\"\"Whitelist environment: no model keys, no credentials (plan section 12.2).\"\"\"\n    env = {\n        \"PATH\": \"/usr/local/bin:/usr/bin:/bin\",\n        \"HOME\": str(workdir),\n        \"LANG\": os.environ.get(\"LANG\", \"C.UTF-8\"),\n        \"LC_ALL\": os.environ.get(\"LC_ALL\", \"\"),\n        \"TERM\": \"dumb\",\n        \"TMPDIR\": \"/tmp\",\n        \"PYTHONIOENCODING\": \"utf-8\",\n    }\n    for key, value in (extra or {}).items():\n        env[key] = value\n    return {k: v for k, v in env.items() if v != \"\"}\n\n\ndef bwrap_argv(workdir: Path, *, network: bool = False,\n               extra_rw: Sequence[Path] = (), extra_ro: Sequence[Path] = (),\n               command: str = \"true\") -&gt; list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Construct the isolation command line (system files ro, workdir rw, no net).\"\"\"\n    argv = [\"bwrap\"]\n    for path in SYSTEM_RO:\n        if Path(path).exists():\n            argv += [\"--ro-bind\", path, path]\n    for target, link in SYSTEM_LINKS:\n        # always create the link inside the new root (the host's /bin is itself a\n        # symlink, so host existence checks are meaningless here)\n        if Path(\"/\" + target).exists():\n            argv += [\"--symlink\", target, link]\n    argv += [\"--proc\", \"/proc\", \"--dev\", \"/dev\", \"--tmpfs\", \"/tmp\"]\n    argv += [\"--bind\", str(workdir), str(workdir)]\n    for path in extra_rw:\n        argv += [\"--bind\", str(path), str(path)]\n    for path in extra_ro:\n        argv += [\"--ro-bind\", str(path), str(path)]\n    argv += [\"--chdir\", str(workdir)]\n    argv += [\"--unshare-pid\", \"--unshare-ipc\", \"--unshare-uts\", \"--die-with-parent\",\n             \"--new-session\"]\n    if not network:\n        argv += [\"--unshare-net\"]\n    argv += [\"--\", \"/bin/bash\", \"-lc\", command]\n    return argv\n\n\ndef run_isolated(command: str, *, workdir: Path, timeout: int = 120,\n                 network: bool = False, extra_rw: Sequence[Path] = (),\n                 extra_ro: Sequence[Path] = (), env: dict[str, str] | None = None,\n                 max_output_bytes: int = 100_000,\n                 artifact_dir: Path | None = None) -&gt; ExecResult:\n    \"\"\"Run one command inside the sandbox. Process-group kill on timeout.\"\"\"\n    if not bwrap_available():\n        raise IsolationUnavailable(\n            \"bwrap is not available: refusing to run commands without isolation\")\n    workdir = Path(workdir).resolve()\n    argv = bwrap_argv(workdir, network=network, extra_rw=extra_rw, extra_ro=extra_ro,\n                      command=command)\n    proc = subprocess.Popen(\n        argv,\n        stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,\n        stdout=subprocess.PIPE,\n        stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,\n        env=_sanitized_env(workdir, env),\n        start_new_session=True,\n        text=False,\n    )\n    timed_out = False\n    try:\n        out_bytes, _ = proc.communicate(timeout=timeout)\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n...\n27:class IsolationUnavailable(RuntimeError):\n32:class ExecResult:\n40:def bwrap_available() -&gt; bool:\n44:def _which(name: str) -&gt; str | None:\n52:def _sanitized_env(workdir: Path, extra: dict[str, str] | None) -&gt; dict[str, str]:\n68:def bwrap_argv(workdir: Path, *, network: bool = False,\n96:def run_isolated(command: str, *, workdir: Path, timeout: int = 120,\n137:def _kill_group(proc: subprocess.Popen) -&gt; None:\n152:def _save_artifact(output: str, artifact_dir: Path | None) -&gt; str | None:\n167:class GuardedFilesystemBackend(FilesystemBackend):\n175:    def __init__(self, root_dir: str | Path, max_file_size_mb: int = 10):\n180:    def _resolve_path(self, key: str) -&gt; Path:\n201:class IsolatedShellBackend(GuardedFilesystemBackend):\n208:    def __init__(self, root_dir: str | Path, *, artifacts_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n225:    def execute(self, command: str, *, timeout: int | None = None) -&gt; ExecuteResponse:\n240:    async def aexecute(self, command: str, *, timeout: int | None = None) -&gt; ExecuteResponse:\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
