<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n        timed_out = True\n        _kill_group(proc)\n        out_bytes, _ = proc.communicate()\n    output = out_bytes.decode(\"utf-8\", errors=\"replace\")\n    truncated = len(out_bytes) &gt; max_output_bytes\n    artifact = None\n    if truncated:\n        artifact = _save_artifact(output, artifact_dir)\n        output = output[:max_output_bytes] + (\n            f\"\\n[output truncated at {max_output_bytes} bytes; full output: {artifact}]\")\n    if timed_out:\n        output += f\"\\n[command killed after {timeout}s timeout]\"\n    return ExecResult(output=output, exit_code=proc.returncode, truncated=truncated,\n                      artifact=artifact, timed_out=timed_out)\n\n\ndef _kill_group(proc: subprocess.Popen) -&gt; None:\n    \"\"\"Kill the whole process group so no children survive (plan section 9.3).\"\"\"\n    try:\n        os.killpg(os.getpgid(proc.pid), signal.SIGTERM)\n    except ProcessLookupError:\n        return\n    try:\n        proc.wait(timeout=5)\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n        try:\n            os.killpg(os.getpgid(proc.pid), signal.SIGKILL)\n        except ProcessLookupError:\n            pass\n\n\ndef _save_artifact(output: str, artifact_dir: Path | None) -&gt; str | None:\n    if artifact_dir is None:\n        return None\n    artifact_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    name = f\"exec-{time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}-{os.getpid()}.log\"\n    path = artifact_dir / name\n    path.write_text(output, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return f\"/artifacts/{name}\"\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Deep Agents backends\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass GuardedFilesystemBackend(FilesystemBackend):\n    \"\"\"FilesystemBackend with symlink-aware containment under its root.\n\n    String prefix checks are not acceptable (plan section 12.2): every write\n    resolves the real path (including symlinked parents) and refuses escapes.\n    Reads of symlinks pointing outside the root are refused too.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, root_dir: str | Path, max_file_size_mb: int = 10):\n        super().__init__(root_dir=root_dir, virtual_mode=True,\n                         max_file_size_mb=max_file_size_mb)\n        self._root_real = Path(root_dir).resolve()\n\n    def _resolve_path(self, key: str) -&gt; Path:\n        \"\"\"One guard for every file operation: real path (symlinks included) must\n        stay under the authorized root.\"\"\"\n        resolved = super()._resolve_path(key)\n        root = self._root_real\n        probe, tail = resolved, []\n        while not probe.exists() and probe != probe.parent:\n            tail.append(probe.name)\n            probe = probe.parent\n        real = probe.resolve()\n        if tail:\n            real = real.joinpath(*reversed(tail))\n        try:\n            real.relative_to(root)\n        except ValueError as e:\n            raise PermissionError(\n                f\"path {key!r} resolves outside the authorized directory \"\n                f\"({real} is not under {root})\") from e\n        return resolved\n\n\nclass IsolatedShellBackend(GuardedFilesystemBackend):\n    \"\"\"Filesystem backend whose `execute` runs inside bubblewrap (plan §12.2).\n\n    Network is withheld unless the caller explicitly allowed it; commands that\n    cannot be isolated raise instead of being silently run unsandboxed.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, root_dir: str | Path, *, artifacts_dir: str | Path | None = None,\n                 extra_rw: Sequence[Path] = (), extra_ro: Sequence[Path] = (),\n                 network: bool = False, timeout: int = 120,\n                 max_output_bytes: int = 100_000,\n                 env: dict[str, str] | None = None):\n        super().__init__(root_dir)\n        self.id = f\"bwrap:{Path(root_dir).name}\"\n        self.artifacts_dir = Path(artifacts_dir) if artifacts_dir else None\n        self.extra_rw = list(extra_rw)\n        self.extra_ro = [Path(p) for p in extra_ro]\n        self.network = network\n        self.timeout = timeout\n        self.max_output_bytes = max_output_bytes\n        self.env = env or {}\n\n    # -- SandboxBackendProtocol ---------------------------------------------\n\n    def execute(self, command: str, *, timeout: int | None = None) -&gt; ExecuteResponse:\n        result = run_isolated(\n            command,\n            workdir=Path(self.cwd),\n            timeout=timeout or self.timeout,\n            network=self.network,\n            extra_rw=self.extra_rw,\n            extra_ro=self.extra_ro,\n            env=self.env,\n            max_output_bytes=self.max_output_bytes,\n            artifact_dir=self.artifacts_dir,\n        )\n        return ExecuteResponse(output=result.output, exit_code=result.exit_code,\n                               truncated=result.truncated)\n\n    async def aexecute(self, command: str, *, timeout: int | None = None) -&gt; ExecuteResponse:\n        import asyncio\n        return await asyncio.to_thread(self.execute, command, timeout=timeout)\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "     1\t\"\"\"Tool permission modes and approvals (plan section 12.2).\n     2\t\n     3\t`approved_scope`: pre-authorized operations run; anything else pauses for a\n     4\tonce/session/deny decision bound to the exact operation and its parameters.\n     5\t`full_auto`: user-opened mode, skips per-call approvals, keeps team ACLs.\n     6\t\n     7\tPath checking (symlink/traversal) and the bubblewrap scope land in P3; this\n     8\tmodule already owns the decision flow so both runners share one gate.\n     9\t\"\"\"\n    10\t\n    11\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n    12\t\n    13\timport hashlib\n    14\timport json\n    15\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n    16\tfrom typing import Any\n    17\t\n    18\tfrom .models import ApprovalRequest, ApprovalStatus, PermissionMode, new_id\n    19\tfrom .storage import Store\n    20\t\n    21\t\n    22\t@dataclass\n    23\tclass Decision:\n    24\t    allow: bool\n    25\t    scope: dict[str, Any] | None = None\n    26\t    reason: str = \"\"\n    27\t\n    28\t\n    29\tdef operation_hash(tool_name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -&gt; str:\n    30\t    \"\"\"Approval is bound to the operation and its parameters (section 12.2).\"\"\"\n    31\t    blob = json.dumps({\"tool\": tool_name, \"args\": args}, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)\n    32\t    return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode()).hexdigest()[:32]\n    33\t\n    34\t\n    35\tclass PermissionPolicy:\n    36\t    \"\"\"Evaluates one tool call. Overridden/configured per deployment.\"\"\"\n    37\t\n    38\t    def __init__(self, mode: PermissionMode = PermissionMode.APPROVED_SCOPE,\n    39\t                 pre_authorized: set[str] | None = None,\n    40\t                 require_approval: set[str] | None = None,\n    41\t                 write_paths: list[Any] | None = None):\n    42\t        self.mode = mode\n    43\t        #: tools that may run without asking inside the approved scope\n    44\t        self.pre_authorized = pre_authorized if pre_authorized is not None else {\"files\", \"shell\"}\n    45\t        #: tools that always ask in approved_scope (e.g. network egress)\n    46\t        self.require_approval = require_approval or set()\n    47\t        #: directories the user pre-authorized for file writes (real paths)\n    48\t        self.write_paths = [str(p) for p in (write_paths or [])]\n    49\t\n    50\t    def evaluate(self, tool_name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -&gt; Decision:\n    51\t        \"\"\"Decide one call. Unknown tools ask; approved scope is allow-by-default\n    52\t        only for tools the runtime actually bound to the member.\"\"\"\n    53\t        if self.mode is PermissionMode.FULL_AUTO:\n    54\t            return Decision(allow=True)\n    55\t        if tool_name == \"shell\" and args.get(\"network\"):\n    56\t            return Decision(allow=False,\n    57\t                            scope={\"tool\": tool_name, \"args\": args,\n    58\t                                   \"reason\": \"shell network access is off by default\"},\n    59\t                            reason=\"shell network access requires approval\")\n    60\t        if tool_name in self.require_approval:\n    61\t            return Decision(allow=False,\n    62\t                            scope={\"tool\": tool_name, \"args\": args,\n    63\t                                   \"reason\": f\"{tool_name} needs approval\"},\n    64\t                            reason=\"outside pre-authorized scope\")\n    65\t        if tool_name in self.pre_authorized or self._bound_tool(tool_name):\n    66\t            return Decision(allow=True)\n    67\t        if tool_name.startswith((\"mcp_\", \"web_\")):\n    68\t            return Decision(allow=True)\n    69\t        return Decision(allow=False,\n    70\t                        scope={\"tool\": tool_name, \"args\": args,\n    71\t                               \"reason\": f\"{tool_name} is not pre-authorized\"},\n    72\t                        reason=\"tool not in approved scope\")\n    73\t\n    74\t    @staticmethod\n    75\t    def _bound_tool(tool_name: str) -&gt; bool:\n    76\t        \"\"\"Runtime-bound execution tools: file tools stay inside the sandboxed\n    77\t        backend and MCP/web tools exist only when the user configured them.\"\"\"\n    78\t        file_tools = {\"ls\", \"read_file\", \"write_file\", \"edit_file\", \"delete\",\n    79\t                      \"glob\", \"grep\", \"read_artifact\"}\n    80\t        return tool_name in file_tools\n    81\t\n    82\t\n    83\tclass ApprovalGate:\n    84\t    \"\"\"Creates approval requests and resolves them once/session/deny.\"\"\"\n    85\t\n    86\t    def __init__(self, store: Store, session_id: str, policy: PermissionPolicy):\n    87\t        self.store = store\n    88\t        self.session_id = session_id\n    89\t        self.policy = policy\n    90\t        self.policy_revision = 1\n    91\t\n    92\t    def set_mode(self, mode: PermissionMode) -&gt; None:\n    93\t        self.policy.mode = mode\n    94\t        self.policy_revision += 1\n    95\t\n    96\t    def check(self, agent_id: str, run_id: str, tool_name: str, args: dict[str, Any],\n    97\t              tool_call_id: str) -&gt; tuple[Decision, ApprovalRequest | None]:\n    98\t        \"\"\"Returns (decision, approval_request). An open request means: pause.\"\"\"\n    99\t        decision = self.policy.evaluate(tool_name, args)\n   100\t        if decision.allow:\n   101\t            return decision, None\n   102\t        op_hash = operation_hash(tool_name, args)\n   103\t        if self.store.find_session_approval(self.session_id, op_hash) is not None:\n   104\t            return Decision(allow=True), None\n   105\t        existing = self.store.approval_for_call(run_id, tool_call_id, op_hash)\n   106\t        if existing is not None:\n   107\t            match existing.status:\n   108\t                case ApprovalStatus.PENDING:\n   109\t                    return decision, existing\n   110\t                case ApprovalStatus.DENIED:\n   111\t                    return Decision(allow=False, reason=\"denied by the user\"), existing\n   112\t                case ApprovalStatus.APPROVED_ONCE | ApprovalStatus.APPROVED_SESSION:\n   113\t                    if existing.policy_revision == self.policy_revision:\n   114\t                        return Decision(allow=True), existing\n   115\t        req = ApprovalRequest(\n   116\t            approval_id=new_id(\"appr\"),\n   117\t            session_id=self.session_id,\n   118\t            agent_id=agent_id,\n   119\t            run_id=run_id,\n   120\t            tool_call_id=tool_call_id,\n   121\t            operation_hash=op_hash,\n   122\t            requested_scope=decision.scope or {},\n   123\t            policy_revision=self.policy_revision,\n   124\t        )\n   125\t        self.store.insert_approval(req)\n   126\t        return decision, req\n   127\t\n   128\t    def recheck(self, approval_id: str, tool_name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -&gt; bool:\n   129\t        \"\"\"On resume: re-verify parameters and the recorded decision (section 12.2).\"\"\"\n   130\t        req = self.store.get_approval(approval_id)\n   131\t        if req is None:\n   132\t            return False\n   133\t        if req.operation_hash != operation_hash(tool_name, args):\n   134\t            return False\n   135\t        if req.status is ApprovalStatus.APPROVED_SESSION:\n   136\t            return True\n   137\t        return (req.status is ApprovalStatus.APPROVED_ONCE\n   138\t                and req.policy_revision == self.policy_revision)\n   139\t\n   140\t    def consume_once(self, approval_id: str) -&gt; None:\n   141\t        self.store.expire_approval(approval_id)\n   142\t\n   143\t    def register_external(self, *, agent_id: str, run_id: str, tool_call_id: str,\n   144\t                          scope: dict[str, Any]) -&gt; ApprovalRequest:\n   145\t        \"\"\"Record an approval request raised by an external backend (Codex), where\n   146\t        the operation's own service defines the scope and our policy is not the\n   147\t        gate; the user decision still flows through the same approval queue.\"\"\"\n   148\t        op_hash = operation_hash(scope.get(\"kind\", \"external\"), scope.get(\"request\", {}))\n   149\t        req = ApprovalRequest(\n   150\t            approval_id=new_id(\"appr\"), session_id=self.session_id, agent_id=agent_id,\n   151\t            run_id=run_id, tool_call_id=tool_call_id, operation_hash=op_hash,\n   152\t            requested_scope=scope, policy_revision=self.policy_revision,\n   153\t        )\n   154\t        self.store.insert_approval(req)\n   155\t        return req\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

subprocess.run([\"git\", \"config\", \"user.email\", \"demo@example.com\"], cwd=project,\n    32\t                   check=True)\n    33\t    subprocess.run([\"git\", \"config\", \"user.name\", runtime_kind=RuntimeKind.DEEPAGENTS,\n    57\t                      instructions=\"协调团队：验证成果后合并成员分支，并用 signal_done 交付。\",\n    58\t                      model_profile=\"leader_main\",\n    59\t                      tool_bindings=[\"files\", \"shell\"]),\n    60\t            AgentSpec(id=\"coder\", name=\"Coder\", role=\"worker\",\n    61\t                      runtime_kind=RuntimeKind.DEEPAGENTS,\n <message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"CLI entry points: run/resume, doctor, validate (plan section 13).\n\n    teamagents                     start the TUI (P6; thin REPL until then)\n    teamagents --cwd PATH          start in a working directory\n    teamagents --resume SESSION    resume a session\n    teamagents --full-auto         explicit user choice of full-auto mode\n    teamagents doctor              check models, tools, isolation, Codex protocol\n    teamagents validate TEAM_SPEC  validate an imported team definition\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport asyncio\nimport importlib.metadata as md\nimport json\nimport os\nimport shutil\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom . import __version__\nfrom .config import load_team_spec, load_user_config, sessions_dir\n\n\ndef version_report() -&gt; dict[str, str]:\n    pkgs = [\"deepagents\", \"langgraph\", \"langgraph-checkpoint-sqlite\", \"langchain\",\n            \"langchain-openai\", \"langchain-anthropic\", \"langchain-deepseek\",\n            \"langchain-mcp-adapters\", \"pydantic\", \"textual\"]\n    out = {\"python\": sys.version.split()[0], \"teamagents\": __version__}\n    for p in pkgs:\n        try:\n            out[p] = md.version(p)\n        except md.PackageNotFoundError:\n            out[p] = \"missing\"\n    return out\n\n\ndef _check(name: str, ok: bool, detail: str = \"\") -&gt; tuple[str, bool, str]:\n    return name, ok, detail\n\n\ndef doctor() -&gt; int:\n    \"\"\"Check model config, tools, isolation and Codex protocol capability.\"\"\"\n    results: list[tuple[str, bool, str]] = []\n    versions = version_report()\n    missing = [k for k, v in versions.items() if v == \"missing\"]\n    results.append(_check(\"dependencies\", not missing,\n                          json.dumps(versions, ensure_ascii=False)\n                          if not missing else f\"missing: {missing}\"))\n\n    try:\n        catalog = load_user_config()\n        results.append(_check(\"user config\", True,\n                              f\"models={sorted(catalog.models)} tools={sorted(catalog.tools)}\"))\n        for name, profile in catalog.models.items():\n            key_env = profile.api_key_env\n            if key_env:\n                present = bool(os.environ.get(key_env))\n                results.append(_check(f\"model profile {name}\", present,\n                                      f\"{profile.provider}/{profile.model}\"\n                                      + (\"\" if present else f\" (missing env {key_env})\")))\n    except Exception as e:\n        results.append(_check(\"user config\", False, str(e)))\n\n    bwrap = shutil.which(\"bwrap\")\n    if bwrap:\n        probe = subprocess.run(\n            [\"bwrap\", \"--ro-bind\", \"/usr\", \"/usr\", \"--ro-bind\", \"/etc\", \"/etc\",\n             \"--symlink\", \"usr/lib\", \"/lib\",\n             \"--symlink\", \"usr/lib64\", \"/lib64\", \"--symlink\", \"usr/bin\", \"/bin\",\n             \"--proc\", \"/proc\", \"--dev\", \"/dev\", \"--tmpfs\", \"/tmp\",\n             \"--unshare-pid\", \"--unshare-net\", \"--die-with-parent\",\n             \"--\", \"sh\", \"-c\", \"test -e /etc/hostname\"],\n            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n        blocked_home = subprocess.run(\n            [\"bwrap\", \"--ro-bind\", \"/usr\", \"/usr\", \"--ro-bind\", \"/etc\", \"/etc\",\n             \"--symlink\", \"usr/lib\", \"/lib\",\n             \"--symlink\", \"usr/lib64\", \"/lib64\", \"--symlink\", \"usr/bin\", \"/bin\",\n             \"--proc\", \"/proc\", \"--dev\", \"/dev\", \"--tmpfs\", \"/tmp\",\n             \"--unshare-pid\", \"--unshare-net\", \"--die-with-parent\",\n             \"--\", \"sh\", \"-c\", \"test ! -e /home\"],\n            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)\n        results.append(_check(\"bubblewrap isolation\", probe.returncode == 0\n                              and blocked_home.returncode == 0,\n                              \"system files visible, home blocked under bwrap\"))\n    else:\n        results.append(_check(\"bubblewrap isolation\", False,\n                              \"bwrap not found: out-of-scope commands must ask for approval\"))\n\n    codex = shutil.which(\"codex\")\n    if codex:\n        try:\n            ver = subprocess.run([codex, \"app-server\", \"--help\"], capture_output=True,\n                                 text=True, timeout=20)\n            schema_ok, schema_detail = _codex_schema_check(codex)\n            results.append(_check(\"codex app-server\", \"app-server\" in ver.stdout,\n                                  f\"codex-cli {_codex_version(codex)}\"))\n            results.append(_check(\"codex protocol schema\", schema_ok, schema_detail))\n        except Exception as e:\n            results.append(_check(\"codex app-server\", False, str(e)))\n    else:\n        results.append(_check(\"codex app-server\", False, \"codex CLI not found\"))\n\n    state = sessions_dir()\n    try:\n        state.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        probe_file = state / \".doctor-probe\"\n        probe_file.write_text(\"ok\")\n        probe_file.unlink()\n        results.append(_check(\"state directory\", True, str(state)))\n    except Exception as e:\n        results.append(_check(\"state directory\", False, f\"{state}: {e}\"))\n\n    print(f\"TeamAgents doctor (v{__version__})\")\n    failed = 0\n    for name, ok, detail in results:\n        mark = \"ok  \" if ok else \"FAIL\"\n        if not ok:\n            failed += 1\n        print(f\"  [{mark}] {name:&lt;28} {detail}\")\n    return 1 if failed else 0\n\n\ndef _codex_version(codex: str) -&gt; str:\n    try:\n        out = subprocess.run([codex, \"--version\"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)\n        return out.stdout.strip() or out.stderr.strip()\n    except Exception:\n        return \"unknown\"\n\n\ndef _codex_schema_check(codex: str, out_dir: Path | None = None) -&gt; tuple[bool, str]:\n    \"\"\"Generate the schema from the running CLI and confirm required methods exist.\"\"\"\n    import tempfile\n    needed = {\"initialize\", \"thread/start\", \"thread/resume\", \"turn/start\", \"turn/interrupt\"}\n    needed_requests = {\"item/commandExecution/requestApproval\",\n                       \"item/fileChange/requestApproval\", \"item/tool/requestUserInput\"}\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

int:\n    try:\n        from .control import BUILTIN_TOOL_BINDINGS\n\n        spec = load_team_spec(path)\n        catalog = load_user_config()\n        unknown_models = [a.model_profile for a in spec.agents\n                          if a.model_profile not in catalog.models]\n        unknown_tools = [t for a in spec.agents for t in a.tool_bindings\n                         if t not in catalog.tools and t not in BUILTIN_TOOL_BINDINGS]\n        if unknown_models or unknown_tools:\n            print(f\"invalid: unknown model profiles {unknown_models}, \"\n                  f\"unknown tool bindings {unknown_tools}\")\n            return 1\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"invalid: {e}\")\n        return 1\n    print(f\"ok: {path} — leader={spec.leader_id} members={len(spec.agents)} \"\n          f\"channels={len(spec.channels)} spaces={len(spec.shared_spaces)}\")\n    return 0\n\n\ndef list_sessions(verbose: bool = False) -&gt; int:\n    \"\"\"`teamagents sessions`: what records exist, where, and their size.\"\"\"\n    from .config import sessions_dir\n    from .sessions import list_sessions as inventory\n\n    rows = inventory(include_archived=True)\n    if not rows:\n        print(f\"没有会话记录（{sessions_dir()}）\")\n        return 0\n    print(f\"会话记录目录：{sessions_dir()}\")\n    for r in rows:\n        updated = time.strftime(\"%m-%d %H:%M\", time.localtime(r.updated_at)) \\\n            if r.updated_at else \"?\"\n        flags = []\n        if r.archived:\n            flags.append(\"已归档\")\n        if r.locked:\n            flags.append(\"运行中\")\n        if r.error:\n            flags.append(f\"读取异常:{r.error[:40]}\")\n        print(f\"  {r.session_id:&lt;24} {r.status:&lt;7} 目标 {r.goal_state:&lt;7} \"\n              f\"事件 {r.events:&lt;5} 任务 {r.tasks:&lt;3} {r.size_mb:&gt;6.1f}MB  {updated}  \"\n              f\"{r.cwd}  {' '.join(flags)}\")\n        if verbose:\n            print(f\"      {r.path}\")\n    print(\"\\n在 TUI 的“会话”面板可切换/新建/归档/删除；命令行恢复：\"\n          \"teamagents --resume &lt;会话 id&gt;\")\n    return 0\n\n\ndef _repl(args) -&gt; int:\n    \"\"\"Line-mode fallback for dumb terminals (`--plain`).\"\"\"\n    from .session import open_session\n\n    async def run() -&gt; int:\n        rt = await open_session(cwd=Path(args.cwd) if args.cwd else Path.cwd(),\n                                session_id=args.resume, full_auto=args.full_auto,\n                                initial_spec=(load_team_spec(args.team) if args.team\n                                              else None))\n        print(f\"session: {rt.session_id} (Ctrl-D to exit)\")\n        await rt.start()\n        try:\n            while True:\n                try:\n                    line = await asyncio.to_thread(input, \"you&gt; \")\n                except EOFError:\n                    break\n                if not line.strip():\n                    continue\n                receipt = rt.user_message(line)\n                print(f\"  [input received: {receipt.result.get('goal_id', '')}]\")\n                await rt.settle(timeout=600)\n                for event in rt.store.events(rt.session_id, after_sequence=rt.ui_cursor):\n                    rt.ui_cursor = event[\"sequence\"]\n                    _print_event(event)\n        finally:\n            await rt.close()\n            rt.store.close()\n        return 0\n\n    return asyncio.run(run())\n\n\ndef _run_tui(args) -&gt; int:\n    \"\"\"Product entry point: the Textual TUI over a live session.\"\"\"\n    from pathlib import Path as _Path\n\n    from .session import open_session\n    from .tui.app import TeamAgentsApp\n\n    cwd = _Path(args.cwd) if args.cwd else _Path.cwd()\n    initial_spec = load_team_spec(args.team) if args.team else None\n\n    async def factory(session_id: str | None = None):\n        return await open_session(cwd=cwd, session_id=session_id or args.resume,\n                                  full_auto=args.full_auto, initial_spec=initial_spec)\n\n    app = TeamAgentsApp(runtime_factory=factory, cwd=cwd)\n    app.run()\n    return 0\n\n\ndef _print_event(event) -&gt; None:\n    kind = event[\"kind\"]\n    actor = event[\"actor_id\"]\n    payload = json.loads(event[\"payload_json\"])\n    if kind == \"user_message\":\n        return\n    if kind in (\"task_completed\", \"task_failed\", \"task_blocked\", \"task_created\",\n                \"goal_done\", \"limit_reached\"):\n        print(f\"  [{kind}] {json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)[:200]}\")\n    elif kind == \"message\":\n        print(f\"  [message] {actor} -&gt; {payload.get('target')}: {payload.get('text', '')[:160]}\")\n    elif kind == \"approval_requested\":\n        print(f\"  [approval] {json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)[:200]}\")\n\n\ndef main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -&gt; int:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog=\"teamagents\", description=\"TeamAgents\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--cwd\", help=\"working directory for the session\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--resume\", help=\"resume the given session id\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--full-auto\", action=\"store_true\",\n                        help=\"user-chosen full-auto permission mode\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--team\", help=\"start a new session from this TeamSpec file\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--plain\", action=\"store_true\",\n                        help=\"line-mode REPL instead of the full TUI\")\n    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest=\"command\")\n    sub.add_parser(\"doctor\", help=\"check models, tools, isolation, Codex protocol\")\n    val = sub.add_parser(\"validate\", help=\"validate a team spec file\")\n    val.add_argument(\"spec\")\n    ses = sub.add_parser(\"sessions\", help=\"列出本机会话记录（路径、状态、大小）\")\n    ses.add_argument(\"-v\", \"--verbose\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"显示完整路径\")\n    sub.add_parser(\"version\")\n    args = parser.parse_args(argv)\n\n    match args.command:\n        case \"doctor\":\n            return doctor()\n        case \"validate\":\n            return validate_spec(args.spec)\n        case \"sessions\":\n            return list_sessions(verbose=args.verbose)\n        case \"version\":\n            print(json.dumps(version_report(), indent=2))\n            return 0\n        case _:\n            return _repl(args) if args.plain else _run_tui(args)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "  外部工具已执行但结果未知。最后一种进入 `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`，**不会**被当作成功或自动重试。\n- 取消是“等待停止确认”，不是回滚：已产生的文件、请求与制品保留；等待中的批准失效后需重新批准。\n\n### 3.1 会话记录在哪里、怎么管\n\n```\n$XDG_STATE_HOME/teamagents/sessions/&lt;会话 id&gt;/     # 默认 ~/.local/state/teamagents/sessions/\n├── team.db            业务事实：动作回执、事件流、任务、回合、投递、批准、共享条目、拓扑补丁\n├── checkpoints.sqlite 成员私有线程（LangGraph 检查点，按 会话+成员+上下文代次 命名）\n├── artifacts/         长输出与制品（工具结果里的 /artifacts/xxx 指向这里）\n├── members/&lt;成员&gt;/work/  隔离/ worktree 成员的专属工作目录\n└── session.lock       执行所有权文件锁（同一会话同时只允许一个运行实例）\n```\n\n```bash\nteamagents sessions            # 列出会话：状态、目标、事件/任务数、占用空间、路径\nteamagents --resume &lt;会话 id&gt;   # 恢复该会话（团队版本、待办、消息位置、成员线程、批准队列）\n```\n\nTUI 里同一件事在「会话」面板完成（`Ctrl+T` 循环到该面板）：\n`s` 或 `Enter` 切换、`n` 在当前目录新建会话、`a` 归档、`d` 删除（连按两次确认）。\n**归档/删除的是当前会话时会直接退出 TUI**；其他会话操作后留在原地并刷新列表。\n运行中的会话（其他进程持有文件锁）不允许切换/归档/删除，会明确告知。\n\n- **默认会话 id** 由工作目录派生（`proj_&lt;12位哈希&gt;`），即“一个项目一条会话线”；换目录或 `--resume` 指定其它会话即为隔离的新会话，互不继承（T6/T24）。\n- **删除**：删掉对应会话目录即可；若成员用过 `git_worktree`，先在项目里 `git worktree remove &lt;路径&gt;`（未合并成果要先处理，见 §5 与 `workspace.py`）。\n- **导出/排查**：`team.db` 是普通 SQLite，可直接查，例如查看最近事件：\n  ```bash\n  sqlite3 ~/.local/state/teamagents/sessions/&lt;id&gt;/team.db \\\n    \"select sequence,kind,actor_id,substr(payload_json,1,120) from events order by sequence desc limit 20;\"\n  ```\n- **不记录什么**：模型密钥（只引用环境变量）、Codex 自身的会话历史（在 `~/.codex`，我们只存线程引用）、TUI 的界面状态（展开/光标等）。\n- **并发保护**：会话文件锁 + SQLite 单写入口；第二个进程会明确报 “session is already running in another process”。\n\n## 4. 故障处理\n\n| 现象 | 处理 |\n|---|---|\n| 界面提示“成员 leader 的模型 profile 'leader_main' 未配置” | 会话启动时即提示（TUI 会直接写出配置路径）。创建 `~/.config/teamagents/config.toml`（可复制 `examples/config.toml`），补 `[models.leader_main]` 后重开会话 |\n| 输入后长时间没有回应 | 先看对话视图：回合失败会以 `✗ …回合失败：&lt;原因&gt;` 显示；若没有该行且状态栏“活动回合 ≥1”，说明模型正在生成（xhigh 思考可能较慢）。最常见原因是模型 profile 未配置或密钥环境变量缺失（`doctor` 可确认） |\n| 报缺少某环境变量 | profile 的 `api_key_env` 指向的变量未导出；导出后重跑 |\n| 命令因“refusing to run without isolation”失败 | 安装 bubblewrap；不要以降低隔离来绕过 |\n| Codex 成员卡在“Reconnecting” | codex 的 provider 凭据不可达：检查 `~/.codex/config.toml` 的默认 provider 与密钥，或给成员配置走环境变量密钥的 profile |\n| 回合因 `LIMIT_REACHED` 停止 | 达到目标回合/步骤上限；调整 `limits` 后继续，不是失败终态 |\n| 任务长期 `BLOCKED` | 依赖失败或成员回合未提交完成申请；Leader 会收到事件，可在 TUI 里取消或重派 |\n| 需要查看发生了什么 | TUI 日志面板 / `sessions/&lt;id&gt;/team.db` 的 events 表 / `run_progress` 事件 |\n| 隔离或协议自检 | `teamagents doctor`（依赖、配置、bubblewrap、codex schema、状态目录） |\n\n## 5. 团队定义（TeamSpec）要点\n\n- 字段：`leader_id`、`agents[]`（id/name/role/runtime_kind/instructions/model_profile/\n====\n22:class PanelReady(Message):\n69:    def action_submit_prompt(self) -&gt; None:\n76:    def action_newline(self) -&gt; None:\n79:    def on_key(self, event) -&gt; None:\n81:        if event.key == \"enter\":\n109:class TeamPanel(Vertical):\n139:                          \" \".join(reach) or \"-\", key=agent.id)\n142:class TasksPanel(Vertical):\n176:                          \",\".join(task.result_refs)[:60] or \"-\", key=task.task_id)\n183:            return str(table.coordinate_to_cell_key(table.cursor_coordinate).row_key.value)\n188:class SharedPanel(Vertical):\n208:                              key=f\"{entry.space_id}:{entry.sequence}\")\n211:class LogPanel(Vertical):\n247:class SettingsPanel(Vertical):\n307:class SessionsPanel(Vertical):\n355:                          \" \".join(marks) or \"-\", key=info.session_id)\n362:            return str(table.coordinate_to_cell_key(table.cursor_coordinate).row_key.value)\n366:    def on_key(self, event) -&gt; None:\n369:        if event.key in (\"enter\", \"s\"):\n373:        elif event.key == \"n\":\n376:        elif event.key == \"a\":\n380:        elif event.key == \"d\":\n391:        elif event.key == \"escape\":\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"Shared test helpers: team spec builders, scripted members, session harness.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom teamagents.agents import FakeMember\nfrom teamagents.models import (\n    AgentSpec,\n    ChannelMode,\n    ChannelSpec,\n    RuntimeKind,\n    TeamSpec,\n)\nfrom teamagents.runtime import SessionRuntime, fake_session\n\n\ndef member(agent_id: str, *, role: str = \"worker\", binds: list[str] | None = None,\n           runtime: RuntimeKind = RuntimeKind.DEEPAGENTS, profile: str = \"test\") -&gt; AgentSpec:\n    return AgentSpec(\n        id=agent_id, name=agent_id.title(), role=role,\n        runtime_kind=runtime, instructions=f\"act as {agent_id}\",\n        model_profile=profile, tool_bindings=binds or [\"files\"],\n    )\n\n\ndef leader() -&gt; AgentSpec:\n    return member(\"leader\", role=\"leader\", binds=[\"files\", \"shell\", \"web\"])\n\n\ndef spec_of(*agents: AgentSpec, channels: list[ChannelSpec] | None = None,\n            observers=None, shared_spaces=None, limits=None) -&gt; TeamSpec:\n    data: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"leader_id\": \"leader\",\n        \"agents\": [a.model_dump() for a in agents],\n        \"channels\": [c.model_dump() for c in (channels or [])],\n    }\n    if observers:\n        data[\"observers\"] = observers\n    if shared_spaces:\n        data[\"shared_spaces\"] = shared_spaces\n    if limits:\n        data[\"limits\"] = limits\n    return TeamSpec.model_validate(data)\n\n\ndef task_channel(source: str, targets: list[str]) -&gt; ChannelSpec:\n    return ChannelSpec(source=source, targets=targets, mode=ChannelMode.TASK)\n\n\ndef msg_channel(source: str, targets: list[str]) -&gt; ChannelSpec:\n    return ChannelSpec(source=source, targets=targets, mode=ChannelMode.MESSAGE)\n\n\ndef scripts(**members: list[tuple]) -&gt; dict[str, FakeMember]:\n    return {name: FakeMember(name, script) for name, script in members.items()}\n\n\nclass Harness:\n    def __init__(self, rt: SessionRuntime, members: dict[str, FakeMember]):\n        self.rt = rt\n        self.members = members\n\n    async def start(self):\n        await self.rt.start()\n        return self\n\n    async def user(self, text: str):\n        receipt = self.rt.user_message(text)\n        assert receipt.ok, receipt.error\n        return receipt\n\n    async def settle(self, timeout: float = 8.0) -&gt; None:\n        ok = await self.rt.settle(timeout)\n        assert ok, \"session did not settle: \" + self.describe()\n\n    def describe(self) -&gt; str:\n        runs = self.rt.store.runs_for_session(\n            self.rt.session_id, [\"QUEUED\", \"RUNNING\", \"WAITING_TASK\", \"WAITING_APPROVAL\"])\n        return \"; \".join(f\"{r.agent_id}:{r.status}\" for r in runs)\n\n    async def aclose(self):\n        await self.rt.close()\n        self.rt.store.close()\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\nasync def harness_factory(tmp_path):\n    created: list[Harness] = []\n\n    async def make(spec: TeamSpec, member_scripts: dict[str, FakeMember],\n                   catalog=None, pre_authorized=None, require_approval=None,\n                   tool_executor=None, session_id: str = \"s1\",\n                   barriers: dict[str, asyncio.Event] | None = None) -&gt; Harness:\n        for m in member_scripts.values():\n            if barriers:\n                m.barriers = {**m.barriers, **barriers}\n        rt = fake_session(tmp_path, spec, member_scripts, catalog=catalog,\n                          pre_authorized=pre_authorized, require_approval=require_approval,\n                          tool_executor=tool_executor, session_id=session_id)\n        h = Harness(rt, member_scripts)\n        created.append(h)\n        return await h.start()\n\n    yield make\n\n    for h in created:\n        try:\n            await h.aclose()\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"P2: persistence, restart recovery and the four crash windows.\n\n- queued execution intents survive a restart and still run\n- a RUNNING run without an external turn is re-queued; with one it becomes\n  OUTCOME_UNKNOWN instead of being blindly retried\n- an action committed before a crash is not applied twice after the retry\n- acknowledged deliveries are never injected twice\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\n\nfrom conftest import Harness, leader, member, msg_channel, scripts, spec_of, task_channel\nfrom teamagents.runtime import fake_session\n\n\nasync def test_crash_after_action_commit_applies_once(harness_factory, tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"Crash window 3: the team action committed but the turn never finished.\n    Re-running the turn replays the same action id -&gt; exactly one task.\"\"\"\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"), channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"])])\n\n    members = scripts(\n        leader=[(\"call\", \"assign_task\", {\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"one task\"}),\n                (\"barrier\", \"hold\"), (\"call\", \"signal_done\", {}), (\"end\",)],\n    )\n    h = await harness_factory(spec, members, barriers={\"hold\": asyncio.Barrier(2)})\n    await h.user(\"go\")\n    # wait until assign_task committed, then simulate a hard process loss\n    deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + 5\n    while not h.rt.store.tasks_for_session(\"s1\"):\n        assert asyncio.get_event_loop().time() &lt; deadline\n        await asyncio.sleep(0.01)\n    await h.rt.close()  # process loss: in-flight task cancelled mid-turn\n\n    # restart in a fresh runtime over the same database\n    fresh = scripts(\n        leader=[(\"call\", \"assign_task\", {\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"one task\"}),\n                (\"call\", \"signal_done\", {}), (\"end\",)],\n        b=[(\"call\", \"complete_task\", {\"task_id\": \"$inbox0.payload.task_id\"}), (\"end\",)],\n    )\n    h2 = Harness(fake_session(tmp_path, spec, fresh, session_id=\"s1\"), fresh)\n    await h2.start()\n    try:\n        await h2.settle(8)\n        tasks = h2.rt.store.tasks_for_session(\"s1\")\n        assert len(tasks) == 1, \"replayed action must dedup, not create a second task\"\n        created = [e for e in h2.rt.store.events(\"s1\") if e[\"kind\"] == \"task_created\"]\n        assert len(created) == 1\n    finally:\n        await h2.aclose()\n\n\nasync def test_running_run_with_external_turn_becomes_outcome_unknown(tmp_path):\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), channels=[])\n    members = scripts(leader=[(\"sleep\", 1), (\"end\",)])\n    rt = fake_session(tmp_path, spec, members, session_id=\"s1\")\n    await rt.start()\n    try:\n        rt.user_message(\"go\")\n        deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + 5\n        while not rt.store.runs_for_session(\"s1\", [\"RUNNING\"]):\n            assert asyncio.get_event_loop().time() &lt; deadline\n            await asyncio.sleep(0.01)\n        run = rt.store.runs_for_session(\"s1\", [\"RUNNING\"])[0]\n        # an external backend turn was submitted before the crash\n        rt.store.set_run_status(run.run_id, run.status, external_turn_id=\"codex-turn-1\")\n    finally:\n        await rt.close()\n        rt.store.close()\n\n    rt2 = fake_session(tmp_path, spec, scripts(leader=[(\"end\",)]), session_id=\"s1\")\n    await rt2.start()\n    try:\n        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)\n        run = rt2.store.get_run(run.run_id)\n        assert run.status == \"OUTCOME_UNKNOWN\"\n        assert not [r for r in rt2.store.runs_for_session(\"s1\", [\"RUNNING\"])], \\\n            \"unknown outcomes are never auto-retried\"\n    finally:\n        await rt2.close()\n        rt2.store.close()\n\n\nasync def test_acked_deliveries_are_not_injected_twice(harness_factory, tmp_path):\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"),\n                   channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"]), msg_channel(\"b\", [\"leader\"])])\n    members = scripts(\n        leader=[(\"call\", \"assign_task\", {\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"job\"}),\n                (\"call\", \"wait_for_tasks\", {\"task_ids\": [\"$r0.result.task_id\"]}),\n                (\"wait\",), (\"call\", \"signal_done\", {}), (\"end\",)],\n        b=[(\"call\", \"complete_task\", {\"task_id\": \"$inbox0.payload.task_id\"}), (\"end\",)],\n    )\n    h = await harness_factory(spec, members)\n    await h.user(\"run the job\")\n    await h.settle()\n    assert h.rt.store.pending_deliveries(\"s1\", \"b\") == []\n\n    # restart: nothing pending, nothing re-injected\n    fresh = scripts(leader=[], b=[])\n    h2 = Harness(fake_session(tmp_path, spec, fresh, session_id=\"s1\"), fresh)\n    await h2.start()\n    try:\n        await asyncio.sleep(0.1)\n        assert h2.rt.store.pending_deliveries(\"s1\", \"b\") == []\n        assert fresh[\"b\"].observed_inbox == []\n    finally:\n        await h2.aclose()\n\n\nasync def test_task_ready_announced_once_across_restart(harness_factory, tmp_path):\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"), channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"])])\n    members = scripts(leader=[(\"call\", \"assign_task\",\n                               {\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"job\"}), (\"end\",)],\n                      b=[(\"end\",)])\n    h = await harness_factory(spec, members)\n    await h.user(\"assign only\")\n    await h.settle(5)\n    before = len([e for e in h.rt.store.events(\"s1\") if e[\"kind\"] == \"task_ready\"])\n\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"P1 guards: ACL/validation violations are rejected with readable errors,\nidentity is runtime-injected, version conflicts never half-apply, and the\nturn budget stops runaway loops with LIMIT_REACHED.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\n\nfrom conftest import leader, member, msg_channel, scripts, spec_of, task_channel\nfrom teamagents.models import RuntimeKind, TeamAction\n\n\ndef act(kind: str, actor: str, payload: dict, action_id: str = None, run_id: str = None):\n    act.n += 1\n    return TeamAction(action_id=action_id or f\"t-{kind}-{actor}-{act.n}\", session_id=\"s1\",\n                      actor_id=actor, run_id=run_id, kind=kind, payload=payload)\nact.n = 0\n\n\nasync def test_violations_are_rejected(harness_factory):\n    spec = spec_of(\n        leader(), member(\"b\"), member(\"c\"),\n        channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"]), msg_channel(\"b\", [\"leader\"])],\n    )\n    h = await harness_factory(spec, scripts(leader=[(\"end\",)], b=[(\"end\",)]))\n\n    cases = [\n        (act(\"assign_task\", \"b\", {\"assignee\": \"ghost\", \"description\": \"x\"}),\n         \"unknown assignee\"),\n        (act(\"assign_task\", \"b\", {\"assignee\": \"leader\", \"description\": \"x\"}),\n         \"not allowed to assign\"),\n        (act(\"send_message\", \"b\", {\"target\": \"leader\", \"text\": \"hi\"}), None),  # allowed\n        (act(\"send_message\", \"leader\", {\"target\": \"b\", \"text\": \"hi\"}),\n         \"not allowed to message\"),\n        (act(\"signal_done\", \"b\", {}), \"only the Leader\"),\n        (act(\"apply_topology_patch\", \"b\", {\"operations\": [{\"op\": \"remove_agent\",\n                                                           \"agent_id\": \"leader\"}],\n                                           \"base_revision\": 1}), \"only the Leader\"),\n        (act(\"complete_task\", \"b\", {\"task_id\": \"nope\"}), \"unknown task\"),\n====\nsrc/teamagents/control.py:1007:                self._emit_limit_reached()\nsrc/teamagents/control.py:1093:    def _emit_limit_reached(self) -&gt; None:\nsrc/teamagents/control.py:1099:        draft = EventDraft(kind=EventKind.LIMIT_REACHED,\nsrc/teamagents/runtime.py:536:                    kind=EventKind.LIMIT_REACHED,\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "tests/conftest.py:35:            observers=None, shared_spaces=None, limits=None) -&gt; TeamSpec:\ntests/conftest.py:45:    if limits:\ntests/conftest.py:46:        data[\"limits\"] = limits\ntests/test_config_cli.py:25:    \"limits\": {\"max_parallel_workers\": 4, \"max_members\": 16, \"max_turns_per_goal\": 200},\ntests/test_config_cli.py:48:    bad[\"limits\"][\"max_members\"] = 0\ntests/test_p1_guards.py:110:async def test_turn_budget_stops_runaway_with_limit_reached(harness_factory):\ntests/test_p1_guards.py:115:        limits={\"max_parallel_workers\": 2, \"max_members\": 8, \"max_turns_per_goal\": 3,\ntests/test_p1_guards.py:132:    assert \"limit_reached\" in kinds, \"runaway scheduling must report LIMIT_REACHED\"\ntests/test_t5_shared.py:69:                                         \"limit\": 10}),\n====\n                        for d in fresh\n                    ]))\n                continue\n            if waiting is not None:\n                if waiting.status is TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL:\n                    continue\n                # ponytail: a waiting turn resumes when ALL waited tasks are\n                # terminal, or on user input / cancel. Partial results stay\n                # queued; split waits into separate waits if partial wake needed.\n                pending_wait = [t for t in waiting.waiting_on\n                                if (task := self.store.get_task(t)) is not None\n                                and task.status not in (TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED, TaskStatus.FAILED,\n                                                        TaskStatus.CANCELLED)]\n                user_input = any(d[\"event_kind\"] == EventKind.USER_MESSAGE for d in pending)\n                if pending_wait and not user_input and not waiting.cancel_requested:\n                    continue\n                if self.store.update_run_status_where(\n                        waiting.run_id, TurnStatus.WAITING_TASK, TurnStatus.RUNNING):\n                    self.store.append_run_inputs(\n                        waiting.run_id, [d[\"delivery_id\"] for d in pending])\n                continue\n            if not self._turn_budget_ok():\n                self._emit_limit_reached()\n                continue\n            task = self._next_ready_task(agent.id)\n            run = TurnRun(\n                run_id=new_id(\"run\"),\n                session_id=session,\n                task_id=task.task_id if task else None,\n                goal_id=self._current_goal_id(),\n                agent_id=agent.id,\n                config_revision=self.store.agent_config_revision(session, agent.id),\n                topology_revision=self.store.current_revision(session),\n                status=TurnStatus.QUEUED,\n                input_delivery_ids=[d[\"delivery_id\"] for d in pending],\n                context_ref=self._context_ref(agent.id),\n            )\n            self.store.insert_run(run)\n\n    def _block_unrunnable_tasks(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"Dependencies that failed/cancelled make dependents BLOCKED, never silently stuck.\"\"\"\n        for task in self.store.tasks_for_session(self.session_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING]):\n            deps = [self.store.get_task(d) for d in task.dependencies]\n            broken = [d for d in deps\n                      if d is not None and d.status in (TaskStatus.FAILED, TaskStatus.CANCELLED)]\n            if not broken:\n                continue\n            if self.store.compare_and_set_task(task.task_id, TaskStatus.PENDING,\n                                               TaskStatus.BLOCKED):\n                draft = EventDraft(\n                    kind=EventKind.TASK_BLOCKED,\n                    payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                             \"requester\": task.requester,\n                             \"reason\": \"dependency \" + \", \".join(\n                                 f\"{d.task_id}:{d.status}\" for d in broken)},\n                    task_id=task.task_id,\n                )\n                action = TeamAction(action_id=f\"blocked:{task.task_id}\",\n                                    session_id=self.session_id, actor_id=\"system\",\n                                    kind=ActionKind.CANCEL_TASK, payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id})\n                self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])\n\n    def _announce_ready_tasks(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:\n        \"\"\"One TASK_READY ping per task, once its dependencies are satisfied.\"\"\"\n        for task in sorted(self.store.tasks_for_session(self.session_id, [TaskStatus.PENDING]),\n                           key=lambda t: t.created_at):\n            deps = [self.store.get_task(d) for d in task.dependencies]\n            if not all(d is not None and d.status is TaskStatus.SUCCEEDED for d in deps):\n                continue\n            if self.store.get_meta(f\"task_ready_announced:{task.task_id}\"):\n                continue\n            draft = EventDraft(\n                kind=EventKind.TASK_READY,\n                payload={\"task_id\": task.task_id, \"assignee\": task.assignee,\n                         \"requester\": task.requester,\n                         \"description\": task.description, \"acceptance\": task.acceptance},\n                task_id=task.task_id,\n            )\n            action = TeamAction(action_id=f\"ready:{task.task_id}\", session_id=self.session_id,\n                                actor_id=\"system\", kind=ActionKind.CANCEL_TASK, payload={})\n            self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])\n            self.store.set_meta(f\"task_ready_announced:{task.task_id}\", \"1\")\n\n    def _apply_boundary_patches(self, spec: TeamSpec) -&gt; None:\n        for patch in self.store.patches_in_status(self.session_id, PatchStatus.WAITING_BOUNDARY):\n            if any(self._agent_has_live_run(a) for a in patch.affected_agents):\n                continue\n            current = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n            new_spec, error = self._apply_operations(current, patch.operations)\n            if error:\n                self.store.set_patch_status(patch.patch_id, PatchStatus.FAILED)\n                continue\n            revision = self.store.save_team_spec(self.session_id, new_spec)\n            self.store.set_patch_status(patch.patch_id, PatchStatus.APPLIED)\n            self._sync_members(new_spec, current)\n            self._persist_patch_event(patch, revision)\n\n    def _persist_patch_event(self, patch: TopologyPatch, revision: int) -&gt; None:\n        spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n        draft = EventDraft(kind=EventKind.TOPOLOGY_APPLIED,\n                           payload={\"patch_id\": patch.patch_id, \"revision\": revision,\n                                    \"decided_by\": patch.decided_by,\n                                    \"operations\": patch.operations})\n        action = TeamAction(action_id=f\"patch-apply:{patch.patch_id}\",\n                            session_id=self.session_id, actor_id=patch.decided_by or patch.proposer,\n                            kind=ActionKind.APPLY_TOPOLOGY_PATCH, payload={})\n        self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])\n\n    def _emit_limit_reached(self) -&gt; None:\n        key = f\"limit_notified:{self._current_goal_id()}\"\n        if self.store.get_meta(key):\n            return\n        self.store.set_meta(key, \"1\")\n        spec = self.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id)\n        draft = EventDraft(kind=EventKind.LIMIT_REACHED,\n                           payload={\"kind\": \"max_turns_per_goal\",\n                                    \"limit\": spec.limits.max_turns_per_goal,\n                                    \"goal_id\": self._current_goal_id()})\n        action = TeamAction(action_id=new_id(\"sys\"), session_id=self.session_id,\n                            actor_id=\"system\", kind=ActionKind.PAUSE_SESSION, payload={})\n        self._persist_events(action, spec, [draft])\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------- small helpers\n\n    def _agent_has_live_run(self, agent_id: str) -&gt; bool:\n        return any(r.agent_id == agent_id for r in self.store.runs_for_session(\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "        openai = ModelProfile(provider=\"openai\", protocol=\"openai\", model=\"gpt-6-astra\",\n                              generation_options={\"reasoning_effort\": \"xhigh\"})\n        options, note = normalize_effort(openai)\n        assert options[\"reasoning_effort\"] == \"xhigh\" and note is None\n    \n&gt;       model = build_chat_model(deepseek)\n                ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^\n\ntests/test_config_cli.py:144: \n_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ \nsrc/teamagents/providers.py:72: in build_chat_model\n    key = _api_key(profile)\n          ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^\n_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ \n\nprofile = ModelProfile(provider='deepseek', protocol='deepseek', model='deepseek-flash', base_url=None, api_key_env='DEEPSEEK_API_KEY', timeout=120, max_retries=5, generation_options={'reasoning_effort': 'xhigh'})\n\n    def _api_key(profile: ModelProfile) -&gt; str | None:\n        if profile.api_key_env:\n            key = os.environ.get(profile.api_key_env)\n            if not key:\n&gt;               raise ModelConfigError(\n                    f\"model profile {profile.provider!r} needs environment variable \"\n                    f\"{profile.api_key_env}; export it (secrets never enter TeamSpec)\")\nE               teamagents.providers.ModelConfigError: model profile 'deepseek' needs environment variable DEEPSEEK_API_KEY; export it (secrets never enter TeamSpec)\n\nsrc/teamagents/providers.py:54: ModelConfigError\n=========================== short test summary info ============================\nFAILED tests/test_config_cli.py::test_xhigh_maps_to_max_for_models_without_xhigh\n1 failed in 0.12s\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool"> 1  # TeamAgents 代码审查协议（v1）
 2  
 3  &gt; 本文件由 Leader 制定，所有审查员必须遵守。基准：`TeamAgents-Implementation-Plan.zh-CN.md`。
 4  
 5  ## 目标
 6  
 7  对 TeamAgents 仓库当前代码实现做系统审查：正确性、安全性、持久化/恢复、并发、与基准方案的一致性、测试证据可信度。产出：每位审查员一份 `review/findings-&lt;area&gt;.md`；Leader 汇总为 `review/REVIEW-REPORT.md`。
 8  
 9  ## 硬性规则
10  
11  1. **只读**：不得修改 `src/**`、`tests/**`、`docs/**`、`examples/**`、`pyproject.toml`、`uv.lock` 等仓库文件；只能在 `review/**` 下新建/写入文件（临时脚本放 `review/tmp/`，可清理）。
12  2. **不跑 live 测试**（沙箱无密钥、无网络）：不要用 `-m live`；不要联网。确定性套件可运行：
13     `.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q`
14     已知环境性失败（沙箱造成，不算代码缺陷，除非你能证明另有原因）：
15     - `tests/test_config_cli.py::test_xhigh_maps_to_max_for_models_without_xhigh`（缺 `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`）
16     - `tests/test_p3_web_tools.py::test_ssrf_guard_blocks_internal_targets`（无 DNS，无法解析 example.com）
17  3. **每条发现必须可核验**：给 `file:line`、代码摘录或命令、影响、建议修复、严重度。禁止无证据的泛泛结论；推测标注「待验证」。
18  4. 区分三类问题：
19     - A 与基准方案/文档声称不符；
20     - B 实现缺陷（正确性/安全/并发/恢复/错误处理）；
21     - C 通用质量问题（可维护性、死代码、边界、测试薄弱）。
22  5. 报告用中文，固定结构：
23     - 一、范围与方法（读了哪些文件、跑了什么命令）
24     - 二、结论（3–5 行）
25     - 三、发现清单（ID｜严重度｜标题｜证据 `file:line`｜影响｜建议）
26     - 四、与方案/文档的偏差
27     - 五、未验证/存疑项
28     - 六、自检（实际运行过的命令与结果）
29  6. 完成后向 Leader 发消息（`send_message`）：一句话结论 + Top 5 发现（ID/严重度/标题）+ 报告路径。**不要**发送完整报告正文。
30  7. 交叉检查：其他审查员的报告会陆续出现在 `review/findings-*.md`，你可以阅读以对齐口径，但不得改写他人文件。
31  
32  ## 审查基准（必须对照）
33  
34  - `TeamAgents-Implementation-Plan.zh-CN.md`：§2.2 八条核心约束、DP-1..12、§4 执行模型、§5 状态机、§6.4 资源上限、§7 团队动作与信息边界、§8 拓扑变更、§9 持久化/恢复/取消、§10 适配器、§11 模型、§12 工具权限与工作目录、§13 入口、§14 配置、§16–17 阶段与验收（T1–T24）。
35  - `docs/DECISIONS.md`：已确认偏离（D-1..D-9）；代码若偏离方案但无 DECISIONS 记录 = 发现。
36  - `docs/STATUS.md`、`docs/ACCEPTANCE.md`：对状态/证据的声称是否被代码与测试支撑。
37  
38  ## 严重度参考
39  
40  - **P0**：安全边界可绕过（权限/隔离/ACL/SSRF/密钥）、数据损坏或恢复错误、把失败/取消/超限谎报为完成、审计可信度问题。
41  - **P1**：关键语义错误、竞态、恢复窗口漏洞、异常被吞导致状态不可恢复。
42  - **P2**：边界/错误处理不当、文档与实现明显不符、测试薄弱可能掩盖回归。
43  - **P3**：可维护性/一致性/清理类。
44  
45  ## 汇总约定
46  
47  - 报告路径：
48    - core 域 → `review/findings-core.md`
49    - runtime 域 → `review/findings-runtime.md`
50    - adapters 域 → `review/findings-adapters.md`
51    - surface 域 → `review/findings-surface.md`
52    - verify 域 → `review/findings-verify.md`
53  - 另将「一句话结论 + 发现计数（P0/P1/P2/P3）」发布到 `review` 共享空间（`publish_shared`），便于交叉阅读。
54  - 只报告**你亲自读过并理解**的代码；没读到的模块不要臆断。</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "    name = \"teamagents\"\n    state_schema = TeamMemberState\n\n    def __init__(self, *, runner: \"DeepAgentsRunner\", approvals: ApprovalGate,\n                 max_model_steps: int):\n        self.runner = runner\n        self.approvals = approvals\n        self.max_model_steps = max_model_steps\n        self.model_steps = 0\n\n    def reset(self) -&gt; None:\n        self.model_steps = 0\n\n    async def abefore_model(self, state, runtime):\n        if self.model_steps &gt;= self.max_model_steps:\n            return None\n        items, batch = self.runner.current_inbox().drain()\n        if not items:\n            return None\n        text = \"\\n\".join(\n            \"&lt;update from=\\\"{}\\\" kind=\\\"{}\\\"&gt;{}&lt;/update&gt;\".format(\n                i.get(\"from\"), i.get(\"kind\"),\n                json.dumps(i.get(\"payload\", {}), ensure_ascii=False))\n            for i in items)\n        return {\"messages\": [HumanMessage(content=text)], \"applied_batch\": batch}\n\n    async def awrap_model_call(self, request, handler):\n        self.model_steps += 1\n        if self.model_steps &gt; self.max_model_steps:\n            raise TurnLimitExceeded(\n                f\"model-step limit {self.max_model_steps} reached for this turn\")\n        return await handler(request)\n\n    async def awrap_tool_call(self, request, handler):\n        tool_call = request.tool_call\n        name = tool_call[\"name\"]\n        if name in TEAM_TOOLS:\n            result = await handler(request)\n            if name == \"wait_for_tasks\":\n                # durable pause: the graph stops here and resumes on the same\n                # run_id when wait conditions resolve (plan section 6.2)\n                try:\n                    payload = json.loads(result.content)\n                except Exception:\n                    payload = {}\n                waiting = (payload.get(\"result\") or {}).get(\"waiting\")\n            return json.dumps({\"ok\": True, \"exit_code\": result.exit_code,\n                               \"output\": result.output,\n                               \"truncated\": result.truncated,\n                               \"artifact\": result.artifact}, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        return shell\n\n    def _ensure_graph(self, run: TurnRun):\n        if self._graph is not None and self._graph_revision == run.config_revision:\n            return self._graph\n        if self.model_override is not None:\n            model = self.model_override\n        else:\n            profile = resolve_profile(self.catalog, self.agent.model_profile)\n            model = build_chat_model(profile)\n        # the turn bound is spec data, never a literal: read it at graph (re)build\n        limits = self.approvals.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id).limits\n        self._middleware = TeamAgentMiddleware(\n            runner=self, approvals=self.approvals,\n            max_model_steps=limits.max_model_steps_per_turn)\n        tools = [*build_team_tools(self), self._shell_tool(),\n                 *(self._bound_tools or []), *self.extra_tools]\n        skills = [f\"/skills/{i}/\" for i, d in enumerate(self.skills_dirs) if d.is_dir()]\n        memory = [f\"/memory/{i}/{f.name}\" for i, f in enumerate(self.memory_files)\n                  if f.is_file()]\n        self._graph = create_deep_agent(\n            model=model,\n            tools=tools,\n            system_prompt=self.agent.instructions or None,\n            middleware=[self._middleware],\n            backend=self._build_backend(),\n            skills=skills or None,\n            memory=memory or None,\n            checkpointer=self.checkpointer,\n            name=self.agent.id,\n        )\n====\n237:class TeamAgentMiddleware(AgentMiddleware):\n355:        self._middleware: TeamAgentMiddleware | None = None\n414:            # TeamAgentMiddleware.awrap_tool_call, before this body runs\n437:        self._middleware = TeamAgentMiddleware(\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

shrink the budget so the loop hits it quickly\n        spec_obj = rt.store.load_team_spec(\"s1\")\n        spec_obj.limits.max_model_steps_per_turn = 3\n        rt.store.save_team_spec(\"s1\", spec_obj)\n        rt.user_message(\"spin forever\")\n        assert await rt.settle(30)\n        kinds = [e[\"kind\"] for e in rt.store.events(\"s1\")]\n        assert \"limit_reached\" in kinds\n        run = rt.store.runs_for_session(\"s1\")[0]\n        assert run.status == \"FAILED\"\n    finally:\n        await rt.close()\n        rt.store.close()\n====\n    assert task.assignee == \"b\"\n\n    events = [dict(e) for e in h.rt.store.events(\"s1\")]\n    kinds = [e[\"kind\"] for e in events]\n    assert \"task_created\" in kinds\n    assert \"task_started\" in kinds\n    assert \"task_completed\" in kinds\n    assert \"goal_done\" in kinds\n\n    # result receipt goes to the requester (Leader) via inbox delivery\n    done_event = next(e for e in events if e[\"kind\"] == \"task_completed\")\n    assert \"leader\" in done_event[\"audience_json\"]\n    assert \"b\" not in done_event[\"audience_json\"] or True  # assignee allowed to see own task\n\n    session = h.rt.store.get_session(\"s1\")\n    assert session[\"goal_state\"] == \"done\"\n\n\nasync def test_scenario_without_delegation_single_leader(harness_factory):\n    \"\"\"T9 baseline: the Leader alone can execute, deliver, and keep talking.\"\"\"\n    spec = spec_of(leader())\n====\n    c_receipts = [r for r in members[\"c\"].results]\n    leak = [r for r in c_receipts if not r.ok and \"not allowed to message\" in (r.error or \"\")]\n    assert leak, \"message outside the channel must be rejected\"\n    d_deliveries = h.rt.store.pending_deliveries(\"s1\", \"d\")\n    assert d_deliveries == []\n\n    # retrying the same action id must not deliver a second time\n    b_send = members[\"b\"].results[0]\n    again = h.rt.submit(TeamAction(\n        action_id=b_send.action_id.replace(\":step0\", \":step0\"), session_id=\"s1\",\n        actor_id=\"b\", run_id=None, kind=\"send_message\",\n        payload={\"target\": \"c\", \"text\": \"ping\"}))\n    assert again == h.rt.control.store.get_action_receipt(b_send.action_id)\n    assert len([e for e in h.rt.store.events(\"s1\") if e[\"kind\"] == \"message\"]) == 2\n====\n        assert runs[\"cx\"].status == \"COMPLETED\"\n        assert runs[\"cx\"].external_turn_id, \"turn id must be recorded\"\n        assert rt.store.get_codex_thread(\"s1\", \"cx\"), \"thread id persisted before submission\"\n        progress = [e for e in rt.store.events(\"s1\") if e[\"kind\"] == \"run_progress\"]\n        assert progress and \"fake reply\" in progress[0][\"payload_json\"]\n\n        # recovery mapping: a fresh runner reads its own thread history\n        fresh = CodexRunner(agent=rt.runners[\"cx\"].agent, session_id=\"s1\",\n                            workdir=cx.workdir, approvals=cx.approvals, store=rt.store,\n                            codex_bin=cx.codex_bin, env={\"FAKE_CODEX_MODE\": \"simple\"})\n        state = await fresh.reconcile(runs[\"cx\"])\n        assert state in (\"COMPLETED\", None), f\"unexpected reconcile state {state}\"\n        await fresh.aclose()\n    finally:\n        await rt.close()\n        rt.store.close()\n\n\nasync def test_codex_approval_accept_flows_through_team_queue(tmp_path):\n    rt, cx, _ = build_runtime(tmp_path, \"approval\")\n    h = Harness(rt, {})\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"P3/P7: real model contract tests (plan section 11) and one live end-to-end\nsession. Providers without credentials are skipped explicitly — a skip is never\ncounted as a release pass (plan section 17).\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, ToolMessage\nfrom langchain_core.tools import tool\n\nfrom teamagents.models import ModelProfile, UserConfig\nfrom teamagents.providers import build_chat_model\n\nDEEPSEEK = ModelProfile(provider=\"deepseek\", protocol=\"deepseek\",\n                        model=\"deepseek-flash\", api_key_env=\"DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\",\n                        timeout=60, max_retries=1,\n                        generation_options={\"reasoning_effort\": \"high\"})\nKIMI = ModelProfile(provider=\"openai\", protocol=\"openai\",\n                    model=\"kimi-k2-turbo-preview\",\n                    base_url=\"https://api.kimi.com/coding/v1\",\n                    api_key_env=\"KIMI_API_KEY\", timeout=60, max_retries=1)\n\nPROVIDERS = [\n    pytest.param(DEEPSEEK, id=\"deepseek\", marks=pytest.mark.skipif(\n        not os.environ.get(\"DEEPSEEK_API_KEY\"), reason=\"DEEPSEEK_API_KEY not set\")),\n    pytest.param(KIMI, id=\"kimi-openai-compat\", marks=pytest.mark.skipif(\n        not os.environ.get(\"KIMI_API_KEY\"), reason=\"KIMI_API_KEY not set\")),\n]\n\npytestmark = pytest.mark.live\n\n\n@tool\ndef add(a: int, b: int) -&gt; int:\n    \"\"\"Add two integers.\"\"\"\n    return a + b\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"profile\", PROVIDERS)\nasync def test_provider_contract_tool_call_and_continuation(profile):\n    model = build_chat_model(profile).bind_tools([add])\n    prompt = (\"You must call the add tool with a=17 and b=25. Do not compute the \"\n              \"result yourself; the tool does the arithmetic.\")\n    first = await model.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content=prompt)])\n    if not first.tool_calls:\n        # model judgement varies between runs; one retry, then fail honestly\n        first = await model.ainvoke([HumanMessage(\n            content=prompt + \" Call the add tool now; answering directly is wrong.\")])\n    assert first.tool_calls, f\"{profile.model}: no tool call produced\"\n    call = first.tool_calls[0]\n    assert call[\"name\"] == \"add\"\n    assert call[\"args\"].get(\"a\") == 17 and call[\"args\"].get(\"b\") == 25, \\\n        \"tool arguments must be assembled completely\"\n    second = await model.ainvoke([\n        HumanMessage(content=prompt), first,\n        ToolMessage(content=\"42\", tool_call_id=call[\"id\"])])\n    assert \"42\" in str(second.content), \"continuation must use the tool result\"\n    assert second.usage_metadata or getattr(second, \"response_metadata\", None), \\\n        \"usage/metadata must be preserved for accounting\"\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"profile\", PROVIDERS)\nasync def test_provider_contract_streaming_and_multi_turn(profile):\n    model = build_chat_model(profile)\n    chunks = []\n    async for chunk in model.astream([HumanMessage(content=\"Count: one two three\")]):\n        chunks.append(chunk)\n    text = \"\".join(str(c.content) for c in chunks)\n    assert chunks and text.strip(), \"streaming must produce content\"\n    again = await model.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content=\"Reply with the single word: ok\")])\n    assert str(again.content).strip()\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"profile\", PROVIDERS)\nasync def test_provider_contract_error_propagation(profile):\n    bad = profile.model_copy(update={\"model\": \"definitely-not-a-model-xyz\"})\n    model = build_chat_model(bad)\n    try:\n        response = await model.ainvoke([HumanMessage(content=\"hi\")])\n    except Exception as err:  # the expected path on a first-party endpoint\n        message = str(err).lower()\n        assert (\"model\" in message or \"not found\" in message or \"invalid\" in message\n                or \"400\" in message or \"404\" in message), \\\n            f\"provider error must surface: {err}\"\n        return\n    if profile.base_url:\n        # a relay may pin its own model and ignore the requested name: the\n        # response is then a normal completion, which is not an error at all\n        assert str(response.content).strip() or response.tool_calls\n        return\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "     1\t# 实施决策记录（偏离与澄清）\n     2\t\n     3\t基准文档：`TeamAgents-Implementation-Plan.zh-CN.md`。**任何偏离方案的做法，先与用户确认再实现。**\n     4\t本文件只记录已确认的决策；未确认的候选方案写在对话里，不写进代码。\n     5\t\n     6\t## D-1 短控制图：事务化步骤管线，不套 LangGraph StateGraph ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-11）\n     7\t\n     8\t- 方案原文：§2.1「LangGraph 负责……短流程团队控制图的执行」，§4 控制图执行\n     9\t  `ingest → validate → reduce → schedule → persist → END`。\n    10\t- 实施：同样一组固定步骤实现在 `control.py::Control.submit`，整步在**一个 SQLite 事务**内提交\n    11\t  （动作回执 + 事件 + 任务/共享空间变更 + 排队中的执行意图），执行意图持久化为 `QUEUED` TurnRun 后再交给运行时。\n    12\t- 理由：该管线不调用模型、无图状态需要检查点；业务数据库是权威，事务原子性强于图检查点恢复。\n    13\t  套一层 StateGraph 只增加壳，不增加能力（DP-1 的实质要求「固定小图 + 拓扑是数据」已满足）。\n    14\t- 代价与升级路径：控制流步骤无法像图节点那样逐节点重放；若将来控制流出现跨事务的长时间步骤\n    15\t  （例如需要中途调用外部系统），再引入 LangGraph 并按节点切分。\n    16\t\n    17\t## D-2 工具权限：统一网关 + 逐调用批准 ✅（按方案 §12.2 执行，非偏离）\n    18\t\n    19\tDeep Agents 的 `permissions` 只覆盖其内置文件工具，不约束 Shell/MCP；因此\n    20\t`ToolGateway`（`agents.py`）是所有成员工具调用的唯一入口：团队动作（ACL 校验）与执行工具（权限评估 + 批准）\n    21\t共用同一路径，身份由运行时注入。此为实现细节，与方案一致。\n    22\t\n    23\t## D-3 Codex 协议 Schema 不入库 ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-11）\n    24\t\n    25\t`teamagents doctor` 用本机 `codex app-server generate-json-schema` 现场生成并校验所需方法集合，\n    26\t避免 4.3MB 生成物随 CLI 升级漂移。方案要求「Schema 根据选定 CLI 版本生成」，此实现满足该要求。\n    27\t\n    28\t## D-4 Codex 成员默认配置 ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-11）\n    29\t\n    30\t- 默认模型：不传 `model`，继承本机 `~/.codex/config.toml` 的默认模型与认证（当前为\n    31\t  `gpt-6-astra`，走 apexin 中转）；成员级可在 TeamSpec/用户配置中覆盖 model 与工作目录。\n    32\t- 思考强度：**固定 `xhigh`**；若所选模型不支持 `xhigh`，自动回退 `max` 并记住结果（一次性校准）。\n    33\t  每个回合通过 `turn/start` 的 `effort` 参数显式传入，不依赖本机默认值。\n    34\t- 沙箱/批准：每回合显式传 `sandbox`/`approvalPolicy`/`approvalsReviewer=\"user\"`，\n    35\t  不沿用本机 `auto_review` 的宽松设置（P0 已实测本机配置会放行 /tmp 写入）。\n    36\t\n    37\t## D-5 网页搜索：AnySearch 走 HTTP 直连工具 ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-11）\n    38\t\n    39\t- 用户选择 (a)：接入 AnySearch。实测其接口为 `POST https://api.anysearch.com/v1/search`\n    40\t  （Bearer `ANYSEARCH_API_KEY`，body `{\"query\",\"max_results\"}`，返回 `code=0/data.results[]`），\n    41\t  **不是 MCP 服务**。\n    42\t- 实施：`tools.py` 提供 `kind=\"web_search\"`（provider=`anysearch`）与 `kind=\"web_fetch\"`\n    43\t  的内置工具；MCP 通用路径保留给其他搜索/提取服务（如 Tavily MCP）。工具是否出现由用户配置的\n    44\t  绑定决定，绑定即授权（无逐次批准）。抓取工具带 SSRF 防护（禁内网/回环/保留地址）与体积上限。\n    45\t\n    46\t## D-6 CLI 增加 `--team FILE`（在方案 §13 五个入口之外的小扩展）\n    47\t\n    48\t- 缘由：以指定 TeamSpec 启动会话是可测/可复现的前提（示例与测试都要用），\n    49\t  且与已有 `validate TEAM_SPEC` 对称。方案 §13 的五个入口全部保留。\n    50\t\n    51\t## D-7 Python 要求提升到 `&gt;=3.12` ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-12）\n    52\t\n    53\t- 方案 §3 原文为 Python 3.11+；用户要求提升到 **3.12+**，避免偏旧的 Python 行为。\n    54\t- 方案正文已同步（§3 现为 Python 3.12+）；实现侧同步：`pyproject.toml` 的\n    55\t  `requires-python`、锁文件重解析、README 与本文档；\n    56\t  本机开发/验收用 3.13.15，测试套件（65 确定性 + 12 实跑）在 3.13 上全绿。\n    57\t\n    58\t## D-8 默认模型统一为 `deepseek-flash` ✅（用户已确认 2026-09-12）\n    59\t\n    60\t- 用户要求：TeamAgents 的默认模型配置全部使用 `deepseek-flash`。\n    61\t- 落地：`examples/config.toml` 与用户 `~/.config/teamagents/config.toml` 的\n    62\t  `leader_main` / `research` / `coding` 全部指向 `deepseek-flash`\n    63\t  （provider=deepseek，`generation_options = { reasoning_effort = \"high\" }`）；\n    64\t  实跑测试与三个端到端示例同步使用它。\n    65\t- 其他服务（Kimi / apexin / Anthropic / GLM / Tavily）在示例配置里保留为注释，按需启用。\n    66\t- **推理档位**（用户确认 2026-09-12）：产品默认配置用 **`max`**，\n    67\t  **测试用 `high`**（跑得快、不改变被测语义）。实测（同一句“1+1 等于几 + signal_done”\n    68\t  的 Leader 回合）：`high` ≈ 10s、不指定 ≈ 27s、`max` ≈ **240s**；交互场景嫌慢就把\n    69\t  `generation_options` 改成 `high`。\n    70\t- **xhigh → max 映射**（用户确认）：模型不支持 `xhigh` 时，配置里的 `xhigh`\n    71\t  **自动映射为 `max`**，而不是报错或丢弃：`providers.normalize_effort` 对已知不支持\n    72\t  xhigh 的协议（DeepSeek）在构建模型时直接映射；其余供应商由成员运行器在首次被拒后\n    73\t  自动改判 `max` 并重建模型重试一次（`DeepAgentsRunner._switch_effort_to_max`，\n    74\t  Codex 适配器同样回退到 `max`）。\n    75\t\n    76\t## D-9 TUI 会话管理：切换 / 新建 / 归档 / 删除 ✅（用户要求 2026-09-12）\n    77\t\n    78\t- 需求：「在当前目录下的多个会话间切换，并支持归档/删除当前目录下的任意会话；\n    79\t  若删除的是当前会话则退出 TUI」。\n    80\t- 落地：\n    81\t  - 新增「会话」面板（`s`/`Enter` 切换、`n` 新建、`a` 归档、`d` 两次确认删除）；\n    82\t  - 归档 = 移动到 `sessions/archived/&lt;id&gt;` 并置 `CLOSED`，记录全部保留；\n    83\t  - 删除前检查：会话被其他进程持有（文件锁）→ 拒绝；成员 worktree 有未提交/未合并成果\n    84\t    → 拒绝并说明；删除当前会话时先释放锁与后端再删除，然后退出 TUI；\n    85\t  - 归档/删除当前会话后直接退出（符合需求）；其他会话操作后留在原地刷新列表；\n    86\t  - `teamagents sessions` 复用同一份清单（含“已归档/运行中”标记）。\n    87\t\n    88\t## D-10 放宽执行上限、删除无效配置 ✅（用户确认 2026-09-12）\n    89\t\n    90\t用户认为原默认值过于保守，逐项给出新默认值：\n    91\t\n    92\t- `TeamSpec.limits`：`max_parallel_workers` 4→**8**、`max_members` 16→**20**、\n    93\t  `max_turns_per_goal` 200→**1000**、`max_model_steps_per_turn` 50→**200**、\n    94\t  `turn_active_timeout_s` 900→**1200**、`cancel_confirm_timeout_s` 30→**60**。\n    95\t- `ModelProfile.max_retries` 2→**5**（模型请求的 SDK 级重试）。\n    96\t- 沙箱命令输出上限 100 KB→**200 KB**（超出仍转存 artifact 文件）。\n    97\t- **删除未被任何代码读取的配置**：`leader_reserve`、`model_request_timeout_s`、\n    98\t  `max_auto_retries`（`Limits` 为 `extra=\"forbid\"`，旧 TeamSpec 里若仍写这三个键会校验失败）。\n    99\t  真正生效的模型请求超时/重试是 `ModelProfile.timeout` / `ModelProfile.max_retries`。\n   100\t- **修复 bug**：`DeepAgentsRunner` 的回合步数上限原先硬编码 50，与\n   101\t  `limits.max_model_steps_per_turn` 脱节（把 spec 调大也仍会在 50 步被掐断）。\n   102\t  现改为在图（重）建时读取会话 TeamSpec，`max_model_steps_per_turn` 真正生效。\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "==T6==\n\"\"\"T6: directed messages stay out of third-party views; no private context\nreaches the Leader automatically; new sessions do not inherit anything.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom conftest import Harness, leader, member, msg_channel, scripts, spec_of, task_channel\nfrom teamagents.runtime import fake_session\n\n\nasync def test_t6_private_message_not_in_third_party_view(harness_factory):\n    spec = spec_of(\n        leader(), member(\"b\"), member(\"c\"),\n        channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\", \"c\"]),\n                  msg_channel(\"b\", [\"c\"]), msg_channel(\"c\", [\"b\"])],\n    )\n    members = scripts(\n        leader=[(\"call\", \"assign_task\", {\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"work\"}),\n                (\"inbox\",),\n                (\"call\", \"wait_for_tasks\", {\"task_ids\": [\"$r0.result.task_id\"]}),\n                (\"wait\",), (\"call\", \"signal_done\", {}), (\"end\",)],\n        b=[(\"call\", \"send_message\", {\"target\": \"c\", \"text\": \"for your eyes only\"}),\n           (\"call\", \"complete_task\", {\"task_id\": \"$inbox0.payload.task_id\"}),\n           (\"end\",)],\n        c=[(\"inbox\",), (\"end\",)],\n    )\n    h = await harness_factory(spec, members)\n    await h.user(\"b and c coordinate\")\n    await h.settle()\n\n    c_texts = [i[\"payload\"][\"text\"] for i in members[\"c\"].observed_inbox\n               if i.get(\"kind\") == \"message\"]\n    assert c_texts == [\"for your eyes only\"]\n    # the Leader was never delivered the private exchange\n    leader_texts = [i[\"payload\"].get(\"text\") for i in members[\"leader\"].observed_inbox\n                    if i.get(\"kind\") == \"message\"]\n    assert \"for your eyes only\" not in leader_texts\n\n\nasync def test_t6_new_session_does_not_inherit(harness_factory, tmp_path):\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"),\n                   channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"])])\n    members = scripts(\n        leader=[(\"call\", \"assign_task\", {\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"one-off\"}),\n                (\"call\", \"wait_for_tasks\", {\"task_ids\": [\"$r0.result.task_id\"]}),\n                (\"wait\",), (\"call\", \"signal_done\", {}), (\"end\",)],\n        b=[(\"call\", \"complete_task\", {\"task_id\": \"$inbox0.payload.task_id\"}), (\"end\",)],\n    )\n    h = await harness_factory(spec, members)\n    await h.user(\"session one work\")\n    await h.settle()\n    assert h.rt.store.tasks_for_session(\"s1\")\n\n    rt2 = fake_session(tmp_path, spec, {\"leader\": members[\"leader\"], \"b\": members[\"b\"]},\n                       session_id=\"s2\")\n    h2 = Harness(rt2, members)\n    await h2.start()\n    try:\n        assert rt2.store.tasks_for_session(\"s2\") == []\n        assert rt2.store.events(\"s2\") == []\n        assert rt2.store.pending_deliveries(\"s2\", \"leader\") == []\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
