<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"P0-4: an exception inside `Control._reduce` must not half-apply its writes.\n\nThe action's transaction rolls back; the failure receipt is recorded afterwards in\na clean transaction, so the same action id replays the refusal while a new action\nid may retry against the clean state.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import leader, member, msg_channel, spec_of, task_channel\nfrom teamagents.control import Control\nfrom teamagents.models import ActionKind, TeamAction\nfrom teamagents.storage import Store\n\n\ndef build_control(tmp_path):\n    store = Store(tmp_path / \"s1.db\")\n    store.create_session(\"s1\", str(tmp_path), \"approved_scope\")\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"),\n                   channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"]), msg_channel(\"b\", [\"leader\"])],\n                   shared_spaces=[{\"id\": \"main\", \"readers\": [\"leader\", \"b\"],\n                                   \"writers\": [\"leader\", \"b\"]}])\n    store.save_team_spec(\"s1\", spec)\n    for agent in spec.agents:\n        store.ensure_agent(\"s1\", agent.id)\n    return store, Control(store, \"s1\")\n\n\ndef test_reduce_failure_rolls_back_shared_entry_and_receipt_semantics(tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    store, control = build_control(tmp_path)\n    original = Store.add_shared_entry\n\n    def boom(self, entry, session_id):\n        original(self, entry, session_id)              # the write happens ...\n        raise RuntimeError(\"simulated failure after write\")  # ... then the step fails\n\n    publish = TeamAction(action_id=\"publish-1\", session_id=\"s1\", actor_id=\"b\",\n                         kind=ActionKind.PUBLISH_SHARED,\n                         payload={\"space_id\": \"main\", \"content\": \"partial-write\"})\n    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:\n        patch.setattr(Store, \"add_shared_entry\", boom)\n        receipt = control.submit(publish)\n\n    assert not receipt.ok\n    assert \"simulated failure after write\" in (receipt.error or \"\")\n    assert store.shared_entries(\"s1\", [\"main\"]) == [], \\\n        \"the entry written before the exception must roll back with the attempt\"\n    assert store.events(\"s1\") == [], \"no event may leak from the failed attempt\"\n\n    # same action id: the recorded refusal replays, nothing runs twice\n    replay = control.submit(publish)\n    assert not replay.ok and replay.error == receipt.error\n    assert store.shared_entries(\"s1\", [\"main\"]) == []\n\n    # new action id: retry succeeds on the clean state, exactly one entry\n    retry = control.submit(TeamAction(action_id=\"publish-2\", session_id=\"s1\", actor_id=\"b\",\n                                      kind=ActionKind.PUBLISH_SHARED,\n                                      payload={\"space_id\": \"main\", \"content\": \"partial-write\"}))\n    assert retry.ok, retry.error\n    entries = store.shared_entries(\"s1\", [\"main\"])\n    assert [e.content for e in entries] == [\"partial-write\"]\n    store.close()\n\n\ndef test_reduce_failure_leaves_task_state_untouched(tmp_path, monkeypatch):\n    store, control = build_control(tmp_path)\n    original = Store.insert_task\n\n    def boom(self, session_id, task):\n        original(self, session_id, task)\n        raise RuntimeError(\"boom after task insert\")\n\n    assign = TeamAction(action_id=\"assign-1\", session_id=\"s1\", actor_id=\"leader\",\n                        kind=ActionKind.ASSIGN_TASK,\n                        payload={\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"job\"})\n    with monkeypatch.context() as patch:\n        patch.setattr(Store, \"insert_task\", boom)\n        receipt = control.submit(assign)\n\n    assert not receipt.ok and \"boom after task insert\" in (receipt.error or \"\")\n    assert store.tasks_for_session(\"s1\") == [], \"the task row must roll back\"\n    assert not [e for e in store.events(\"s1\") if e[\"kind\"] == \"task_created\"]\n\n    ok = control.submit(TeamAction(action_id=\"assign-2\", session_id=\"s1\", actor_id=\"leader\",\n                                   kind=ActionKind.ASSIGN_TASK,\n                                   payload={\"assignee\": \"b\", \"description\": \"job\"}))\n    assert ok.ok, ok.error\n    tasks = store.tasks_for_session(\"s1\")\n    assert [t.task_id for t in tasks] == [ok.result[\"task_id\"]]\n    store.close()\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"kind,payload\", [\n    (\"send_message\", {\"target\": \"nobody\", \"text\": \"hi\"}),\n    (\"cancel_run\", {\"run_id\": \"missing-run\"}),\n    (\"approval_decision\", {\"approval_id\": \"missing-approval\", \"decision\": \"once\"}),\n])\ndef test_validation_refusals_still_get_a_readable_receipt(tmp_path, kind, payload):\n    store, control = build_control(tmp_path)\n    actor = \"user\" if kind in (\"cancel_run\", \"approval_decision\") else \"b\"\n    action = TeamAction(action_id=f\"x-{kind}\", session_id=\"s1\", actor_id=actor, kind=kind,\n                        payload=payload)\n    receipt = control.submit(action)\n    assert receipt.ok is False and receipt.error\n    assert store.get_action_receipt(action.action_id) is not None, \"refusal is replayable\"\n    store.close()\n==== RT06 TEST ====\n\"\"\"RT-06: a turn that ends (or is cancelled) never keeps a PENDING approval.\n\nOtherwise `pending_approvals` / `WAITING_APPROVAL` runs block `_completion_blockers`\nforever and `signal_done` can never succeed (reproduced on the live session).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import leader, member, scripts, spec_of, task_channel\nfrom teamagents.agents import FakeMember, TurnOutcome\nfrom teamagents.models import (\n    ActionKind,\n    AgentStatus,\n    ApprovalRequest,\n    ApprovalStatus,\n    TeamAction,\n    TurnRun,\n    TurnStatus,\n    UserConfig,\n    new_id,\n)\nfrom teamagents.runtime import SessionRuntime, fake_session\nfrom teamagents.storage import Store\n\n\ndef build_store(tmp_path, spec):\n    store = Store(tmp_path / \"s1.db\")\n    store.create_session(\"s1\", str(tmp_path), \"approved_scope\")\n    store.save_team_spec(\"s1\", spec)\n    for agent in spec.agents:\n        store.ensure_agent(\"s1\", agent.id)\n    return store\n\n\nasync def wait_for(pred, timeout: float = 5.0, what: str = \"condition\"):\n    deadline = asyncio.get_event_loop().time() + timeout\n    while not pred():\n        assert asyncio.get_event_loop().time() &lt; deadline, f\"timeout waiting for {what}\"\n        await asyncio.sleep(0.01)\n\n\ndef insert_pending_approval(store, run_id: str, agent_id: str = \"leader\") -&gt; ApprovalRequest:\n    req = ApprovalRequest(approval_id=new_id(\"appr\"), session_id=\"s1\", agent_id=agent_id,\n                          run_id=run_id, tool_call_id=f\"{run_id}:call-1\",\n                          operation_hash=\"op-hash\", requested_scope={\"tool\": \"shell\"},\n                          policy_revision=1)\n    store.insert_approval(req)\n    return req\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------- runtime\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"status\", [TurnStatus.COMPLETED, TurnStatus.FAILED,\n                                    TurnStatus.CANCELLED, TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN])\ndef test_finalize_terminal_expires_the_runs_pending_approval(tmp_path, status):\n    spec = spec_of(leader())\n    store = build_store(tmp_path, spec)\n    run = TurnRun(run_id=new_id(\"run\"), session_id=\"s1\", agent_id=\"leader\",\n                  config_revision=1, topology_revision=1, status=TurnStatus.RUNNING)\n    store.insert_run(run)\n    req = insert_pending_approval(store, run.run_id)\n    rt = SessionRuntime(store, \"s1\", UserConfig(), runners={\"leader\": FakeMember(\"leader\")})\n\n    rt._finalize(store.get_run(run.run_id), TurnOutcome(status=status))\n\n    assert store.get_run(run.run_id).status is status\n    assert store.get_approval(req.approval_id).status is ApprovalStatus.EXPIRED\n    assert store.pending_approvals(\"s1\") == []\n    decided = [e for e in store.events(\"s1\")\n               if e[\"kind\"] == \"approval_decided\" and \"EXPIRED\" in e[\"payload_json\"]]\n    assert decided, \"expiry is auditable\"\n    store.close()\n\n\ndef test_finalize_waiting_approval_keeps_the_request_open(tmp_path):\n    spec = spec_of(leader())\n    store = build_store(tmp_path, spec)\n    run = TurnRun(run_id=new_id(\"run\"), session_id=\"s1\", agent_id=\"leader\",\n                  config_revision=1, topology_revision=1, status=TurnStatus.RUNNING)\n    store.insert_run(run)\n    req = insert_pending_approval(store, run.run_id)\n    rt = SessionRuntime(store, \"s1\", UserConfig(), runners={\"leader\": FakeMember(\"leader\")})\n\n    rt._finalize(store.get_run(run.run_id), TurnOutcome(status=TurnStatus.WAITING_APPROVAL))\n\n    assert store.get_approval(req.approval_id).status is ApprovalStatus.PENDING\n    store.close()\n\n\nasync def test_reconcile_outcome_unknown_expires_the_pending_approval(tmp_path):\n    spec = spec_of(leader())\n    store = build_store(tmp_path, spec)\n    run = TurnRun(run_id=new_id(\"run\"), session_id=\"s1\", agent_id=\"leader\",\n                  config_revision=1, topology_revision=1, status=TurnStatus.RUNNING,\n                  external_turn_id=\"codex-turn-1\")\n    store.insert_run(run)\n    req = insert_pending_approval(store, run.run_id)\n    rt = SessionRuntime(store, \"s1\", UserConfig(), runners={\"leader\": FakeMember(\"leader\")})\n\n    await rt.reconcile()\n\n    assert store.get_run(run.run_id).status is TurnStatus.OUTCOME_UNKNOWN\n    assert store.get_approval(req.approval_id).status is ApprovalStatus.EXPIRED\n    assert store.pending_approvals(\"s1\") == []\n    kinds = [e[\"kind\"] for e in store.events(\"s1\")]\n    assert \"run_failed\" in kinds and \"approval_decided\" in kinds\n    store.close()\n\n\n# ------------------------------------------------------------------ cancel path\n\n\nasync def test_cancel_run_on_a_parked_turn_expires_the_approval(tmp_path):\n    spec = spec_of(leader())\n    members = scripts(leader=[(\"call\", \"shell\", {\"command\": \"echo x\"}), (\"end\",)])\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

0:\n988-            self.store._lock.release()\n989-            return False\n990-        if exc_type is None:\n991-            self.conn.execute(\"COMMIT\")\n992-        else:\n993-            self.conn.execute(\"ROLLBACK\")\n994-        self.store._lock.release()\n995-        return False\n996-\n997-\n998-class _LockedConn:\n999-    \"\"\"sqlite3 connection whose statements are serialized across threads.\"\"\"\n1000-\n1001-    def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, lock: threading.RLock):\n1002-        object.__setattr__(self, \"_conn\", conn)\n==== evaluate ====\n                 require_approval: set[str] | None = None,\n                 write_paths: list[Any] | None = None):\n        self.mode = mode\n        #: tools that may run without asking inside the approved scope\n        self.pre_authorized = pre_authorized if pre_authorized is not None else {\"files\", \"shell\"}\n        #: tools that always ask in approved_scope (e.g. network egress)\n        self.require_approval = require_approval or set()\n        #: directories the user pre-authorized for file writes (real paths)\n        self.write_paths = [str(p) for p in (write_paths or [])]\n\n    def evaluate(self, tool_name: str, args: dict[str, Any]) -&gt; Decision:\n        \"\"\"Decide one call. Unknown tools ask; approved scope is allow-by-default\n        only for tools the runtime actually bound to the member.\"\"\"\n        if self.mode is PermissionMode.FULL_AUTO:\n            return Decision(allow=True)\n        if tool_name == \"shell\" and args.get(\"network\"):\n            return Decision(allow=False,\n                            scope={\"tool\": tool_name, \"args\": args,\n                                   \"reason\": \"shell network access is off by default\"},\n                            reason=\"shell network access requires approval\")\n        if tool_name in self.require_approval:\n            return Decision(allow=False,\n                            scope={\"tool\": tool_name, \"args\": args,\n                                   \"reason\": f\"{tool_name} needs approval\"},\n                            reason=\"outside pre-authorized scope\")\n        if tool_name in self.pre_authorized or self._bound_tool(tool_name):\n            return Decision(allow=True)\n        if tool_name.startswith((\"mcp_\", \"web_\")):\n            return Decision(allow=True)\n        return Decision(allow=False,\n                        scope={\"tool\": tool_name, \"args\": args,\n                               \"reason\": f\"{tool_name} is not pre-authorized\"},\n                        reason=\"tool not in approved scope\")\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _bound_tool(tool_name: str) -&gt; bool:\n        \"\"\"Runtime-bound execution tools: file tools stay inside the sandboxed\n        backend and MCP/web tools exist only when the user configured them.\"\"\"\n        file_tools = {\"ls\", \"read_file\", \"write_file\", \"edit_file\", \"delete\",\n                      \"glob\", \"grep\", \"read_artifact\"}\n        return tool_name in file_tools\n\n\nclass ApprovalGate:\n    \"\"\"Creates approval requests and resolves them once/session/deny.\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, store: Store, session_id: str, policy: PermissionPolicy):\n        self.store = store\n        self.session_id = session_id\n        self.policy = policy\n        self.policy_revision = 1\n\n    def set_mode(self, mode: PermissionMode) -&gt; None:\n        self.policy.mode = mode\n        self.policy_revision += 1\n\n    def check(self, agent_id: str, run_id: str, tool_name: str, args: dict[str, Any],\n              tool_call_id: str) -&gt; tuple[Decision, ApprovalRequest | None]:\n        \"\"\"Returns (decision, approval_request). An open request means: pause.\"\"\"\n        decision = self.policy.evaluate(tool_name, args)\n        if decision.allow:\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>

spec)\n    run = TurnRun(run_id=new_id(\"run\"), session_id=\"s1\", agent_id=\"leader\",\n==== TUI TEST ====\n\"\"\"P6 follow-up (A-03 / B-02 / B-06): cancel a task from the task panel and\nverify the periodic refresh actually repaints status/panels/log.\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom conftest import leader, member, msg_channel, scripts, spec_of, task_channel\nfrom teamagents.models import (\n    ActionKind,\n    EventKind,\n    Task,\n    TaskStatus,\n    TeamAction,\n    TeamEvent,\n    TurnRun,\n)\nfrom teamagents.tui.app import TeamAgentsApp\nfrom teamagents.tui.panels import StatusBar, TasksPanel\n\n\nasync def _session(harness_factory, *, b_script=None):\n    spec = spec_of(leader(), member(\"b\"),\n                   channels=[task_channel(\"leader\", [\"b\"]), msg_channel(\"b\", [\"leader\"])])\n    return await harness_factory(spec, scripts(leader=[(\"end\",)],\n                                               b=b_script or [(\"end\",)]))\n\n\ndef _task(task_id: str, status: TaskStatus, assignee: str = \"b\") -&gt; Task:\n    return Task(task_id=task_id, requester=\"leader\", assignee=assignee,\n                description=f\"work {task_id}\", status=status)\n\n\ndef _seed(rt, task: Task, run: TurnRun | None = None) -&gt; None:\n    with rt.store.tx():\n        rt.store.insert_task(rt.session_id, task)\n        if run is not None:\n            rt.store.insert_run(run)\n\n\nasync def _open_tasks(pilot, app) -&gt; \"object\":\n    await pilot.pause(0.4)\n    app.query_one(\"TabbedContent\").active = \"tab-tasks\"\n    await pilot.pause(0.4)\n    table = app.query_one(TasksPanel).query_one(\"#tasks-table\")\n    table.move_cursor(row=0)\n    table.focus()\n    await pilot.pause(0.1)\n    return table\n\n\ndef _chat_text(app) -&gt; str:\n    return \"\\n\".join(str(line) for line in app.query_one(\"#chat-stream\").lines)\n\n\nasync def test_tui_task_panel_cancels_blocked_task(harness_factory):\n    \"\"\"A-03：BLOCKED 任务（被中断回合留下的唯一出口）可在任务面板按 c 取消。\"\"\"\n    h = await _session(harness_factory)\n    _seed(h.rt, _task(\"task-blocked-1\", TaskStatus.BLOCKED))\n    app = TeamAgentsApp(runtime=h.rt)\n    async with app.run_test(size=(120, 40)) as pilot:\n        table = await _open_tasks(pilot, app)\n        assert table.row_count == 1\n        await pilot.press(\"c\")\n        await pilot.pause(0.3)\n        assert h.rt.store.get_task(\"task-blocked-1\").status == TaskStatus.CANCELLED\n        assert \"已取消\" in _chat_text(app), \"取消结果必须有界面反馈\"\n\n\nasync def test_tui_task_panel_requests_cancel_of_running_task(harness_factory):\n    \"\"\"RUNNING 且有活动回合：只发出取消请求（回合收到 CANCEL_REQUESTED），任务不直接终结。\"\"\"\n    h = await _session(harness_factory, b_script=[(\"sleep\", 5), (\"end\",)])\n    _seed(h.rt, _task(\"task-running-1\", TaskStatus.RUNNING),\n          TurnRun(run_id=\"run-running-1\", session_id=\"s1\", task_id=\"task-running-1\",\n                  agent_id=\"b\", config_revision=1, topology_revision=1,\n                  status=\"RUNNING\"))\n    app = TeamAgentsApp(runtime=h.rt)\n    async with app.run_test(size=(120, 40)) as pilot:\n        await _open_tasks(pilot, app)\n        await pilot.press(\"c\")\n        await pilot.pause(0.3)\n        assert h.rt.store.run_cancel_requested(\"run-running-1\"), \\\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
<message type="tool">{"ok": true, "exit_code": 0, "output": "\"\"\"User configuration (TOML, stdlib), TeamSpec import/export (JSON/YAML),\nand XDG paths.\n\nConfig parsing never executes Python, imports arbitrary modules, or resolves\nYAML tags (plan section 14). Project config may add model profiles, tools and\nskills, but cannot open full-auto mode or widen pre-authorized scopes.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport os\nimport tomllib\nimport warnings\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom typing import Any\n\nimport yaml\n\nfrom .models import PermissionMode, TeamSpec, UserConfig\n\nAPP = \"teamagents\"\n\n# User-level opt-in that lets repository-local tool bindings (MCP commands,\n# web tools) take effect. Default off: opening a cloned repository must never\n# be enough to run its configured commands (plan sections 12.2 and 14).\nTRUST_PROJECT_TOOLS = \"trust_project_tools\"\n\n\nclass ProjectConfigWarning(UserWarning):\n    \"\"\"A repository-local .teamagents/config.toml entry was ignored.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef _warn_ignored(message: str) -&gt; None:\n    # stacklevel: _warn_ignored -&gt; _merge_* -&gt; load_user_config -&gt; caller\n    warnings.warn(f\"teamagents: {message}\", ProjectConfigWarning, stacklevel=4)\n\n\ndef _trust_project_tools(user_data: dict[str, Any]) -&gt; bool:\n    \"\"\"Read the user-config opt-in for project-defined tool bindings.\"\"\"\n    permissions = user_data.get(\"permissions\", {})\n    if not isinstance(permissions, dict):\n        raise ValueError(\"[permissions] must be a table in user config\")\n    value = permissions.get(TRUST_PROJECT_TOOLS, False)\n    if not isinstance(value, bool):\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"permissions.{TRUST_PROJECT_TOOLS} must be true/false, got {value!r}\")\n    return value\n\n\ndef _merge_project_models(user_models: dict[str, Any],\n                          project_models: dict[str, Any]) -&gt; dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Project may add profiles; a user-defined name always wins.\"\"\"\n    merged = dict(user_models)\n    for name, profile in project_models.items():\n        if name in user_models:\n            _warn_ignored(f\"项目配置试图覆盖用户模型 profile {name!r}，已忽略项目定义\")\n            continue\n        merged[name] = profile\n    return merged\n\n\ndef _merge_project_tools(user_tools: dict[str, Any], project_tools: dict[str, Any],\n                         *, trusted: bool) -&gt; dict[str, Any]:\n    \"\"\"Project tools load only after the user opts in; user-defined names win.\"\"\"\n    merged = dict(user_tools)\n    for name, binding in project_tools.items():\n        if name in user_tools:\n            _warn_ignored(f\"项目配置试图覆盖用户工具 {name!r}，已忽略项目定义\")\n            continue\n        if not trusted:\n            _warn_ignored(\n                f\"项目配置定义了工具 {name!r}，默认不信任项目工具，已忽略；\"\n                f\"确认安全后可在用户配置设置 [permissions] {TRUST_PROJECT_TOOLS} = true\")\n            continue\n        merged[name] = binding\n    return merged\n\n\ndef xdg_config_home() -&gt; Path:\n    return Path(os.environ.get(\"XDG_CONFIG_HOME\", Path.home() / \".config\"))\n\n\ndef xdg_state_home() -&gt; Path:\n    return Path(os.environ.get(\"XDG_STATE_HOME\", Path.home() / \".local\" / \"state\"))\n\n\ndef user_config_path() -&gt; Path:\n    return xdg_config_home() / APP / \"config.toml\"\n\n\ndef project_config_path(cwd: Path) -&gt; Path:\n    return cwd / \".teamagents\" / \"config.toml\"\n\n\ndef state_dir() -&gt; Path:\n    return xdg_state_home() / APP\n\n\ndef sessions_dir() -&gt; Path:\n    return state_dir() / \"sessions\"\n\n\ndef default_session_id(cwd: Path | None = None) -&gt; str:\n    \"\"\"One session per project directory unless the user picks one.\"\"\"\n    base = str((cwd or Path.cwd()).resolve())\n    import hashlib\n    return \"proj_\" + hashlib.sha256(base.encode()).hexdigest()[:12]\n\n\ndef _read_toml(path: Path) -&gt; dict[str, Any]:\n    if not path.exists():\n        return {}\n    with path.open(\"rb\") as fh:\n        return tomllib.load(fh)\n\n\ndef load_user_config(cwd: Path | None = None) -&gt; UserConfig:\n    \"\"\"User config plus repository-local project config (user-defined names win).\n\n    A project file may add model profiles, but it can never replace a\n    user-defined name and its tool bindings only load after the user opts in\n    with ``[permissions] trust_project_tools = true`` (plan sections 12.2/14).\n    \"\"\"\n    data = _read_toml(user_config_path())\n    project = _read_toml(project_config_path(cwd or Path.cwd()))\n    merged: dict[str, Any] = {\n        \"models\": _merge_project_models(data.get(\"models\", {}), project.get(\"models\", {})),\n        \"tools\": _merge_project_tools(data.get(\"tools\", {}), project.get(\"tools\", {}),\n                                      trusted=_trust_project_tools(data)),\n        \"skills_paths\": list(data.get(\"skills_paths\", [])) + list(project.get(\"skills_paths\", [])),\n        \"instruction_files\": (list(data.get(\"instruction_files\", []))\n                              + list(project.get(\"instruction_files\", []))),\n    }\n    catalog = UserConfig.model_validate(merged)\n    _validate_skill_paths(catalog)\n    return catalog\n\n\ndef _validate_skill_paths(catalog: UserConfig) -&gt; None:\n==== GP condition ====\n56:    GENERAL_PURPOSE_SUBAGENT,\n409:            `general_purpose_subagent=GeneralPurposeSubagentProfile(enabled=False)`\n447:            `general_purpose_subagent=GeneralPurposeSubagentProfile(enabled=False)`\n663:    inline_subagents: list[SubAgent | CompiledSubAgent] = []\n672:            inline_subagents.append(spec)\n786:                inline_subagents.append(cast(\"SubAgent\", processed))\n788:                inline_subagents.append(cast(\"SubAgent\", processed))\n795:    gp_profile = _profile.general_purpose_subagent or GeneralPurposeSubagentProfile()\n796:    if gp_profile.enabled is not False and not any(spec[\"name\"] == GENERAL_PURPOSE_SUBAGENT[\"name\"] for spec in inline_subagents):\n835:        general_purpose_spec: SubAgent = {\n836:            **GENERAL_PURPOSE_SUBAGENT,\n842:            general_purpose_spec[\"description\"] = gp_profile.description\n849:            general_purpose_spec[\"system_prompt\"] = gp_prompt\n851:            general_purpose_spec[\"system_prompt\"] = _apply_profile_prompt(_profile, GENERAL_PURPOSE_SUBAGENT[\"system_prompt\"])\n857:            general_purpose_spec[\"interrupt_on\"] = gp_interrupt_on\n859:        inline_subagents.insert(0, general_purpose_spec)\n873:    if inline_subagents:\n876:            subagents=inline_subagents,\n", "truncated": false, "artifact": null}</message>
